@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def _available_test_model_secret(monkeypatch) -> None:
    """Keep opaque fixture references usable without touching host credentials."""

    import app.native_baseline_regression as native_regression

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        native_regression,
        "secret_reference_available",
        lambda reference: bool(reference),
    )


def _prompt(node_id: str, *, source_model_config_id: int = 1) -> dict:
    return {
        "node_instance_id": node_id,
        "kind": "prompt",
        "config": {
            "max_tokens": 200,
            "model_snapshot": {
                "source_model_config_id": source_model_config_id,
                "model_id": "budget-model",
            },
        },
    }


def _real_jpeg_bytes(width: int = 64, height: int = 48) -> bytes:
    """执行器与老评测路径共用输入预处理后，素材必须是真实可解码的图片。"""

    import io

    from PIL import Image

    buffer = io.BytesIO()
    Image.new("RGB", (width, height), (20, 40, 60)).save(buffer, format="JPEG")
    return buffer.getvalue()


def _priced_model(**overrides) -> SimpleNamespace:
    values = {
        "id": 1,
        "max_input_tokens": 1_000,
        "max_tokens": 200,
        "input_micros_per_million_tokens": 100_000,
        "output_micros_per_million_tokens": 200_000,
        "benchmark_enabled": True,
    }
    values.update(overrides)
    return SimpleNamespace(**values)


def test_prompt_nodes_from_snapshot_discovers_a_single_prompt_without_prompt_b() -> None:
    """Catches stock regression assuming every frozen workflow has A and B calls."""

    from app.native_baseline_regression import prompt_nodes_from_snapshot

    snapshot = {
        "workflow": {
            "manifest": {
                "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
                "nodes": [
                    {
                        "node_instance_id": "upstream",
                        "kind": "upstream_connector",
                    },
                    _prompt("prompt_unified"),
                    {
                        "node_instance_id": "score",
                        "kind": "rule_module",
                    },
                ],
            }
        }
    }

    prompts = prompt_nodes_from_snapshot(snapshot)

    assert [node["node_instance_id"] for node in prompts] == ["prompt_unified"]


def test_prompt_nodes_from_snapshot_discovers_nested_foreach_prompt() -> None:
    from app.native_baseline_regression import prompt_nodes_from_snapshot

    nested = _prompt("foreach_images")
    snapshot = {
        "workflow": {
            "manifest": {
                "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
                "nodes": [
                    {
                        **nested,
                        "kind": "foreach_image",
                        "config": {
                            "items_path": "$.case.images",
                            "item_identity_path": "$.cloud_id",
                            "max_items": 10,
                            "max_parallel_items": 2,
                            "failure_policy": "fail_open",
                            "item_executor": {
                                "kind": "prompt",
                                **nested["config"],
                            },
                        },
                    }
                ],
            }
        }
    }

    prompts = prompt_nodes_from_snapshot(snapshot)

    assert len(prompts) == 1
    assert prompts[0]["node_instance_id"] == "foreach_images"
    assert prompts[0]["kind"] == "prompt"


def test_single_prompt_snapshot_exposes_frozen_contract_and_vision_hashes() -> None:
    from app.native_baseline_regression import prompt_contract_identities_from_snapshot

    output_contract = {
        "mode": "json_schema",
        "name": "combined_output",
        "strict": True,
        "schema": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {"score": {"type": "integer"}},
            "required": ["score"],
            "additionalProperties": False,
        },
    }
    snapshot = {
        "workflow": {
            "manifest": {
                "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
                "nodes": [
                    {
                        "node_instance_id": "prompt_combined",
                        "kind": "prompt",
                        "config": {
                            "output_contract": output_contract,
                            "vision_policy_hash": "a" * 64,
                        },
                    }
                ],
            }
        }
    }

    identities = prompt_contract_identities_from_snapshot(snapshot)

    assert [item["node_instance_id"] for item in identities] == [
        "prompt_combined"
    ]
    assert len(identities[0]["output_contract_hash"]) == 64
    assert identities[0]["vision_policy_hash"] == "a" * 64


def test_real_budget_counts_one_prompt_workflow_without_reserving_a_second_call() -> None:
    """Catches a one-call workflow being charged and gated as a legacy A/B pipeline."""

    from app.workflow_model_budget import real_execution_budget_for_selection

    prompt = _prompt("prompt_unified")
    prompt["retry_policy"] = {"kind": "none"}
    budget = real_execution_budget_for_selection(
        {
            "nodes": [prompt],
            "edges": [],
        },
        item_count=2,
        model_configs={1: _priced_model()},
    )

    assert budget.expected_requests == 2
    assert budget.expected_output_tokens == 400
    assert budget.max_requests == 2
    assert budget.max_input_tokens == 2_000
    assert budget.max_output_tokens == 400
    assert budget.max_cost_micros == 280


def test_legacy_retry_headroom_uses_full_reservation_in_every_dimension() -> None:
    from app.workflow_model_budget import real_execution_budget_for_selection

    prompt = _prompt("prompt_legacy")
    prompt["config"]["component_snapshot"] = {
        "kind": "prompt",
        "content": {
            "stage": "A",
            "system_prompt": "分类。",
            "user_prompt": "返回结构化结果。",
        },
    }
    budget = real_execution_budget_for_selection(
        {"nodes": [prompt], "edges": []},
        item_count=1,
        model_configs={1: _priced_model()},
        input_manifests=[{"asset_id": 7}],
    )

    assert budget.expected_input_tokens < 1_000
    assert budget.max_requests == 2
    assert budget.max_input_tokens == 2_000
    assert budget.max_output_tokens == 400
    assert budget.max_cost_micros == 280


def test_real_budget_uses_v3_tier_in_preference_to_legacy_flat_prices() -> None:
    from app.workflow_model_budget import (
        real_execution_budget_for_selection,
        real_execution_node_limits,
    )

    prompt = _prompt("prompt_tiered")
    prompt["config"]["max_tokens"] = 1_000
    prompt["config"]["model_snapshot"]["provider"] = "doubao"
    model = _priced_model(
        max_input_tokens=32_001,
        max_tokens=1_000,
        input_micros_per_million_tokens=999_999_999,
        output_micros_per_million_tokens=999_999_999,
        pricing_contract_json=canonical_json(
            doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing()
        ),
    )

    budget = real_execution_budget_for_selection(
        {"nodes": [prompt], "edges": []},
        item_count=1,
        model_configs={1: model},
    )
    limits = real_execution_node_limits(
        {"nodes": [prompt], "edges": []},
        model_configs={1: model},
    )

    assert budget.expected_cost_micros == 34_201
    assert limits["prompt_tiered"]["pricing_version"] == "model-pricing-v3"
    assert limits["prompt_tiered"]["pricing_tier_key"] == "32000-128000"
    assert limits["prompt_tiered"]["pricing_snapshot"] == (
        doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing()
    )


def test_real_budget_rejects_decreasing_tiers_before_hard_limit_reservation() -> None:
    from app.workflow_model_budget import (
        WorkflowModelBudgetError,
        real_execution_budget_for_selection,
    )

    prompt = _prompt("prompt_unsafe_tariff")
    prompt["config"]["max_tokens"] = 4_096
    pricing = doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing()
    pricing["tiers"][0]["input_micros_per_1k"] = 1_000_000
    pricing["tiers"][0]["output_micros_per_1k"] = 1_000_000
    pricing["tiers"][1]["input_micros_per_1k"] = 1
    pricing["tiers"][1]["output_micros_per_1k"] = 1
    pricing["tiers"][2]["input_micros_per_1k"] = 1
    pricing["tiers"][2]["output_micros_per_1k"] = 1
    model = _priced_model(
        max_input_tokens=256_000,
        max_tokens=4_096,
        pricing_contract_json=canonical_json(pricing),
    )

    with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError) as exc_info:
        real_execution_budget_for_selection(
            {"nodes": [prompt], "edges": []},
            item_count=1,
            model_configs={1: model},
        )

    assert exc_info.value.code == "model_pricing_tiers_invalid"


def test_real_budget_prices_expected_prompt_and_image_tokens_not_model_context() -> None:
    """Catches the UI estimate selecting a tier from the model context ceiling."""

    from app.model_pricing import price_usage
    from app.workflow_model_budget import (
        real_execution_budget_for_selection,
        real_execution_node_limits,
    )

    prompt = _prompt("prompt_expected")
    prompt["config"].update(
        {
            "max_tokens": 1_000,
            "input_modality": "image",
            "component_snapshot": {
                "kind": "prompt",
                "content": {
                    "stage": "A",
                    "system_prompt": "判断图片内容。",
                    "user_prompt": "只返回结构化结果。",
                },
            },
            "vision_input_policy": {
                "version": "vision-input-policy-v1",
                "provider_profile": "volcengine-seed-2.0-v20260901",
                "roles": {
                    "target": {
                        "fidelity": {"mode": "detail", "value": "high"},
                        "preprocess": {"mode": "original"},
                    },
                    "anchor": {
                        "fidelity": {"mode": "detail", "value": "high"},
                        "preprocess": {"mode": "original"},
                    },
                },
                "transport": {
                    "mode": "lossless",
                    "size_limit": "provider_profile",
                },
            },
        }
    )
    prompt["config"]["model_snapshot"]["provider"] = "doubao"
    pricing = doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing()
    model = _priced_model(
        max_input_tokens=32_001,
        max_tokens=1_000,
        pricing_contract_json=canonical_json(pricing),
    )

    budget = real_execution_budget_for_selection(
        {"nodes": [prompt], "edges": []},
        item_count=1,
        model_configs={1: model},
        input_manifests=[{"asset_id": 7, "asset_sha256": "a" * 64}],
    )
    limits = real_execution_node_limits(
        {"nodes": [prompt], "edges": []},
        model_configs={1: model},
        input_manifests=[{"asset_id": 7, "asset_sha256": "a" * 64}],
    )

    assert 1_280 < budget.expected_input_tokens < 32_000
    assert budget.expected_cost_micros == price_usage(
        pricing,
        input_tokens=budget.expected_input_tokens,
        output_tokens=1_000,
    ).total_cost_micros
    assert limits["prompt_expected"]["max_input_tokens"] == 32_001
    assert limits["prompt_expected"]["pricing_tier_key"] == "32000-128000"
    assert limits["prompt_expected"]["expected_request_count"] == 1
    assert limits["prompt_expected"]["expected_pricing_tier_key"] == "0-32000"
    assert limits["prompt_expected"]["expected_tier_buckets"] == [
        {
            "pricing_tier_key": "0-32000",
            "request_count": 1,
            "input_tokens": budget.expected_input_tokens,
            "output_tokens": 1_000,
            "cost_micros": budget.expected_cost_micros,
        }
    ]


def test_node_limits_report_each_expected_pricing_tier_bucket() -> None:
    from app.workflow_model_budget import real_execution_node_limits

    prompt = _prompt("prompt_cross_tier")
    prompt["config"].update(
        {
            "max_tokens": 1_000,
            "component_snapshot": {
                "kind": "prompt",
                "content": {
                    "stage": "A",
                    "system_prompt": "分类。",
                    "user_prompt": "返回结构化结果。",
                },
            },
        }
    )
    prompt["config"]["model_snapshot"]["provider"] = "doubao"
    model = _priced_model(
        max_input_tokens=128_001,
        max_tokens=1_000,
        pricing_contract_json=canonical_json(
            doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing()
        ),
    )

    limits = real_execution_node_limits(
        {"nodes": [prompt], "edges": []},
        model_configs={1: model},
        input_manifests=[
            {"description": "small"},
            {"description": "x" * 110_000},
        ],
    )["prompt_cross_tier"]

    assert limits["expected_request_count"] == 2
    assert [
        bucket["pricing_tier_key"]
        for bucket in limits["expected_tier_buckets"]
    ] == ["0-32000", "32000-128000"]
    assert sum(
        bucket["request_count"]
        for bucket in limits["expected_tier_buckets"]
    ) == 2
    assert sum(
        bucket["cost_micros"]
        for bucket in limits["expected_tier_buckets"]
    ) == limits["expected_cost_micros"]


def test_expected_prompt_budget_includes_dynamic_direct_upstream_envelope() -> None:
    """Case aggregate and chained prompt output are part of the paid request body."""

    from app.workflow_model_budget import real_execution_budget_for_selection

    nested = _prompt("foreach_images")
    nested["config"].update(
        {
            "max_tokens": 1_200,
            "component_snapshot": {
                "kind": "prompt",
                "content": {
                    "system_prompt": "逐图分析。",
                    "user_prompt": "返回严格结构。",
                },
            },
        }
    )
    foreach = {
        **nested,
        "kind": "foreach_image",
        "config": {
            "items_path": "$.case.images",
            "item_identity_path": "$.cloud_id",
            "max_items": 10,
            "max_parallel_items": 2,
            "failure_policy": "fail_open",
            "item_executor": {"kind": "prompt", **nested["config"]},
        },
    }
    aggregate = {
        "node_instance_id": "aggregate_images",
        "kind": "aggregate_case",
        "config": {"foreach_node_id": "foreach_images"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object", "properties": {}},
    }
    text_prompt = _prompt("case_text_prompt")
    text_prompt["config"].update(
        {
            "max_tokens": 1_000,
            "component_snapshot": {
                "kind": "prompt",
                "content": {
                    "system_prompt": "综合案例。",
                    "user_prompt": "结合逐图结果输出。",
                },
            },
        }
    )
    inputs = [
        {
            "case": {
                "images": [
                    {"cloud_id": "image-1"},
                    {"cloud_id": "image-2"},
                ]
            }
        }
    ]
    model = _priced_model(max_input_tokens=32_001, max_tokens=1_200)
    disconnected = real_execution_budget_for_selection(
        {"nodes": [foreach, aggregate, text_prompt], "edges": []},
        item_count=1,
        model_configs={1: model},
        input_manifests=inputs,
    )
    connected = real_execution_budget_for_selection(
        {
            "nodes": [foreach, aggregate, text_prompt],
            "edges": [
                {"from": "foreach_images", "to": "aggregate_images"},
                {"from": "aggregate_images", "to": "case_text_prompt"},
            ],
        },
        item_count=1,
        model_configs={1: model},
        input_manifests=inputs,
    )

    assert connected.expected_input_tokens >= (
        disconnected.expected_input_tokens + 2_400
    )


def test_real_budget_counts_each_frozen_foreach_image_child() -> None:
    from app.workflow_model_budget import real_execution_budget_for_selection

    prompt = _prompt("foreach_images")
    foreach = {
        **prompt,

            "items_path": "$.case.images",
            "item_identity_path": "$.cloud_id",
            "max_items": 4,
            "max_parallel_items": 2,
            "failure_policy": "fail_open",
            "item_executor": {
                "kind": "prompt",
                **prompt["config"],
            },
        },
    }

    budget = real_execution_budget_for_selection(
        {"nodes": [foreach], "edges": []},
        item_count=2,
        model_configs={1: _priced_model()},
    )

    assert budget.expected_requests == 8
    assert budget.expected_output_tokens == 1_600


def test_real_budget_reserves_every_configured_prompt_retry() -> None:
    """A five-attempt prompt must not hit the run budget on attempt three."""

    from app.workflow_model_budget import real_execution_budget_for_selection

    prompt = _prompt("prompt_retry")
    prompt["retry_policy"] = {
        "kind": "exponential",
        "max_attempts": 5,
        "delay_seconds": 2,
    }

    budget = real_execution_budget_for_selection(
        {"nodes": [prompt], "edges": []},
        item_count=2,
        model_configs={1: _priced_model()},
    )

    assert budget.expected_requests == 2
    assert budget.max_requests == 10
    assert budget.max_input_tokens == 10_000
    assert budget.max_output_tokens == 2_000
    assert budget.max_cost_micros == 1_400


def test_real_budget_preserves_legacy_headroom_in_mixed_retry_snapshot() -> None:
    """One explicit node must not remove retry headroom from legacy nodes."""

    from app.workflow_model_budget import real_execution_budget_for_selection

    explicit = _prompt("prompt_explicit")
    explicit["retry_policy"] = {"kind": "none"}
    legacy = _prompt("prompt_legacy")

    budget = real_execution_budget_for_selection(
        {
            "nodes": [explicit, legacy],
            "edges": [{"from": "prompt_explicit", "to": "prompt_legacy"}],
        },
        item_count=2,
        model_configs={1: _priced_model()},
    )

    assert budget.expected_requests == 4
    assert budget.max_requests == 5
    assert budget.max_input_tokens == 5_000
    assert budget.max_output_tokens == 1_000
    assert budget.max_cost_micros == 700


def test_real_budget_counts_every_sequential_prompt_for_each_item() -> None:
    """Catches under-reserving a sequential A/B workflow as one model call."""

    from app.workflow_model_budget import real_execution_budget_for_selection

    manifest = {
        "nodes": [_prompt("prompt_a"), _prompt("prompt_b")],
        "edges": [{"from": "prompt_a", "to": "prompt_b"}],
    }

    budget = real_execution_budget_for_selection(
        manifest,
        item_count=2,
        model_configs={1: _priced_model()},
    )

    assert budget.as_dict() == {
        "expected_requests": 4,
        "expected_input_tokens": 4_000,
        "expected_output_tokens": 800,
        "expected_cost_micros": 560,
        "max_requests": 6,
        "max_input_tokens": 6_000,
        "max_output_tokens": 1_200,
        "max_cost_micros": 840,
    }


def test_real_budget_keeps_one_full_item_retry_for_a_two_prompt_canary() -> None:
    """A one-item A/B canary must be resumable after semantic finalization."""

    from app.workflow_model_budget import real_execution_budget_for_selection

    manifest = {
        "nodes": [_prompt("prompt_a"), _prompt("prompt_b")],
        "edges": [{"from": "prompt_a", "to": "prompt_b"}],
    }

    budget = real_execution_budget_for_selection(
        manifest,
        item_count=1,
        model_configs={1: _priced_model()},
    )

    assert budget.as_dict() == {
        "expected_requests": 2,
        "expected_input_tokens": 2_000,
        "expected_output_tokens": 400,
        "expected_cost_micros": 280,
        "max_requests": 4,
        "max_input_tokens": 4_000,
        "max_output_tokens": 800,
        "max_cost_micros": 560,
    }


def test_real_budget_counts_only_largest_mutually_exclusive_prompt_branch() -> None:
    """Catches charging all four KG routes when each item can execute only one."""

    from app.workflow_model_budget import real_execution_budget_for_selection

    branches = ["domestic_whole", "domestic_single", "overseas_whole", "overseas_single"]
    nodes = [
        {
            "node_instance_id": "route_selector",
            "kind": "branch",
            "config": {
                "branch_key": "kg_route",
                "branches": branches,
                "mode": "exclusive",
            },
        },
        *[_prompt(f"prompt_{branch}") for branch in branches],
        {
            "node_instance_id": "route_join",
            "kind": "join",
            "config": {"join_for": "kg_route", "mode": "all"},
        },
    ]
    edges = [
        {
            "from": "route_selector",
            "to": f"prompt_{branch}",
            "branch": branch,
            "condition": {"op": "eq", "path": "input.route", "value": branch},
        }
        for branch in branches
    ] + [
        {"from": f"prompt_{branch}", "to": "route_join"}
        for branch in branches
    ]

    budget = real_execution_budget_for_selection(
        {"nodes": nodes, "edges": edges},
        item_count=4,
        model_configs={1: _priced_model()},
    )

    assert budget.as_dict() == {
        "expected_requests": 4,
        "expected_input_tokens": 4_000,
        "expected_output_tokens": 800,
        "expected_cost_micros": 560,
        "max_requests": 6,
        "max_input_tokens": 6_000,
        "max_output_tokens": 1_200,
        "max_cost_micros": 840,
    }


def test_real_budget_takes_componentwise_max_across_heterogeneous_routes() -> None:
    """No exclusive route may be under-reserved by another route's call count."""

    from app.workflow_model_budget import real_execution_budget_for_selection

    prompt_a1 = _prompt("prompt_a1")
    prompt_a2 = _prompt("prompt_a2")
    for prompt in (prompt_a1, prompt_a2):
        prompt["retry_policy"] = {
            "kind": "fixed",
            "max_attempts": 5,
            "delay_seconds": 1,
        }
    prompt_b = _prompt("prompt_b", source_model_config_id=2)
    prompt_b["config"]["max_tokens"] = 800
    prompt_b["retry_policy"] = {"kind": "none"}
    nodes = [
        {
            "node_instance_id": "route_selector",
            "kind": "branch",
            "config": {
                "branch_key": "route",
                "branches": ["a", "b"],
                "mode": "exclusive",
            },
        },
        prompt_a1,
        prompt_a2,
        prompt_b,
        {
            "node_instance_id": "route_join",
            "kind": "join",
            "config": {"join_for": "route", "mode": "all"},
        },
    ]
    edges = [
        {"from": "route_selector", "to": "prompt_a1", "branch": "a"},
        {"from": "prompt_a1", "to": "prompt_a2"},
        {"from": "prompt_a2", "to": "route_join"},
        {"from": "route_selector", "to": "prompt_b", "branch": "b"},
        {"from": "prompt_b", "to": "route_join"},
    ]

    budget = real_execution_budget_for_selection(
        {"nodes": nodes, "edges": edges},
        item_count=1,
        model_configs={
            1: _priced_model(),
            2: _priced_model(
                id=2,
                max_input_tokens=5_000,
                max_tokens=800,
                input_micros_per_million_tokens=500_000,
                output_micros_per_million_tokens=900_000,
            ),
        },
    )

    assert budget.as_dict() == {
        "expected_requests": 2,
        "expected_input_tokens": 5_000,
        "expected_output_tokens": 800,
        "expected_cost_micros": 3_220,
            "max_requests": 10,
            "max_input_tokens": 10_000,
            "max_output_tokens": 2_000,
            "max_cost_micros": 3_220,
        }


def test_real_budget_rejects_unpriced_models() -> None:

    from app.workflow_model_budget import (

        real_execution_budget_for_selection,
    )

            {"nodes": [_prompt("prompt_a")], "edges": []},

            },

    assert exc_info.value.code == "model_pricing_unverified"

def _attach_priced_model(db: Session, workflow) -> ModelConfig:
    model = ModelConfig(
        name="真实回归模型",
        provider="doubao",
        protocol="openai_chat",
        base_url="https://fixture.invalid",
        api_path="/chat/completions",
        model_id="budget-model",
        max_tokens=8_192,
        max_input_tokens=12_000,
        input_micros_per_million_tokens=1_000_000,
        output_micros_per_million_tokens=2_000_000,
        benchmark_enabled=True,
        active=True,
        encrypted_api_key="test-only:model-secret-reference",
    )
    db.add(model)
    db.flush()
    nodes = json.loads(workflow.step_manifest_json)
    prompt = next(node for node in nodes if node["kind"] == "prompt")
    model_snapshot = {
        "model_registry_id": 7,
        "name": model.name,
        "provider": model.provider,
        "protocol": model.protocol,
        "model_id": model.model_id,
        "source_model_config_id": model.id,
        "capabilities": ["text", "vision", "structured_output"],
        "structured_output": True,
        "runtime_config": build_model_config_snapshot(model),
    }
    model_snapshot["snapshot_hash"] = hashlib.sha256(
        canonical_json(model_snapshot).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()
    prompt["config"]["model_snapshot"] = model_snapshot
    workflow.step_manifest_json = canonical_json(nodes)
    workflow.canonical_hash = hashlib.sha256(
        canonical_json(persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow)).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()
    db.flush()
    return model


def test_real_native_regression_requires_explicit_cost_confirmation(tmp_path) -> None:
    """Catches any service caller bypassing the operator's paid-run acknowledgement."""

    from app.native_baseline_regression import (
        NativeBaselineRegressionError,
        create_native_baseline_run,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-confirmation.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            workflow = _mechanism(db)

            with pytest.raises(NativeBaselineRegressionError) as exc_info:
                create_native_baseline_run(
                    db,
                    baseline_set=baseline,
                    workflow_version=workflow,
                    actor="ops",
                    idempotency_key="real-confirmation-required",
                    model_execution_mode="real",
                    real_execution_confirmed=False,
                )

            assert exc_info.value.code == "real_execution_confirmation_required"
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id))) == 0
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_real_run_rejects_missing_secret_before_persisting_any_run(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """Catches stale database references leaving half-created paid runs."""

    import app.native_baseline_regression as native_regression

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-missing-secret.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            model = _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)
            model.encrypted_api_key = "keychain:v1:model-config"
            monkeypatch.setattr(
                native_regression,
                "secret_reference_available",
                lambda _reference: False,
                raising=False,
            )

            with pytest.raises(
                native_regression.NativeBaselineRegressionError
            ) as exc_info:
                native_regression.create_native_baseline_run(
                    db,
                    baseline_set=baseline,
                    workflow_version=workflow,
                    actor="ops",
                    idempotency_key="real-missing-secret",
                    model_execution_mode="real",
                    real_execution_confirmed=True,
                )

            assert exc_info.value.code == "model_secret_unavailable"
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(BaselineRegressionRun.id))) == 0
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id))) == 0
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id))) == 0
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_real_native_regression_freezes_budget_and_keeps_connectors_dry_run(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    """Catches losing paid-mode identity or accidentally enabling real connectors."""

    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-frozen-budget.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)

            run = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="real-frozen-budget",
                model_execution_mode="real",
                real_execution_confirmed=True,
            )
            db.flush()

            execution = json.loads(run.execution_snapshot_json)
            flat_pricing_snapshot = {
                "schema_version": "model-pricing-flat-v1",
                "currency": "CNY",
                "unit_tokens": 1_000_000,
                "input_micros_per_million_tokens": 1_000_000,
                "output_micros_per_million_tokens": 2_000_000,
            }
            assert execution["model_execution_mode"] == "real"
            assert execution["real_execution_budget"] == {
                "expected_requests": 1,
                "expected_input_tokens": 1_108,
                "expected_output_tokens": 8_192,
                "expected_cost_micros": 17_492,
                "max_requests": 1,
                "max_input_tokens": 12_000,
                "max_output_tokens": 8_192,
                "max_cost_micros": 28_384,
            }
            assert execution["real_execution_node_limits"] == {
                "prompt_c": {
                    "provider": "doubao",
                    "model_id": "budget-model",
                    "source_model_config_id": 1,
                        "max_input_tokens": 12_000,
                        "max_output_tokens": 8_192,
                        "estimated_cost_micros": 28_384,
                        "call_count": 1,
                        "expected_input_tokens_per_request": 1_108,
                        "expected_output_tokens_per_request": 8_192,
                        "expected_cost_micros_per_request": 17_492,
                        "expected_pricing_tier_key": "flat",
                        "expected_request_count": 1,
                        "expected_input_tokens": 1_108,
                        "expected_output_tokens": 8_192,
                        "expected_cost_micros": 17_492,
                        "expected_tier_buckets": [
                            {
                                "pricing_tier_key": "flat",
                                "request_count": 1,
                                "input_tokens": 1_108,
                                "output_tokens": 8_192,
                                "cost_micros": 17_492,
                            }
                        ],
                        "configured_max_attempts": 1,
                        "retry_reserve_request_count": 0,
                        "expected_output_basis": {
                            "source": "output_cap",
                            "sample_size": 0,
                            "p95_output_tokens": None,
                            "output_tokens_per_request": 8_192,
                            "output_cap_per_request": 8_192,
                        },
                        "input_micros_per_million_tokens": 1_000_000,
                        "output_micros_per_million_tokens": 2_000_000,
                        "pricing_version": "model-pricing-flat-v1",
                        "pricing_tier_key": "flat",
                        "pricing_contract_hash": hashlib.sha256(
                            canonical_json(flat_pricing_snapshot).encode("utf-8")
                        ).hexdigest(),
                        "pricing_snapshot": flat_pricing_snapshot,
                    }
                }
            production = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun))
            assert production is not None
            assert production.model_execution_mode == "real"
            assert production.environment == "dry_run"
            runtime = json.loads(production.snapshot_json)["runtime_context"]
            assert runtime["model_execution_mode"] == "real"
            assert runtime["run_mode"] == "stock_regression"
            assert runtime["real_execution_node_limits"] == execution[
                "real_execution_node_limits"
            ]
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_model_invocation_reservation_is_idempotent_and_uses_frozen_node_limits(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    """Catches duplicate charging or reading mutable model limits during a retry."""

    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.workflow_model_budget import (
        WorkflowModelBudgetError,
        reserve_model_invocation,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-reservation.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)
            aggregate = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="real-reservation",
                model_execution_mode="real",
                real_execution_confirmed=True,
            )
            production_id = aggregate.items[0].production_run_id
            db.commit()

        with Session(engine) as db:
            production = db.get(ProductionRun, production_id)
            assert production is not None
            attempt = db.scalar(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                    ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == production.id,
                    ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == "prompt_c",
                )
            )
            assert attempt is not None
            snapshot = json.loads(production.snapshot_json)
            node = next(
                item
                for item in snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]
                if item["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_c"
            )
            invocation = reserve_model_invocation(
                db,
                attempt_id=attempt.id,
                node=node,
                run_snapshot=snapshot,
                dispatch_identity="a" * 64,
            )
            duplicate = reserve_model_invocation(
                db,
                attempt_id=attempt.id,
                node=node,
                run_snapshot=snapshot,
                dispatch_identity="a" * 64,
            )
            with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError) as excinfo:
                reserve_model_invocation(
                    db,
                    attempt_id=attempt.id,
                    node=node,
                    run_snapshot=snapshot,
                    dispatch_identity="b" * 64,
                )
            db.flush()

            assert duplicate.id == invocation.id
            assert excinfo.value.code == "model_invocation_idempotency_conflict"
            assert invocation.baseline_run_id is not None
            assert invocation.production_run_id == production.id
            assert invocation.node_instance_id == "prompt_c"
            assert invocation.reserved_input_tokens == 12_000
            assert invocation.reserved_output_tokens == 8_192
            assert invocation.estimated_cost_micros == 28_384
            assert invocation.quota_pool_id is None
            assert invocation.quota_lease_token is None
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id))) == 1
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_non_foreach_step_cannot_reserve_a_second_paid_invocation(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    """A plain prompt budget must reject a second paid-call identity."""

    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.workflow_model_budget import (
        WorkflowModelBudgetError,
        reserve_model_invocation,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'foreach-reservations.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)
            aggregate = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="foreach-reservations",
                model_execution_mode="real",
                real_execution_confirmed=True,
            )
            production_id = aggregate.items[0].production_run_id
            db.commit()

        with Session(engine) as db:
            production = db.get(ProductionRun, production_id)
            assert production is not None
            attempt = db.scalar(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                    ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == production.id,
                    ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == "prompt_c",
                )
            )
            assert attempt is not None
            snapshot = json.loads(production.snapshot_json)
            node = next(
                item
                for item in snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]
                if item["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_c"
            )

            first = reserve_model_invocation(
                db,
                attempt_id=attempt.id,
                node=node,
                run_snapshot=snapshot,
                invocation_key="item:image-a:attempt:1",
                dispatch_identity="a" * 64,
            )
            with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError) as exc_info:
                reserve_model_invocation(
                    db,
                    attempt_id=attempt.id,
                    node=node,
                    run_snapshot=snapshot,
                    invocation_key="item:image-b:attempt:1",
                    dispatch_identity="b" * 64,
                )
            replay = reserve_model_invocation(
                db,
                attempt_id=attempt.id,
                node=node,
                run_snapshot=snapshot,
                invocation_key="item:image-a:attempt:1",
                dispatch_identity="a" * 64,
            )
            db.flush()

            assert replay.id == first.id
            assert exc_info.value.code == "model_budget_requests_exhausted"
            assert [
                item.invocation_key
                for item in db.scalars(
                    select(WorkflowModelInvocation).order_by(
                        WorkflowModelInvocation.invocation_key
                    )
                )
            ] == ["item:image-a:attempt:1"]
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_model_invocation_shared_pool_blocks_a_second_provider_reservation(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    """The same provider endpoint must not gain capacity from a second key."""

    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.workflow_model_budget import (
        WorkflowModelBudgetError,
        reserve_model_invocation,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-quota-pool.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    targets: list[tuple[int, dict, dict]] = []
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            _add_level_truth_item(db, baseline, suffix="quota-2")
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            pool = ModelQuotaPool(
                pool_key="doubao-shared-endpoint",
                name="豆包共享端点",
                provider="doubao",
                scope_kind="endpoint",
                scope_identifier="ep-shared",
                max_concurrency=1,
                lease_seconds=30,
                enabled=True,
                created_by="test",
            )
            db.add(pool)
            db.flush()
            model = _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)
            model.quota_pool_id = pool.id
            aggregate = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="real-quota-shared",
                model_execution_mode="real",
                real_execution_confirmed=True,
            )
            for aggregate_item in aggregate.items:
                production = db.get(
                    ProductionRun, aggregate_item.production_run_id
                )
                assert production is not None
                attempt_id = db.scalar(
                    select(ProductionStepAttempt.id).where(
                        ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == production.id,
                        ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == "prompt_c",
                    )
                )
                assert attempt_id is not None
                snapshot = json.loads(production.snapshot_json)
                node = next(
                    item
                    for item in snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]
                    if item["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_c"
                )
                targets.append((attempt_id, node, snapshot))
            db.commit()

        with Session(engine) as db:
            first = reserve_model_invocation(
                db,
                attempt_id=targets[0][0],
                node=targets[0][1],
                run_snapshot=targets[0][2],
                reserved_images=1,
            )
            db.commit()
            assert first.quota_pool_id is not None
            assert first.quota_lease_token is not None

        with Session(engine) as db:
            with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError) as exc_info:
                reserve_model_invocation(
                    db,
                    attempt_id=targets[1][0],
                    node=targets[1][1],
                    run_snapshot=targets[1][2],
                    reserved_images=1,
                )
            assert exc_info.value.code == "quota_pool_concurrency_exhausted"
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id))) == 1
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count(ModelQuotaLease.id)).where(
                    ModelQuotaLease.status == "active"
                )
            ) == 1
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("provider_delay,expected_delay", [(1, 60), (120, 120)])
def test_model_invocation_success_releases_quota_and_429_cools_the_pool(
    tmp_path, provider_delay, expected_delay,
) -> None:
    from app.model_quota import model_quota_snapshot
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.workflow_model_budget import (
        fail_model_invocation,
        reserve_model_invocation,
        settle_model_invocation,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-quota-settle.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    targets: list[tuple[int, dict, dict]] = []
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            _add_level_truth_item(db, baseline, suffix="settlement-2")
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            pool = ModelQuotaPool(
                pool_key="doubao-settlement",
                name="豆包结算池",
                provider="doubao",
                scope_kind="endpoint",
                scope_identifier="ep-settlement",
                max_concurrency=2,
                lease_seconds=30,
                enabled=True,
                created_by="test",
            )
            db.add(pool)
            db.flush()
            model = _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)
            model.quota_pool_id = pool.id
            pool_id = pool.id
            aggregate = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="real-quota-settlement",
                model_execution_mode="real",
                real_execution_confirmed=True,
            )
            for aggregate_item in aggregate.items:
                production = db.get(
                    ProductionRun, aggregate_item.production_run_id
                )
                assert production is not None
                attempt_id = db.scalar(
                    select(ProductionStepAttempt.id).where(
                        ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == production.id,
                        ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == "prompt_c",
                    )
                )
                assert attempt_id is not None
                snapshot = json.loads(production.snapshot_json)
                node = next(
                    item
                    for item in snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]
                    if item["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_c"
                )
                targets.append((attempt_id, node, snapshot))
            db.commit()

        with Session(engine) as db:
            succeeded = reserve_model_invocation(
                db,
                attempt_id=targets[0][0],
                node=targets[0][1],
                run_snapshot=targets[0][2],
                reserved_images=1,
            )
            succeeded_id = succeeded.id
            db.commit()
        with Session(engine) as db:
            settle_model_invocation(
                db,
                invocation_id=succeeded_id,
                request_correlation_id="req-success",
                upstream_status_code=200,
                input_tokens=100,
                output_tokens=50,
                evidence={"response_hash": "b" * 64},
            )
            db.commit()
            assert model_quota_snapshot(db, quota_pool_id=pool_id)[
                "active_concurrency"
            ] == 0

        with Session(engine) as db:
            rate_limited = reserve_model_invocation(
                db,
                attempt_id=targets[1][0],
                node=targets[1][1],
                run_snapshot=targets[1][2],
                reserved_images=1,
            )
            rate_limited_id = rate_limited.id
            db.commit()
        with Session(engine) as db:
            fail_model_invocation(
                db,
                invocation_id=rate_limited_id,
                error_code="model_provider_429",
                error_message="模型服务暂时不可用",
                upstream_status_code=429,
                evidence={"retryable": True, "retry_after_seconds": provider_delay},
            )
            db.commit()
            snapshot = model_quota_snapshot(db, quota_pool_id=pool_id)
            assert snapshot["active_concurrency"] == 0
            assert snapshot["cooling_down"] is True
            pool = db.get(ModelQuotaPool, pool_id)
            assert (pool.cooldown_until - pool.updated_at).total_seconds() == expected_delay
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_model_invocation_reservation_stops_before_exceeding_request_cap(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    """Catches reserving a provider call after the frozen request cap is exhausted."""

    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.workflow_model_budget import (
        WorkflowModelBudgetError,
        reserve_model_invocation,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-reservation-cap.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)
            aggregate = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="real-reservation-cap",
                model_execution_mode="real",
                real_execution_confirmed=True,
            )
            production = db.get(
                ProductionRun, aggregate.items[0].production_run_id
            )
            assert production is not None
            snapshot = json.loads(production.snapshot_json)
            snapshot["runtime_context"]["real_execution_budget"][
                "max_requests"
            ] = 0
            production.snapshot_json = canonical_json(snapshot)
            production.snapshot_hash = hashlib.sha256(
                production.snapshot_json.encode("utf-8")
            ).hexdigest()
            production_id = production.id
            attempt_id = db.scalar(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt.id).where(
                    ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == production.id,
                    ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == "prompt_c",
                )
            )
            db.commit()

        with Session(engine) as db:
            production = db.get(ProductionRun, production_id)
            assert production is not None and attempt_id is not None
            snapshot = json.loads(production.snapshot_json)
            node = next(
                item
                for item in snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]
                if item["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_c"
            )
            with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError) as exc_info:
                reserve_model_invocation(
                    db,
                    attempt_id=attempt_id,
                    node=node,
                    run_snapshot=snapshot,
                )

            assert exc_info.value.code == "model_budget_requests_exhausted"
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id))) == 0
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_model_invocation_reservation_stops_before_exceeding_cost_cap(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    """Catches dispatch after cumulative reserved cost reaches the hard stop."""

    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    import app.workflow_model_budget as budget_module

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-reservation-cost-cap.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)
            aggregate = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="real-reservation-cost-cap",
                model_execution_mode="real",
                real_execution_confirmed=True,
            )
            production_id = aggregate.items[0].production_run_id
            attempt_id = db.scalar(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt.id).where(
                    ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == production_id,
                    ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == "prompt_c",
                )
            )
            db.commit()

        # 全局天花板 2026-09-16 起默认关闭；这里显式打开，因为本测试要证的就是它。
        monkeypatch.setattr(budget_module, "REAL_EXECUTION_CEILINGS_ENFORCED", True)
        monkeypatch.setattr(budget_module, "MAX_REAL_COST_MICROS", 1)
        with Session(engine) as db:
            production = db.get(ProductionRun, production_id)
            assert production is not None and attempt_id is not None
            snapshot = json.loads(production.snapshot_json)
            node = next(
                item
                for item in snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]
                if item["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_c"
            )
            with pytest.raises(budget_module.WorkflowModelBudgetError) as exc_info:
                budget_module.reserve_model_invocation(
                    db,
                    attempt_id=attempt_id,
                    node=node,
                    run_snapshot=snapshot,
                )

            assert exc_info.value.code == "model_budget_cost_exhausted"
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id))) == 0
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("settlement", ["success", "known_failure", "unknown_failure", "pending"])
def test_settled_usage_releases_headroom_but_unknown_liability_stays_reserved(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch, settlement,
) -> None:
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    import app.workflow_model_budget as budget

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'settled-headroom.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            _add_level_truth_item(db, baseline, suffix="headroom-2")
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)
            aggregate = create_native_baseline_run(
                db, baseline_set=baseline, workflow_version=workflow, actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="headroom", model_execution_mode="real",
                real_execution_confirmed=True,
            )
            targets = []
            for item in aggregate.items:
                production = db.get(ProductionRun, item.production_run_id)
                snapshot = json.loads(production.snapshot_json)
                node = next(n for n in snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]
                            if n["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_c")
                attempt_id = db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt.id).where(
                    ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == production.id,
                    ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == "prompt_c",
                ))
                targets.append(dict(attempt_id=attempt_id, node=node, run_snapshot=snapshot))
            first = budget.reserve_model_invocation(db, **targets[0])
            reserved = first.estimated_cost_micros
            if settlement == "success":
                budget.settle_model_invocation(
                    db, invocation_id=first.id, request_correlation_id="headroom",
                    upstream_status_code=200, input_tokens=10, output_tokens=5,
                    evidence={},
                )
            elif settlement.endswith("failure"):
                budget.fail_model_invocation(
                    db, invocation_id=first.id, error_code="invalid_json",
                    error_message="invalid JSON", provider_usage=(
                        {"input_tokens": 10, "output_tokens": 5}
                        if settlement == "known_failure" else None
                    ),
                )
            # Enough for one fresh worst-case reservation plus known usage,
            # never enough for two unknown reservations.
            monkeypatch.setattr(budget, "REAL_EXECUTION_CEILINGS_ENFORCED", True)
            monkeypatch.setattr(budget, "MAX_REAL_COST_MICROS", reserved + reserved // 2)
            if settlement in {"success", "known_failure"}:
                second = budget.reserve_model_invocation(db, **targets[1])
                assert second.status == "reserved"
                assert first.estimated_cost_micros == reserved
                assert first.actual_cost_micros < reserved // 2
            else:
                with pytest.raises(budget.WorkflowModelBudgetError) as error:
                    budget.reserve_model_invocation(db, **targets[1])
                assert error.value.code == "model_budget_cost_exhausted"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_native_run_cost_cap_is_frozen_and_part_of_idempotency(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run, NativeBaselineRegressionError

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'run-cap.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)
            args = dict(baseline_set=baseline, workflow_version=workflow, actor="ops",
                        idempotency_key="cap", model_execution_mode="real",
                        real_execution_confirmed=True, cost_cap_micros=100)
            run = create_native_baseline_run(db, **args)
            assert json.loads(run.execution_snapshot_json)["real_execution_budget"]["max_cost_micros"] == 100
            for item in run.items:
                snapshot = json.loads(db.get(ProductionRun, item.production_run_id).snapshot_json)
                assert snapshot["runtime_context"]["real_execution_budget"]["max_cost_micros"] == 100
            assert create_native_baseline_run(db, **args).id == run.id
            with pytest.raises(NativeBaselineRegressionError) as error:
                create_native_baseline_run(db, **{**args, "cost_cap_micros": 101})
            assert error.value.code == "native_baseline_idempotency_conflict"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_model_invocation_reservation_stops_after_two_consecutive_failures(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch
) -> None:
    """Catches continuing paid calls after the approved failure stop condition."""

    # 生产默认连击阈值已提为 4；本测试固定为 2 验证熔断语义本身。
    monkeypatch.setenv("MODEL_FAILURE_STREAK_LIMIT", "2")

    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.workflow_model_budget import (
        WorkflowModelBudgetError,
        fail_model_invocation,
        reserve_model_invocation,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-failure-streak.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            for index in (2, 3):
                asset = Asset(
                    original_name=f"failure-streak-{index}.jpg",
                    stored_name=f"failure-streak-{index}.jpg",
                    mime_type="image/jpeg",
                    size_bytes=20,
                    sha256=f"{index}" * 64,
                    category_key="space_image",
                )
                db.add(asset)
                db.flush()
                db.add(
                    BaselineSetItem(
                        baseline_set_id=baseline.id,
                        asset_id=asset.id,
                        expected_level="L2",
                        expected_result_json=canonical_json({"level": "L2"}),
                        truth_contract_key="level.v1",
                        asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
                            {"asset_id": asset.id, "sha256": asset.sha256}
                        ),
                    )
                )
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)
            aggregate = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="real-failure-streak",
                model_execution_mode="real",
                real_execution_confirmed=True,
            )
            productions = [
                db.get(ProductionRun, item.production_run_id)
                for item in aggregate.items
            ]
            assert all(item is not None for item in productions)

            for production in productions[:2]:
                assert production is not None
                attempt = db.scalar(
                    select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                        ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == production.id,
                        ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == "prompt_c",
                    )
                )
                assert attempt is not None
                snapshot = json.loads(production.snapshot_json)
                node = next(
                    item
                    for item in snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]
                    if item["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_c"
                )
                invocation = reserve_model_invocation(
                    db,
                    attempt_id=attempt.id,
                    node=node,
                    run_snapshot=snapshot,
                )
                fail_model_invocation(
                    db,
                    invocation_id=invocation.id,
                    error_code="model_provider_timeout",
                    error_message="模型响应超时",
                    evidence={"retryable": True},
                )

            third = productions[2]
            assert third is not None
            third_attempt = db.scalar(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                    ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == third.id,
                    ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == "prompt_c",
                )
            )
            assert third_attempt is not None
            third_snapshot = json.loads(third.snapshot_json)
            third_node = next(
                item
                for item in third_snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]
                if item["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_c"
            )

            with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError) as exc_info:
                reserve_model_invocation(
                    db,
                    attempt_id=third_attempt.id,
                    node=third_node,
                    run_snapshot=third_snapshot,
                )

            assert exc_info.value.code == "model_failure_streak_exhausted"
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id))) == 2
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_database_lock_failures_do_not_trip_the_model_failure_breaker(
    tmp_path, monkeypatch
) -> None:
    """数据库锁超时不是模型故障，不能熔断整轮回归。

    2026-08-28 run133：250 条素材里 18 次调用成功后，两次 reserve 撞上 SQLite
    写锁超时（attempt_count=0，付费请求根本没发出去），被记成「连续两次模型失败」
    触发熔断，剩下 246 条零调用直接判死、整轮 0 条成功。
    """

    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.workflow_model_budget import (
        INFRASTRUCTURE_FAILURE_CODE,
        fail_model_invocation,
        reserve_model_invocation,
    )

    monkeypatch.setenv("MODEL_FAILURE_STREAK_LIMIT", "2")
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'infra-failure.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            for index in (2, 3):
                asset = Asset(
                    original_name=f"infra-{index}.jpg",
                    stored_name=f"infra-{index}.jpg",
                    mime_type="image/jpeg",
                    size_bytes=20,
                    sha256=f"{index}" * 64,
                    category_key="space_image",
                )
                db.add(asset)
                db.flush()
                db.add(
                    BaselineSetItem(
                        baseline_set_id=baseline.id,
                        asset_id=asset.id,
                        expected_level="L2",
                        expected_result_json=canonical_json({"level": "L2"}),
                        truth_contract_key="level.v1",
                        asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
                            {"asset_id": asset.id, "sha256": asset.sha256}
                        ),
                    )
                )
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)
            aggregate = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="infra-failure",
                model_execution_mode="real",
                real_execution_confirmed=True,
            )
            productions = [
                db.get(ProductionRun, item.production_run_id)
                for item in aggregate.items
            ]

            # 前两条按「数据库繁忙」结算——付费请求没发出去。
            for production in productions[:2]:
                assert production is not None
                attempt = db.scalar(
                    select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                        ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == production.id,
                        ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == "prompt_c",
                    )
                )
                assert attempt is not None
                snapshot = json.loads(production.snapshot_json)
                node = next(
                    item
                    for item in snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]
                    if item["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_c"
                )
                invocation = reserve_model_invocation(
                    db,
                    attempt_id=attempt.id,
                    node=node,
                    run_snapshot=snapshot,
                )
                fail_model_invocation(
                    db,
                    invocation_id=invocation.id,
                    error_code=INFRASTRUCTURE_FAILURE_CODE,
                    error_message="数据库繁忙，本次未发出模型请求，稍后自动重试",
                    evidence={"retryable": True, "attempt_count": 0},
                )

            # 第三条必须还能继续预约：整轮不该被基础设施抖动判死。
            third = productions[2]
            assert third is not None
            third_attempt = db.scalar(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                    ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == third.id,
                    ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == "prompt_c",
                )
            )
            assert third_attempt is not None
            third_snapshot = json.loads(third.snapshot_json)
            third_node = next(
                item
                for item in third_snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]
                if item["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_c"
            )
            survivor = reserve_model_invocation(
                db,
                attempt_id=third_attempt.id,
                node=third_node,
                run_snapshot=third_snapshot,
            )
            assert survivor.status == "reserved"
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id))) == 3
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def _ledger_row(
    db: Session,
    *,
    step_attempt_id: int = 1,
) -> WorkflowModelInvocation:
    run = ProductionRun(
        id=1,
        run_key=f"ledger-run-{step_attempt_id}",
        idempotency_key=f"ledger-run-{step_attempt_id}",
        workflow_definition_id=1,
        workflow_version_id=1,
        snapshot_json="{}",
        snapshot_hash="a" * 64,
        queue_class="validation",
        status="running",
        blockers_json="[]",
        requested_by="tests",
        owner="tests",
        reason="model ledger unit fixture",
        environment="dry_run",
        model_execution_mode="real",
        total_steps=1,
        completed_steps=0,
        failed_steps=0,
        attempt_count=0,
        error_code="",
        error_message="",
    )
    attempt = ProductionStepAttempt(
        id=step_attempt_id,
        run_id=1,
        step_key="prompt_a",
        step_type="model_call",
        sequence=1,
        script_version_id=1,
        status="running",
        attempt_no=1,
        idempotency_key=f"ledger-attempt-{step_attempt_id}",
        input_manifest_json="{}",
        input_hash="b" * 64,
        output_manifest_json="{}",
        checkpoint_json="{}",
        last_error_code="",
        last_error_message="",
    )
    db.add_all((run, attempt))
    db.flush()
    return WorkflowModelInvocation(
        baseline_run_id=1,
        production_run_id=1,
        step_attempt_id=step_attempt_id,
        node_instance_id="prompt_a",
        status="reserved",
        provider="doubao",
        model_id="budget-model",
        reserved_input_tokens=100,
        reserved_output_tokens=50,
        estimated_cost_micros=20,
        evidence_json=canonical_json(
            {
                "schema_version": "workflow-model-invocation-v1",
                "reservation": {"snapshot_hash": "a" * 64},
            }
        ),
    )


def test_settle_model_invocation_persists_actual_usage_and_result_evidence(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    """Catches successful provider calls remaining reserved or losing token evidence."""

    from app.workflow_model_budget import settle_model_invocation

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-settlement.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            row = _ledger_row(db)
            db.add(row)
            db.flush()

            settled = settle_model_invocation(
                db,
                invocation_id=row.id,
                request_correlation_id="req-safe-123",
                upstream_status_code=200,
                input_tokens=80,
                output_tokens=30,
                evidence={"response_hash": "b" * 64},
            )
            db.flush()

            assert settled.status == "succeeded"
            assert settled.request_correlation_id == "req-safe-123"
            assert settled.upstream_status_code == 200
            assert (settled.input_tokens, settled.output_tokens, settled.total_tokens) == (
                80,
                30,
                110,
            )
            persisted = json.loads(settled.evidence_json)
            assert persisted["reservation"]["snapshot_hash"] == "a" * 64
            assert persisted["result"] == {"response_hash": "b" * 64}
            assert settled.finished_at is not None
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_failed_parse_settles_usage_without_becoming_success(tmp_path):
    from app.workflow_model_budget import fail_model_invocation
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'failed-usage.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            row = _ledger_row(db)
            row.pricing_snapshot_json = canonical_json(doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing())
            db.add(row)
            db.flush()
            kwargs = dict(invocation_id=row.id, error_code="model_provider_json_truncated",
                error_message="truncated", upstream_status_code=200,
                provider_usage={"input_tokens": 90, "output_tokens": 40,
                    "cached_input_tokens": None, "cache_observation_status": "unreported"})
            result = fail_model_invocation(db, **kwargs)
            assert result.status == "failed"
            assert result.total_tokens == 130
            assert result.actual_cost_micros > 0
            assert result.error_code == "model_provider_json_truncated"
            assert fail_model_invocation(db, **kwargs).actual_cost_micros == result.actual_cost_micros
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("reported", [True, False])
def test_executor_parse_failure_keeps_usage_or_pending_reconciliation(tmp_path, monkeypatch, reported):
    from app import workflow_fixture_executor as executor
    from app.doubao import DoubaoParseError
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'executor-usage.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "SessionLocal", sessions)
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            row = _ledger_row(db)
            row.pricing_snapshot_json = canonical_json(doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing())
            db.add(row)
            db.commit()
            invocation_id = row.id
        error = DoubaoParseError("truncated", truncated=True)
        error.upstream_status_code = 200
        error.finish_reason = "length"
        if reported:
            error.provider_usage = {"input_tokens": 90, "output_tokens": 40,
                "cached_input_tokens": None, "cache_observation_status": "unreported"}
        executor._model_failure(invocation_id, code="model_provider_json_truncated",
            message="truncated", retryable=True, error=error, provider_dispatched=True)
        with sessions() as db:
            row = db.get(WorkflowModelInvocation, invocation_id)
            failure = json.loads(row.evidence_json)["failure"]
            assert row.status == "failed"
            assert row.estimated_cost_micros == 20
            assert row.actual_cost_micros == (198 if reported else 0)
            assert failure["finish_reason"] == "length"
            assert failure["usage_status"] == ("provider_reported" if reported else "pending_reconciliation")
    finally:
        engine.dispose()
