                    WorkflowModelInvocation(
                        baseline_run_id=run.id,
                        production_run_id=4,
                        step_attempt_id=4,
                        invocation_key="item:4",
                        node_instance_id="prompt_a",
                        status="succeeded",
                        provider="doubao",
                        model_id="seed",
                        estimated_cost_micros=10,
                        actual_cost_micros=0,
                        pricing_tier_key="flat",
                        evidence_json="{}",
                    ),
                ]
            )
            db.flush()

            model_cost = _baseline_run_summary(run)["model_cost"]

            assert model_cost == {
                "schema_version": "baseline-model-cost-v2",
                "invocation_count": 4,
                "succeeded": 2,
                "failed": 2,
                "reserved": 0,
                "input_tokens": 120,
                "output_tokens": 30,
                "estimated_cost_micros": 60,
                "reserved_cost_micros": 170,
                "actual_cost_micros": 35,
                "actual_cost_known": False,
                "unknown_invocation_count": 2,
                "variance_micros": None,
                "tiers": [{"tier_key": "0-32000", "count": 2}],
                "reserved_tiers": [
                    {"tier_key": "0-32000", "count": 1},
                    {"tier_key": "32000-128000", "count": 2},
                    {"tier_key": "flat", "count": 1},
                ],
                "cache_observation": {
                    "schema_version": "baseline-cache-observation-v1",
                    "cached_input_tokens": 0,
                    "observed_input_tokens": 0,
                    "cached_token_ratio": None,
                    "hit_count": 0,
                    "miss_count": 0,
                    "unreported_count": 0,
                    "invalid_count": 0,
                    "legacy_count": 2,
                    "cache_savings_micros": None,
                    "cache_adjusted_cost_exact": False,
                },
                "complete": False,
            }
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_running_baseline_never_reports_actual_cost_as_known(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.main import _baseline_run_summary

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'running-cost-summary.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run = BaselineRegressionRun(
                baseline_set_id=1,
                sequence_no=1,
                strategy_bundle_id=None,
                workflow_version_id=None,
                execution_engine="legacy_worker",
                category_key="inspiration_image",
                strategy_snapshot_json="{}",
                execution_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
                    {
                        "model_execution_mode": "real",
                        "real_execution_budget": {
                            "expected_cost_micros": 60,
                            "max_cost_micros": 90,
                        },
                    }
                ),
                baseline_set_fingerprint="f" * 64,
                status="running",
                total=1,
                completed=0,
                valid_predictions=0,
                failed=0,
                metrics_json="{}",
                created_by="ops",
            )
            db.add(run)
            db.flush()

            model_cost = _baseline_run_summary(run)["model_cost"]

            assert model_cost["actual_cost_micros"] == 0
            assert model_cost["actual_cost_known"] is False
            assert model_cost["variance_micros"] is None
            assert model_cost["complete"] is False
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_settle_model_invocation_reprices_actual_usage_from_frozen_v3_contract(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.workflow_model_budget import settle_model_invocation
    from app.migrations.runner import MIGRATIONS

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'tiered-settlement.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        next(item for item in MIGRATIONS if item.version == 96).up(connection)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            row = _ledger_row(db, step_attempt_id=44)
            row.pricing_snapshot_json = canonical_json(
                doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing()
            )
            row.pricing_tier_key = "32000-128000"
            row.estimated_cost_micros = 34_201
            row.reserved_input_tokens = 32_001
            row.reserved_output_tokens = 1_000
            db.add(row)
            db.flush()

            settled = settle_model_invocation(
                db,
                invocation_id=row.id,
                request_correlation_id="req-tiered-actual",
                upstream_status_code=200,
                input_tokens=80,
                output_tokens=30,
                evidence={"response_hash": "c" * 64},
            )

            assert settled.actual_cost_micros == 156
            assert settled.pricing_tier_key == "0-32000"
            assert json.loads(settled.pricing_snapshot_json) == (
                doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing()
            )
            pricing = json.loads(settled.evidence_json)["pricing_settlement"]
            assert pricing["schema_version"] == (
                "workflow-model-pricing-settlement-v2"
            )
            assert pricing["settlement_basis"] == "conservative_full_input"
            assert pricing["cache_observation_status"] == "unreported"
            assert pricing["cached_input_tokens"] == 0
            assert pricing["pricing_tier_key"] == "0-32000"
            assert pricing["actual_cost_micros"] == 156
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    (
        "cache_status",
        "reported_cached_tokens",
        "expected_cost",
        "expected_basis",
        "expected_savings",
    ),
    [
        ("hit", 7_000, 2_520, "provider_cache_reported", 3_360),
        ("miss", 0, 5_880, "provider_cache_reported", 0),
        ("unreported", None, 5_880, "conservative_full_input", 0),
        ("invalid", None, 5_880, "conservative_full_input", 0),
    ],
)
def test_settle_model_invocation_uses_only_valid_provider_cache_observation(
    tmp_path,
    cache_status: str,
    reported_cached_tokens: int | None,
    expected_cost: int,
    expected_basis: str,
    expected_savings: int,
) -> None:
    from app.workflow_model_budget import settle_model_invocation

    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / f'cache-settlement-{cache_status}.db'}"
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            row = _ledger_row(db, step_attempt_id=45)
            row.pricing_snapshot_json = canonical_json(
                doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing()
            )
            row.reserved_input_tokens = 8_000
            row.reserved_output_tokens = 300
            db.add(row)
            db.flush()

            settled = settle_model_invocation(
                db,
                invocation_id=row.id,
                request_correlation_id=f"req-cache-{cache_status}",
                upstream_status_code=200,
                input_tokens=8_000,
                output_tokens=300,
                cached_input_tokens=reported_cached_tokens,
                cache_observation_status=cache_status,
                evidence={"response_hash": "d" * 64},
            )

            pricing = json.loads(settled.evidence_json)["pricing_settlement"]
            assert settled.actual_cost_micros == expected_cost
            assert pricing["settlement_basis"] == expected_basis
            assert pricing["cache_observation_status"] == cache_status
            assert pricing["reported_cached_input_tokens"] == (
                reported_cached_tokens
            )
            assert pricing["cached_input_tokens"] == (
                reported_cached_tokens
                if expected_basis == "provider_cache_reported"
                else 0
            )
            assert pricing["uncached_input_tokens"] == (
                8_000 - pricing["cached_input_tokens"]
            )
            assert pricing["full_price_cost_micros"] == 5_880
            assert pricing["cache_savings_micros"] == expected_savings
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_settle_model_invocation_keeps_frozen_v2_at_full_input_price(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.workflow_model_budget import settle_model_invocation

    legacy = doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing()
    legacy["schema_version"] = "model-pricing-v2"
    legacy["source_url"] = "https://www.volcengine.com/docs/84458/1585097"
    for tier in legacy["tiers"]:
        tier.pop("cached_input_micros_per_1k")

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'legacy-v2-settlement.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            row = _ledger_row(db, step_attempt_id=46)
            row.pricing_snapshot_json = canonical_json(legacy)
            row.reserved_input_tokens = 8_000
            row.reserved_output_tokens = 300
            db.add(row)
            db.flush()

            settled = settle_model_invocation(
                db,
                invocation_id=row.id,
                request_correlation_id="req-cache-legacy",
                upstream_status_code=200,
                input_tokens=8_000,
                output_tokens=300,
                cached_input_tokens=7_000,
                cache_observation_status="hit",
                evidence={"response_hash": "e" * 64},
            )

            pricing = json.loads(settled.evidence_json)["pricing_settlement"]
            assert settled.actual_cost_micros == 5_880
            assert pricing["settlement_basis"] == "legacy_no_cache_rate"
            assert pricing["reported_cached_input_tokens"] == 7_000
            assert pricing["cached_input_tokens"] == 0
            assert pricing["cache_savings_micros"] == 0
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_legacy_v1_terminal_settlement_replays_only_without_new_cache_facts(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.model_pricing import pricing_contract_hash
    from app.workflow_model_budget import (
        WorkflowModelBudgetError,
        settle_model_invocation,
    )

    legacy = doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing()
    legacy["schema_version"] = "model-pricing-v2"
    legacy["source_url"] = "https://www.volcengine.com/docs/84458/1585097"
    for tier in legacy["tiers"]:
        tier.pop("cached_input_micros_per_1k")

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'legacy-v1-replay.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            legacy_result = {
                "schema_version": "workflow-model-evidence-v1",
                "invocation_key": "case-461:attempt-1",
                "provider": "doubao",
                "model_id": "doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260428",
                "request_correlation_id": "req-cache-legacy-v1",
                "upstream_status_code": 200,
                "attempt_count": 1,
                "input_tokens": 8_000,
                "output_tokens": 300,
                "total_tokens": 8_300,
                "output_budget": 2_048,
                "thinking_mode": "disabled",
                "reasoning_effort": None,
                "component_hash": "b" * 64,
                "model_snapshot_hash": "c" * 64,
                "media_usage": [],
                "raw_payload": {"id": "req-cache-legacy-v1"},
                "parsed_output": {"score": 88, "label": "通过"},
                "coercions": [],
            }
            current_unreported_result = {
                **legacy_result,
                "cache_observation": {
                    "status": "unreported",
                    "cached_input_tokens": None,
                    "source": None,
                },
            }
            row = _ledger_row(db, step_attempt_id=461)
            row.pricing_snapshot_json = canonical_json(legacy)
            row.pricing_tier_key = "0-32000"
            row.reserved_input_tokens = 8_000
            row.reserved_output_tokens = 300
            row.status = "succeeded"
            row.request_correlation_id = "req-cache-legacy-v1"
            row.upstream_status_code = 200
            row.input_tokens = 8_000
            row.output_tokens = 300
            row.total_tokens = 8_300
            row.actual_cost_micros = 5_880
            row.evidence_json = canonical_json(
                {
                    "schema_version": "workflow-model-invocation-v1",
                    "reservation": {"snapshot_hash": "a" * 64},
                    "result": legacy_result,
                    "pricing_settlement": {
                        "schema_version": "workflow-model-pricing-settlement-v1",
                        "pricing_version": "model-pricing-v2",
                        "pricing_tier_key": "0-32000",
                        "pricing_contract_hash": pricing_contract_hash(legacy),
                        "input_tokens": 8_000,
                        "output_tokens": 300,
                        "actual_cost_micros": 5_880,
                    },
                }
            )
            frozen_evidence = row.evidence_json
            db.add(row)
            db.flush()

            duplicate = settle_model_invocation(
                db,
                invocation_id=row.id,
                request_correlation_id="req-cache-legacy-v1",
                upstream_status_code=200,
                input_tokens=8_000,
                output_tokens=300,
                evidence=current_unreported_result,
            )

            assert duplicate.id == row.id
            assert duplicate.evidence_json == frozen_evidence

            with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError) as exc_info:
                settle_model_invocation(
                    db,
                    invocation_id=row.id,
                    request_correlation_id="req-cache-legacy-v1",
                    upstream_status_code=200,
                    input_tokens=8_000,
                    output_tokens=300,
                    cached_input_tokens=7_000,
                    cache_observation_status="hit",
                    evidence={
                        **legacy_result,
                        "cache_observation": {
                            "status": "hit",
                            "cached_input_tokens": 7_000,
                            "source": (
                                "usage.prompt_tokens_details.cached_tokens"
                            ),
                        },
                    },
                )

            assert exc_info.value.code == (
                "model_invocation_idempotency_conflict"
            )

            with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError) as exc_info:
                settle_model_invocation(
                    db,
                    invocation_id=row.id,
                    request_correlation_id="req-cache-legacy-v1",
                    upstream_status_code=200,
                    input_tokens=8_000,
                    output_tokens=300,
                    evidence={
                        **current_unreported_result,
                        "parsed_output": {"score": 89, "label": "通过"},
                    },
                )

            assert exc_info.value.code == (
                "model_invocation_idempotency_conflict"
            )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_cache_aware_settlement_replay_rejects_changed_cached_tokens(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.workflow_model_budget import (
        WorkflowModelBudgetError,
        settle_model_invocation,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'cache-replay.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            row = _ledger_row(db, step_attempt_id=47)
            row.pricing_snapshot_json = canonical_json(
                doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing()
            )
            row.reserved_input_tokens = 8_000
            row.reserved_output_tokens = 300
            db.add(row)
            db.flush()
            arguments = {
                "invocation_id": row.id,
                "request_correlation_id": "req-cache-replay",
                "upstream_status_code": 200,
                "input_tokens": 8_000,
                "output_tokens": 300,
                "cache_observation_status": "hit",
                "evidence": {"response_hash": "f" * 64},
            }

            first = settle_model_invocation(
                db,
                cached_input_tokens=7_000,
                **arguments,
            )
            duplicate = settle_model_invocation(
                db,
                cached_input_tokens=7_000,
                **arguments,
            )
            assert duplicate.id == first.id

            with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError) as exc_info:
                settle_model_invocation(
                    db,
                    cached_input_tokens=6_999,
                    **arguments,
                )

            assert exc_info.value.code == (
                "model_invocation_idempotency_conflict"
            )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_settle_model_invocation_rejects_different_terminal_replay(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    """Catches a duplicate provider callback rewriting immutable usage evidence."""

    from app.workflow_model_budget import (
        WorkflowModelBudgetError,
        settle_model_invocation,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-settlement-replay.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            row = _ledger_row(db, step_attempt_id=3)
            db.add(row)
            db.flush()
            settle_model_invocation(
                db,
                invocation_id=row.id,
                request_correlation_id="req-safe-123",
                upstream_status_code=200,
                input_tokens=80,
                output_tokens=30,
                evidence={"response_hash": "b" * 64},
            )
            first_finished_at = row.finished_at
            duplicate = settle_model_invocation(
                db,
                invocation_id=row.id,
                request_correlation_id="req-safe-123",
                upstream_status_code=200,
                input_tokens=80,
                output_tokens=30,
                evidence={"response_hash": "b" * 64},
            )
            assert duplicate.id == row.id
            assert duplicate.finished_at == first_finished_at

            with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError) as exc_info:
                settle_model_invocation(
                    db,
                    invocation_id=row.id,
                    request_correlation_id="req-safe-456",
                    upstream_status_code=200,
                    input_tokens=81,
                    output_tokens=31,
                    evidence={"response_hash": "c" * 64},
                )

            assert exc_info.value.code == "model_invocation_idempotency_conflict"
            assert row.request_correlation_id == "req-safe-123"
            assert (row.input_tokens, row.output_tokens) == (80, 30)
            assert json.loads(row.evidence_json)["result"] == {
                "response_hash": "b" * 64
            }
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_fail_model_invocation_records_terminal_safe_error(tmp_path) -> None:
    """Catches failed provider calls leaving a reusable reserved ledger row."""

    from app.workflow_model_budget import fail_model_invocation

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-failure.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            row = _ledger_row(db, step_attempt_id=2)
            db.add(row)
            db.flush()

            failed = fail_model_invocation(
                db,
                invocation_id=row.id,
                error_code="model_provider_timeout",
                error_message="模型响应超时",
                request_correlation_id="req-timeout-safe",
                upstream_status_code=504,
                evidence={"retryable": True},
            )
            db.flush()

            assert failed.status == "failed"
            assert failed.error_code == "model_provider_timeout"
            assert failed.error_message == "模型响应超时"
            assert failed.request_correlation_id == "req-timeout-safe"
            assert failed.upstream_status_code == 504
            assert json.loads(failed.evidence_json)["failure"] == {
                "retryable": True
            }
            assert failed.finished_at is not None
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_fail_model_invocation_rejects_different_terminal_replay(tmp_path) -> None:
    """Catches a later failure handler overwriting the first terminal error."""

    from app.workflow_model_budget import (
        WorkflowModelBudgetError,
        fail_model_invocation,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-failure-replay.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            row = _ledger_row(db, step_attempt_id=4)
            db.add(row)
            db.flush()
            fail_model_invocation(
                db,
                invocation_id=row.id,
                error_code="model_provider_timeout",
                error_message="模型响应超时",
                request_correlation_id="req-timeout-safe",
                upstream_status_code=504,
                evidence={"retryable": True},
            )
            first_finished_at = row.finished_at
            duplicate = fail_model_invocation(
                db,
                invocation_id=row.id,
                error_code="model_provider_timeout",
                error_message="模型响应超时",
                request_correlation_id="req-timeout-safe",
                upstream_status_code=504,
                evidence={"retryable": True},
            )
            assert duplicate.id == row.id
            assert duplicate.finished_at == first_finished_at

            with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError) as exc_info:
                fail_model_invocation(
                    db,
                    invocation_id=row.id,
                    error_code="model_provider_error",
                    error_message="模型返回其他错误",
                    request_correlation_id="req-other",
                    upstream_status_code=500,
                    evidence={"retryable": False},
                )

            assert exc_info.value.code == "model_invocation_idempotency_conflict"
            assert row.error_code == "model_provider_timeout"
            assert row.request_correlation_id == "req-timeout-safe"
            assert json.loads(row.evidence_json)["failure"] == {
                "retryable": True
            }
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_real_native_worker_calls_one_model_and_completes_local_nodes(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """Catches the leased worker still returning prompt fixtures in real mode."""

    from app.doubao import DoubaoResponse
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.workflow_fixture_executor import process_runtime_step_once

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-worker.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        image_bytes = _real_jpeg_bytes()
        image_path = tmp_path / "native-stock.jpg"
        image_path.write_bytes(image_bytes)
        with sessions() as db:
            asset = Asset(
                original_name="native-stock.jpg",
                stored_name="native-stock.jpg",
                mime_type="image/jpeg",
                size_bytes=len(image_bytes),
                sha256=hashlib.sha256(image_bytes).hexdigest(),
                category_key="space_image",
            )
            baseline = BaselineSet(
                category_key="space_image",
                name="真实原生 Worker 回归",
                description="",
                default_expected_level="L1",
                fingerprint="8" * 64,
                created_by="ops",
            )
            db.add_all([asset, baseline])
            db.flush()
            db.add(
                BaselineSetItem(
                    baseline_set_id=baseline.id,
                    asset_id=asset.id,
                    expected_level="L2",
                    expected_result_json=canonical_json({"level": "L2"}),
                    truth_contract_key="level.v1",
                    asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
                        {"asset_id": asset.id, "sha256": asset.sha256}
                    ),
                )
            )
            db.flush()
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            model = _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)
            quota_pool = ModelQuotaPool(
                pool_key="real-worker-endpoint",
                name="真实回归测试端点",
                provider="doubao",
                scope_kind="endpoint",
                scope_identifier="ep-real-worker",
                max_concurrency=1,
                lease_seconds=60,
                enabled=True,
                created_by="test",
            )
            db.add(quota_pool)
            db.flush()
            model.quota_pool_id = quota_pool.id
            quota_pool_id = quota_pool.id
            model.encrypted_api_key = "encrypted:test-only-reference"
            aggregate = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="real-worker",
                model_execution_mode="real",
                real_execution_confirmed=True,
            )
            aggregate_id = aggregate.id
            production_id = aggregate.items[0].production_run_id
            db.commit()

        import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor
        import app.workflow_model_executor as model_executor

        monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "SessionLocal", sessions)
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            model_executor,
            "get_settings",
            lambda: SimpleNamespace(upload_dir=tmp_path, nas_maps_root=None),
        )
        calls: list[dict] = []

        class FakeDoubaoClient:
            def __init__(self, config):
                self.config = config

            async def chat_json(self, system_prompt, user_prompt, **options):
                with sessions() as dispatch_db:
                    from app.model_quota import model_quota_snapshot

                    assert model_quota_snapshot(
                        dispatch_db, quota_pool_id=quota_pool_id
                    )["active_concurrency"] == 1
                    outbound_media = dispatch_db.scalars(
                        select(WorkflowModelMediaUsage)
                    ).all()
                    assert len(outbound_media) == 1
                    assert outbound_media[0].media_role == "target"
                    assert outbound_media[0].usage_source == "unavailable"
                    assert outbound_media[0].settled_at is None
                calls.append(
                    {
                        "model_id": self.config.model_id,
                        "system_prompt": system_prompt,
                        "user_prompt": user_prompt,
                        "options": options,
                    }
                )
                return DoubaoResponse(
                    parsed={"level": "L2", "score": 88},
                    raw_text='{"level":"L2","score":88}',
                    raw_payload={"id": "req-real-worker"},
                    upstream_status_code=200,
                    request_correlation_id="req-real-worker",
                    attempt_count=1,
                    output_budget=options["output_budget"],
                    reasoning_effort=options["reasoning_effort"],
                    thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode,
                    input_image_tokens=80,
                    per_image_tokens=(80,),
                    input_tokens=200,
                    output_tokens=30,
                    total_tokens=230,
                )

        processed = 0
        while process_runtime_step_once(
            "real-worker",
            model_client_type=FakeDoubaoClient,
        ):
            processed += 1
            assert processed <= 8

        assert len(calls) == 1
        with sessions() as db:
            aggregate = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, aggregate_id)
            production = db.get(ProductionRun, production_id)
            invocation = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
            assert aggregate is not None and aggregate.status == "completed"
            assert aggregate.valid_predictions == 1
            assert production is not None and production.status == "succeeded"
            assert invocation is not None and invocation.status == "succeeded"
            assert invocation.quota_pool_id == quota_pool_id
            assert invocation.quota_lease_token is not None
            quota_lease = db.scalar(
                select(ModelQuotaLease).where(
                    ModelQuotaLease.lease_token == invocation.quota_lease_token
                )
            )
            assert quota_lease is not None and quota_lease.status == "released"
            assert invocation.input_tokens == 200
            media = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelMediaUsage))
            assert media is not None
            assert media.invocation_id == invocation.id
            assert media.media_role == "target"
            assert media.usage_source == "provider_actual"
            assert media.image_tokens == 80
            assert media.provider_correlation_id == "req-real-worker"
            assert media.settled_at is not None
            attempts = db.scalars(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt)
                .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == production_id)
                .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.sequence)
            ).all()
            prompt_attempt = next(
                item for item in attempts if item.step_key == "prompt_c"
            )
            prompt_result = json.loads(prompt_attempt.output_manifest_json)
            assert prompt_result["output"] == {"level": "L2", "score": 88}
            assert prompt_result["model_invocation"][
                "request_correlation_id"
            ] == "req-real-worker"
            assert all(item.status == "succeeded" for item in attempts)
            assert all(
                "model_invocation" not in json.loads(item.output_manifest_json)
                for item in attempts
                if item.step_key != "prompt_c"
            )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_correction_view_paid_rerun_calls_real_downstream_prompt_and_settles(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """A prompt-A correction must really rerun prompt B, never replay a fixture."""

    from app.correction_view import build_correction_view, submit_correction_nodes
    from app.doubao import DoubaoResponse
    from app.evaluation_component_catalog import canonical_component_hash
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.workflow_fixture_executor import process_runtime_step_once

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'paid-correction-rerun.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        image_bytes = _real_jpeg_bytes()
        (tmp_path / "paid-correction.jpg").write_bytes(image_bytes)
        with sessions() as db:
            asset = Asset(
                original_name="paid-correction.jpg",
                stored_name="paid-correction.jpg",
                mime_type="image/jpeg",
                size_bytes=len(image_bytes),
                sha256=hashlib.sha256(image_bytes).hexdigest(),
                category_key="space_image",
            )
            baseline = BaselineSet(
                category_key="space_image",
                name="付费纠偏重跑",
                description="",
                default_expected_level="L2",
                fingerprint="d" * 64,
                created_by="ops",
            )
            db.add_all([asset, baseline])
            db.flush()
            db.add(
                BaselineSetItem(
                    baseline_set_id=baseline.id,
                    asset_id=asset.id,
                    expected_level="L2",
                    expected_result_json=canonical_json({"level": "L2"}),
                    truth_contract_key="level.v1",
                    asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
                        {"asset_id": asset.id, "sha256": asset.sha256}
                    ),
                )
            )
            db.flush()
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)
            nodes = json.loads(workflow.step_manifest_json)
            prompt_a = next(node for node in nodes if node["kind"] == "prompt")
            prompt_a["node_instance_id"] = "prompt_a"
            prompt_a["display_name"] = "提示词 A"
            prompt_a_contract = {
                "mode": "json_schema",
                "name": "paid_prompt_a",
                "strict": True,
                "schema": {
                    "type": "object",
                    "properties": {
                        "style": {"type": "string", "title": "风格"}
                    },
                    "required": ["style"],
                    "additionalProperties": False,
                },
            }
            prompt_a_content = {
                "stage": "A",
                "system_prompt": "阶段A：识别风格",
                "user_prompt": "返回风格",
                "rubric_version": "paid-correction-v1",
            }
            prompt_a["config"].update(
                {
                    "max_tokens": 200,
                    "prompt_version_id": 31,
                    "structured_output": True,
                    "thinking_enabled": False,
                    "thinking_level": "none",
                    "output_contract": prompt_a_contract,
                    "component_snapshot": {
                        "kind": "prompt",
                        "version_id": 31,
                        "version": "paid-A-v1",
                        "content": prompt_a_content,
                        "content_hash": canonical_component_hash(
                            prompt_a_content
                        ),
                    },
                }
            )
            prompt_a["output_schema"] = prompt_a_contract["schema"]
            prompt_b = json.loads(canonical_json(prompt_a))
            prompt_b["node_instance_id"] = "prompt_b"
            prompt_b["display_name"] = "提示词 B"
            prompt_b_contract = {
                "mode": "json_schema",
                "name": "paid_prompt_b",
                "strict": True,
                "schema": {
                    "type": "object",
                    "properties": {
                        "level": {
                            "type": "string",
                            "title": "等级",
                            "enum": ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"],
                        },
                        "score": {
                            "type": "integer",
                            "title": "评分",
                            "minimum": 0,
                            "maximum": 100,
                        },
                    },
                    "required": ["level", "score"],
                    "additionalProperties": False,
                },
            }
            prompt_b_content = {
                "stage": "B",
                "system_prompt": "阶段B：根据A结果定级",
                "user_prompt": "根据 {{previous_output}} 返回等级",
                "rubric_version": "paid-correction-v1",
            }
            prompt_b["config"].update(
                {
                    "max_tokens": 200,
                    "asset_key": "prompt_b",
                    "prompt_version_id": 32,
                    "output_contract": prompt_b_contract,
                    "component_snapshot": {
                        "kind": "prompt",
                        "version_id": 32,
                        "version": "paid-B-v1",
                        "content": prompt_b_content,
                        "content_hash": canonical_component_hash(
                            prompt_b_content
                        ),
                    },
                }
            )
            prompt_b["output_schema"] = prompt_b_contract["schema"]
            redline = next(node for node in nodes if node["kind"] == "rule_module")
            redline["config"]["input_bindings"] = {
                "prompt": "prompt_b.output"
            }
            nodes.insert(nodes.index(redline), prompt_b)
            workflow.step_manifest_json = canonical_json(nodes)
            workflow.edge_manifest_json = canonical_json(
                [
                    {"from": "upstream_1", "to": "prompt_a"},
                    {"from": "prompt_a", "to": "prompt_b"},
                    {"from": "prompt_b", "to": "redline_1"},
                    {"from": "redline_1", "to": "downstream_1"},
                ]
            )
            workflow.canonical_hash = hashlib.sha256(
                canonical_json(persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow)).encode(
                    "utf-8"
                )
            ).hexdigest()
            db.flush()
            aggregate = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="paid-correction-source",
                model_execution_mode="real",
                real_execution_confirmed=True,
            )
            aggregate_id = aggregate.id
            production_id = aggregate.items[0].production_run_id
            db.commit()

        import app.workflow_correction as correction
        import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor
        import app.workflow_model_executor as model_executor

        monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "SessionLocal", sessions)
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            model_executor,
            "get_settings",
            lambda: SimpleNamespace(upload_dir=tmp_path, nas_maps_root=None),
        )
        calls: list[tuple[str, str]] = []

        class FakePaidClient:
            def __init__(self, config):
                self.config = config

            async def chat_json(self, system_prompt, user_prompt, **options):
                calls.append((system_prompt, user_prompt))
                if "阶段A" in system_prompt:
                    parsed = {"style": "旧风格"}
                else:
                    corrected = "现代简约" in user_prompt
                    parsed = {
                        "level": "L1" if corrected else "L2",
                        "score": 95 if corrected else 80,
                    }
                return DoubaoResponse(
                    parsed=parsed,
                    raw_text=canonical_json(parsed),
                    raw_payload={"id": f"req-paid-{len(calls)}"},
                    upstream_status_code=200,
                    request_correlation_id=f"req-paid-{len(calls)}",
                    attempt_count=1,
                    output_budget=options["output_budget"],
                    reasoning_effort=options["reasoning_effort"],
                    thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode,
                    input_image_tokens=80,
                    per_image_tokens=(80,),
                    input_tokens=200,
                    output_tokens=20,
                    total_tokens=220,
                )

        monkeypatch.setattr(correction, "DoubaoClient", FakePaidClient)
        processed = 0
        while process_runtime_step_once(
            "paid-correction-worker",
            model_client_type=FakePaidClient,
        ):
            processed += 1
            assert processed <= 8
        assert len(calls) == 2

        with sessions() as db:
            aggregate = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, aggregate_id)
            assert aggregate is not None and aggregate.status == "completed"
            item = aggregate.items[0]
            view = build_correction_view(db, run=aggregate, item=item)
            assert view["read_only"] is False, view["unavailable_reason"]
            node = next(
                candidate
                for candidate in view["nodes"]
                if candidate["node_key"] == "workflow.prompt_a.style"
            )
            assert node["metadata"]["paid_rerun_required"] is True
            response = submit_correction_nodes(
                db,
                run=aggregate,
                item=item,
                contract_hash=view["contract"]["contract_hash"],
                nodes=[
                    {
                        "node_key": node["node_key"],
                        "human_value": "现代简约",
                        "reason": "人工核对后修正风格",
                        "evidence": [{"text": "案例图片风格一致"}],
                    }
                ],
                review_revision=view["review_revision"],
                idempotency_key="paid-correction-submit-1",
                actor="运营甲",
                real_execution_confirmed=True,
            )
            db.commit()

            assert response["review_revision"] == 1
            assert len(calls) == 3
            assert "现代简约" in calls[-1][1]
            evaluation = db.scalar(
                select(EvaluationResult).where(
                    EvaluationResult.production_run_id == production_id
                )
            )
            assert evaluation is not None
            outputs = json.loads(evaluation.workflow_node_outputs_json)
            assert outputs["prompt_b"]["output"] == {
                "level": "L1",
                "score": 95,
            }
            attempts = db.scalars(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt)
                .where(
                    ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == production_id,
                    ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == "prompt_b",
                )
                .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no)
            ).all()
            assert [attempt.status for attempt in attempts] == [
                "succeeded",
                "succeeded",
            ]
            assert attempts[-1].attempt_no == 2
            invocations = db.scalars(
                select(WorkflowModelInvocation).where(
                    WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id == production_id
                )
            ).all()
            assert len(invocations) == 3
            assert all(row.status == "succeeded" for row in invocations)
            paid_evidence = json.loads(invocations[-1].evidence_json)
            assert paid_evidence["reservation"]["paid_correction"] == {
                "real_execution_confirmed": True,
                "correction_key": "contract:"
                + hashlib.sha256(
                    b"paid-correction-submit-1:workflow.prompt_a.style"
                ).hexdigest()[:48],
                "budget_scope": "explicit_confirmation_with_global_hard_caps",
            }
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_paid_correction_closes_provider_call_when_settlement_fails(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    import app.workflow_correction as correction
    from app.doubao import DoubaoResponse
    from test_workflow_model_executor import _case as _real_model_case

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'paid-correction-failure.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    provider_calls = 0
    correction_key = "paid-correction-settlement-failure"
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            production, base_attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _real_model_case(
                db,
                tmp_path,
                monkeypatch,
                strict_output=True,
            )
            production.status = "succeeded"
            base_attempt.status = "succeeded"
            base_attempt.lease_owner = None
            base_attempt.lease_token = None
            base_attempt.lease_expires_at = None
            run_id = production.id
            node_id = node["node_instance_id"]
            db.commit()

        class SuccessClient:
            def __init__(self, config):
                self.config = config

            async def chat_json(self, *_args, **options):
                nonlocal provider_calls
                provider_calls += 1
                return DoubaoResponse(
                    parsed={"score": 88, "label": "provider-returned"},
                    raw_text='{"score":88,"label":"provider-returned"}',
                    raw_payload={"id": "req-paid-settlement-failure"},
                    upstream_status_code=200,
                    request_correlation_id="req-paid-settlement-failure",
                    attempt_count=1,
                    output_budget=options["output_budget"],
                    reasoning_effort=options["reasoning_effort"],
                    thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode,
                    input_tokens=80,
                    output_tokens=20,
                    total_tokens=100,
                )

        def fail_settlement(*_args, **_kwargs):
            raise WorkflowModelBudgetError(
                "model_pricing_settlement_invalid",
                "结算快照损坏",
                status_code=409,
            )

        monkeypatch.setattr(
            correction,
            "settle_model_invocation",
            fail_settlement,
        )

        def rerun() -> None:
            with sessions() as db:
                production = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
                assert production is not None
                snapshot = json.loads(production.snapshot_json)
                frozen_node = next(
                    item
                    for item in snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]
                    if item["node_instance_id"] == node_id
                )
                base_attempt = db.scalar(
                    select(ProductionStepAttempt)
                    .where(
                        ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id,
                        ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == node_id,
                    )
                    .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no)
                )
                assert base_attempt is not None
                with pytest.raises(correction.WorkflowCorrectionError):
                    correction._execute_paid_correction_prompt(
                        db,
                        run=production,
                        snapshot=snapshot,
                        node=frozen_node,
                        input_manifest=json.loads(
                            base_attempt.input_manifest_json
                        ),
                        correction_key=correction_key,
                        model_client_type=SuccessClient,
                    )

        rerun()
        assert provider_calls == 1
        with sessions() as db:
            invocation = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelInvocation))
            assert invocation is not None
            assert invocation.status == "failed"
            assert invocation.error_code == "model_invocation_outcome_unknown", (
                invocation.error_message
            )
            paid_attempt = db.get(
                ProductionStepAttempt,
                invocation.step_attempt_id,
            )
            assert paid_attempt is not None
            assert paid_attempt.status == "failed"
            assert paid_attempt.lease_owner is None
            assert paid_attempt.lease_token is None
            assert paid_attempt.lease_expires_at is None

        rerun()
        assert provider_calls == 1
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_materialized_design_case_real_graph_calls_two_images_then_case_prompt(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """The exact released design-case graph must close with three paid calls."""

    from app.baseline_truth import DESIGN_CASE_TRUTH_CONTRACT
    from app.doubao import DoubaoResponse
    from app.legacy_workflow_release import materialize_legacy_workflows
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.workflow_fixture_executor import process_runtime_step_once
    from tests.test_legacy_workflow_release import _workflow
    from tests.test_strict_prompt_release import _prepare_database

    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        first_image_bytes = _real_jpeg_bytes(width=64, height=48)
        second_image_bytes = _real_jpeg_bytes(width=65, height=48)
        (tmp_path / "released-case-a.jpg").write_bytes(first_image_bytes)
        (tmp_path / "released-case-b.jpg").write_bytes(second_image_bytes)
        with sessions() as db:
            materialize_legacy_workflows(
                db, actor="system:design-case-real-e2e", apply=True
            )
            workflow = _workflow(db, "design_case.legacy_doubao_review")
            workflow.status = "active"
            for model in db.scalars(select(ModelConfig)).all():
                model.encrypted_api_key = "encrypted:test-only-reference"
                model.max_input_tokens = 128_000
                model.input_micros_per_million_tokens = 1_000_000
                model.output_micros_per_million_tokens = 2_000_000
                model.benchmark_enabled = True
            first = Asset(
                original_name="released-case-a.jpg",
                stored_name="released-case-a.jpg",
                mime_type="image/jpeg",
                size_bytes=len(first_image_bytes),
                sha256=hashlib.sha256(first_image_bytes).hexdigest(),
                category_key="design_case",
            )
            second = Asset(
                original_name="released-case-b.jpg",
                stored_name="released-case-b.jpg",
                mime_type="image/jpeg",
                size_bytes=len(second_image_bytes),
                sha256=hashlib.sha256(second_image_bytes).hexdigest(),
                category_key="design_case",
            )
            baseline = BaselineSet(
                category_key="design_case",
                name="案例真实双图发布图回归",
                description="",
                default_expected_level="L1",
                fingerprint="e" * 64,
                created_by="ops",
            )
            db.add_all([first, second, baseline])
            db.flush()
            workflow_input = {
                "case": {
                    "cloud_id": "case-real-42",
                    "project_id": "project-real-42",
                    "case_name": "真实双图案例",
                    "case_text": "验证逐图和案例综合提示词",
                    "quality_score": 10,
                    "time_score": 5,
                    "filter_threshold": 60,
                    "images": [
                        {
                            "cloud_id": "real-image-a",
                            "oss_url": "https://example.invalid/real-a.jpg",
                            "asset_id": first.id,
                            "asset_sha256": first.sha256,
                        },
                        {
                            "cloud_id": "real-image-b",
                            "oss_url": "https://example.invalid/real-b.jpg",
                            "asset_id": second.id,
                            "asset_sha256": second.sha256,
                        },
                    ],
                }
            }
            db.add(
                BaselineSetItem(
                    baseline_set_id=baseline.id,
                    asset_id=first.id,
                    expected_level=None,
                    expected_result_json=canonical_json(
                        {"case_cloud_id": "case-real-42", "level": "L1"}
                    ),
                    truth_contract_key=DESIGN_CASE_TRUTH_CONTRACT,
                    asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
                        {
                            "asset_id": first.id,
                            "sha256": first.sha256,
                            "workflow_input": workflow_input,
                        }
                    ),
                )
            )
            db.flush()
            aggregate = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="released-design-case-real",
                model_execution_mode="real",
                real_execution_confirmed=True,
            )
            aggregate_id = aggregate.id
            production_id = aggregate.items[0].production_run_id
            db.commit()

        import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor
        import app.workflow_model_executor as model_executor

        monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "SessionLocal", sessions)
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            model_executor,
            "get_settings",
            lambda: SimpleNamespace(upload_dir=tmp_path, nas_maps_root=None),
        )
        calls: list[str] = []
        image_output = {
            "title": "案例图",
            "seo_title": "案例图片",
            "category": "建筑设计",
            "sub_category": "住宅",
            "style": "现代",
            "tags": ["空间", "建筑", "材质", "光影"],
            "cons": "无",
            "design": "空间清晰",
            "score": 82,
            "reason": "",
            "image_defects": "",
            "trait": "实景照片",
        }
        case_output = {
            "score": 70,
            "reason": "内容完整",
            "seo_title": "现代住宅案例",
            "category_one": "建筑设计",
            "category_two": "住宅",
            "style": "现代",
            "matcraft": ["石材"],
            "designtactic": ["借景"],
            "spaceshape": ["开放"],
            "clientInfo": "无",
            "projattr": "住宅",
            "designer": "无",
            "region_type": "国内",
            "location_detail": "无",
            "scale": "无",
            "dtime": "无",
            "btime": "无",
        }

        class FakeDesignCaseClient:
            def __init__(self, config):
                self.config = config

            def _response(self, parsed, options, *, image_count: int):
                calls.append("image" if image_count else "text")
                input_image_tokens = 80 * image_count
                return DoubaoResponse(
                    parsed=parsed,
                    raw_text=canonical_json(parsed),
                    raw_payload={"id": f"req-case-{len(calls)}"},
                    upstream_status_code=200,
                    request_correlation_id=f"req-case-{len(calls)}",
                    attempt_count=1,
                    output_budget=options["output_budget"],
                    reasoning_effort=options["reasoning_effort"],
                    thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode,
                    input_image_tokens=input_image_tokens,
                    per_image_tokens=tuple(80 for _ in range(image_count)),
                    input_tokens=200 + input_image_tokens,
                    output_tokens=60,
                    total_tokens=260 + input_image_tokens,
                )

            async def chat_json_images(
                self, system_prompt, image_samples, **options
            ):
                return self._response(
                    image_output,
                    options,
                    image_count=len(image_samples),
                )

            async def chat_json(self, system_prompt, user_prompt, **options):
                return self._response(case_output, options, image_count=0)

        processed = 0
        while process_runtime_step_once(
            "released-design-case-real-worker",
            model_client_type=FakeDesignCaseClient,
        ):
            processed += 1
            assert processed <= 10

        with sessions() as db:
            aggregate = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, aggregate_id)
            assert aggregate is not None and aggregate.status == "completed"
            assert calls == ["image", "image", "text"]
            children = db.scalars(
                select(WorkflowForeachItem)
                .where(WorkflowForeachItem.production_run_id == production_id)
                .order_by(WorkflowForeachItem.ordinal)
            ).all()
            assert [(row.item_key, row.status) for row in children] == [
                ("real-image-a", "succeeded"),
                ("real-image-b", "succeeded"),
            ]
            invocations = db.scalars(
                select(WorkflowModelInvocation)
                .where(WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id == production_id)
                .order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.id)
            ).all()
            assert len(invocations) == 3
            assert all(row.status == "succeeded" for row in invocations)
            assert all(
                json.loads(row.evidence_json)["result"]["thinking_mode"]
                == "disabled"
                and json.loads(row.evidence_json)["result"][
                    "reasoning_effort"
                ]
                is None
                for row in invocations
            )
            normalized = json.loads(aggregate.items[0].result_snapshot_json)[
                "normalized_result"
            ]
            assert normalized["case_cloud_id"] == "case-real-42"
            assert normalized["score"] == 85
            assert normalized["level"] == "L1"
            assert normalized["side_effect_executed"] is False
            assert normalized["deletion_mode"] == "recommend_only"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_real_knowledge_graph_worker_calls_only_the_four_selected_routes(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """Catches a four-route DAG charging unselected prompt branches."""

    from app.doubao import DoubaoResponse
    from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.native_workflow_migration import ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow
    from app.seed import seed_defaults
    from app.workflow_fixture_executor import process_runtime_step_once

    routes = (
        ("domestic", 0, "ll_id", "ll-real-whole"),
        ("domestic", 1, "ll_id", "ll-real-single"),
        ("overseas", 0, "res_id", "res-real-whole"),
        ("overseas", 1, "res_id", "res-real-single"),
    )
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-kg-worker.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            seed_defaults(db)
            source_model = db.scalar(
                select(ModelConfig)
                .where(ModelConfig.active.is_(True))
                .order_by(ModelConfig.id)
            )
            assert source_model is not None
            source_model.encrypted_api_key = "encrypted:test-only-reference"
            source_model.max_input_tokens = 12_000
            source_model.input_micros_per_million_tokens = 1_000_000
            source_model.output_micros_per_million_tokens = 2_000_000
            source_model.benchmark_enabled = True
            workflow = ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow(
                db,
                actor="system:real-kg-worker",
                category_key="knowledge_graph",
            )
            baseline = BaselineSet(
                category_key="knowledge_graph",
                name="真实知识图谱四路回归",
                description="",
                default_expected_level="L1",
                fingerprint="9" * 64,
                created_by="ops",
            )
            db.add(baseline)
            db.flush()
            for index, (source, is_single, identity_field, identity) in enumerate(
                routes, start=1
            ):
                asset_scope = "single" if is_single else "whole"
                fixture_key = f"kg.{source}.{asset_scope}.v1"
                contract_key = (
                    f"kg.knowledge_graph.{source}.{asset_scope}.v1"
                )
                expected = resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture(
                    fixture_key,
                    contract_key,
                    expected_asset_id=identity,
                )
                image_bytes = _real_jpeg_bytes(64 + index, 48)
                stored_name = f"real-kg-{index}.jpg"
                (tmp_path / stored_name).write_bytes(image_bytes)
                asset = Asset(
                    original_name=stored_name,
                    stored_name=stored_name,
                    mime_type="image/jpeg",
                    size_bytes=len(image_bytes),
                    sha256=hashlib.sha256(image_bytes).hexdigest(),
                    category_key="knowledge_graph",
                )
                db.add(asset)
                db.flush()
                db.add(
                    BaselineSetItem(
                        baseline_set_id=baseline.id,
                        asset_id=asset.id,
                        expected_level=None,
                        expected_result_json=canonical_json(expected),
                        truth_contract_key=contract_key,
                        asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
                            {
                                "asset_id": asset.id,
                                "sha256": asset.sha256,
                                "workflow_input": {
                                    "source": source,
                                    "is_single": is_single,
                                    identity_field: identity,
                                    "fixture_key": fixture_key,
                                },
                            }
                        ),
                    )
                )
            db.flush()
            aggregate = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="real-kg-worker",
                model_execution_mode="real",
                real_execution_confirmed=True,
            )
            aggregate_id = aggregate.id
            production_ids = [item.production_run_id for item in aggregate.items]
            db.commit()

        import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor
        import app.workflow_model_executor as model_executor

        monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "SessionLocal", sessions)
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            model_executor,
            "get_settings",
            lambda: SimpleNamespace(upload_dir=tmp_path, nas_maps_root=None),
        )
        calls: list[str] = []

        class FakeKnowledgeGraphClient:
            def __init__(self, config):
                self.config = config

            async def chat_json(self, system_prompt, user_prompt, **options):
                prompt_text = system_prompt + "\n" + user_prompt
                matched = next(
                    route for route in routes if route[3] in prompt_text
                )
                source, is_single, _identity_field, identity = matched
                asset_scope = "single" if is_single else "whole"
                expected = resolve_knowledge_graph_fixture(
                    f"kg.{source}.{asset_scope}.v1",
                    f"kg.knowledge_graph.{source}.{asset_scope}.v1",
                    expected_asset_id=identity,
                )
                calls.append(identity)
                return DoubaoResponse(
                    parsed=expected,
                    raw_text=canonical_json(expected),
                    raw_payload={"id": f"req-{identity}"},
                    upstream_status_code=200,
                    request_correlation_id=f"req-{identity}",
                    attempt_count=1,
                    output_budget=options["output_budget"],
                    reasoning_effort=options["reasoning_effort"],
                    thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode,
                    input_tokens=250,
                    output_tokens=120,
                    total_tokens=370,
                )

        processed = 0
        while process_runtime_step_once(
            "real-kg-worker",
            global_limit=1,
            model_client_type=FakeKnowledgeGraphClient,
        ):
            processed += 1
            assert processed <= 24

        assert sorted(calls) == sorted(route[3] for route in routes)
        assert len(calls) == 4
        with sessions() as db:
            aggregate = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, aggregate_id)
            assert aggregate is not None and aggregate.status == "completed"
            assert aggregate.valid_predictions == 4
            invocations = db.scalars(
                select(WorkflowModelInvocation).order_by(
                    WorkflowModelInvocation.id
                )
            ).all()
            assert len(invocations) == 4
            assert all(item.status == "succeeded" for item in invocations)
            prompt_attempts = db.scalars(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                    ProductionStepAttempt.run_id.in_(production_ids),
                    ProductionStepAttempt.step_type == "model_call",
                )
            ).all()
            assert sum(item.status == "succeeded" for item in prompt_attempts) == 4
            assert sum(item.status == "skipped" for item in prompt_attempts) == 12
    finally:
        engine.dispose()
