def test_baseline_api_passes_real_execution_identity_to_native_runs() -> None:
    """Catches the endpoint accepting real mode but creating simulated workflow runs."""

    from app.main import app, current_user

    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        user = User(
            username="ops",
            password_hash="unused",
            display_name="运营",
            is_admin=True,
        )
        db.add(user)
        baseline = _baseline(db)
        workflow = _mechanism(db)
        _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)
        db.add(
            PromptVersion(
                id=31,
                category_key="space_image",
                pipeline_scope="shared",
                stage="C",
                name="知识图谱字段提取",
                version="component-v2",
                system_prompt="提取知识图谱字段",
                user_prompt="返回结构化结果",
                rubric_version="rubric-v1",
                status="published",
                source="workflow_component",
                created_by="ops",
            )
        )
        add_active_v3_contract(db)
        db.commit()

        app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: (yield db)
        app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
        client = TestClient(app)
        response = client.post(
            f"/api/baseline-sets/{baseline.id}/runs",
            json={
                # 这条夹具没有批量接入点；开关默认关闭＝批量会被受控拒绝，
                # 本用例验的是真实执行身份，因此显式打开「使用在线推理」。
                "inference_mode": "online",
                "workflow_version_id": workflow.id,
                "model_execution_mode": "real",
                "real_execution_confirmed": True,
            },
        )

        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        selection = response.json()["selection"]
        assert selection["model_execution_mode"] == "real"
        assert selection["real_execution_budget"]["expected_requests"] == 1
        production_runs = db.scalars(select(ProductionRun)).all()
        assert production_runs
        assert all(row.model_execution_mode == "real" for row in production_runs)
        assert all(row.environment == "dry_run" for row in production_runs)
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def _real_correction_case(
    db: Session,
    *,
    real_execution_confirmed: bool,
) -> tuple[BaselineRegressionRun, BaselineCorrectionRun, WorkflowVersion]:
    """Freeze a paid source run and one candidate-regression request."""

    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run

    baseline = _baseline(db)
    workflow = _mechanism(db)
    _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)
    source = create_native_baseline_run(
        db,
        baseline_set=baseline,
        workflow_version=workflow,
        actor="ops",
        idempotency_key="real-correction-source",
        model_execution_mode="real",
        real_execution_confirmed=True,
    )
    source.status = "completed"
    source.completed = source.total
    source.valid_predictions = source.total
    candidate_manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow)
    candidate_prompt = next(
        node
        for node in candidate_manifest["nodes"]
        if node["kind"] == "prompt"
    )
    candidate_prompt["config"]["prompt_version_id"] = 32
    candidate_component = candidate_prompt["config"]["component_snapshot"]
    candidate_component["version_id"] = 32
    candidate_component["version"] = "component-v3"
    candidate_content = dict(candidate_component["content"])
    candidate_content["system_prompt"] += "；采用人工纠偏后的候选边界"
    candidate_component["content"] = candidate_content
    candidate_component["content_hash"] = hashlib.sha256(
        canonical_json(candidate_content).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()
    candidate_workflow = WorkflowVersion(
        workflow_definition_id=workflow.workflow_definition_id,
        version="candidate-real-1",
        status="active",
        workflow_schema_version="workflow-v3",
        step_manifest_json=canonical_json(candidate_manifest["nodes"]),
        edge_manifest_json=canonical_json(candidate_manifest["edges"]),
        input_schema_json=canonical_json(candidate_manifest["input_schema"]),
        output_schema_json=canonical_json(candidate_manifest["output_schema"]),
        resource_policy_json=canonical_json(
            {
                **candidate_manifest["resource_policy"],
                "queue_class": candidate_manifest["queue_class"],
                "category_key": candidate_manifest["category_key"],
                "lane_key": candidate_manifest["lane_key"],
                "run_modes": candidate_manifest["run_modes"],
            }
        ),
        canonical_hash=hashlib.sha256(
            canonical_json(candidate_manifest).encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest(),
        validation_report_json=canonical_json({"ok": True, "errors": []}),
        created_by="ops",
    )
    db.add(candidate_workflow)
    db.flush()
    correction = BaselineCorrectionRun(
        idempotency_key="real-correction-candidate",
        baseline_run_id=source.id,
        category_key=source.category_key,
        selected_item_ids_json=canonical_json([source.items[0].id]),
        input_snapshot_json=canonical_json(
            {
                "candidate_execution": {
                    "schema_version": (
                        "baseline-correction-candidate-execution-v1"
                    ),
                    "model_execution_mode": "real",
                    "real_execution_confirmed": real_execution_confirmed,
                }
            }
        ),
        candidate_workflow_version_id=candidate_workflow.id,
        created_by="ops",
    )
    db.add(correction)
    db.flush()
    return source, correction, candidate_workflow


def test_real_correction_candidate_inherits_source_mode_and_recalculates_budget(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    """Catches a paid correction silently falling back to fixture execution."""

    from app.workflow_correction import start_single_candidate_regression

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-correction-mode.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            source, correction, candidate_workflow = _real_correction_case(
                db,
                real_execution_confirmed=True,
            )

            candidate = start_single_candidate_regression(
                db,
                correction,
                "ops",
            )

            source_execution = json.loads(source.execution_snapshot_json)
            candidate_execution = json.loads(candidate.execution_snapshot_json)
            assert candidate_execution["model_execution_mode"] == "real"
            assert candidate.workflow_version_id == candidate_workflow.id
            candidate_prompt = next(
                node
                for node in candidate_execution["workflow_snapshot"][
                    "workflow"
                ]["manifest"]["nodes"]
                if node["kind"] == "prompt"
            )
            assert candidate_prompt["config"]["prompt_version_id"] == 32
            assert candidate_execution["real_execution_budget"] != (
                source_execution["real_execution_budget"]
            )
            assert candidate_execution["real_execution_budget"][
                "expected_input_tokens"
            ] > source_execution["real_execution_budget"][
                "expected_input_tokens"
            ]
            assert candidate_execution["real_execution_budget"][
                "expected_cost_micros"
            ] > source_execution["real_execution_budget"][
                "expected_cost_micros"
            ]
            production_runs = [
                db.get(ProductionRun, item.production_run_id)
                for item in candidate.items
            ]
            assert all(row is not None for row in production_runs)
            assert all(
                row.model_execution_mode == "real"
                for row in production_runs
                if row is not None
            )
            assert all(
                json.loads(row.snapshot_json)["runtime_context"][
                    "real_execution_confirmed"
                ]
                is True
                for row in production_runs
                if row is not None
            )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_real_correction_candidate_rejects_missing_fresh_confirmation(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    """Catches reusing the source run acknowledgement for a new paid rerun."""

    from app.workflow_correction import (
        WorkflowCorrectionError,
        start_single_candidate_regression,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-correction-confirm.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            _, correction, _ = _real_correction_case(
                db,
                real_execution_confirmed=False,
            )

            with pytest.raises(WorkflowCorrectionError) as exc_info:
                start_single_candidate_regression(db, correction, "ops")

            assert exc_info.value.code == (
                "workflow_candidate_real_confirmation_required"
            )
            assert correction.regression_run_id is None
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_real_correction_api_requires_confirmation_for_candidate_rerun(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    """Catches the HTTP correction entry point starting a second paid run silently."""

    from app.main import app, current_user
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-correction-api.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)
            source = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="real-correction-api-source",
                model_execution_mode="real",
                real_execution_confirmed=True,
            )
            source.status = "completed"
            source.completed = source.total
            source.valid_predictions = source.total
            source_id = source.id
            item_id = source.items[0].id
            db.commit()

        def override_db():
            with sessions() as db:
                yield db

        app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = override_db
        app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: SimpleNamespace(
            username="ops", role="admin", active=True, is_admin=True
        )

        response = TestClient(app).post(
            f"/api/baseline-regressions/{source_id}/corrections",
            json={
                "item_ids": [item_id],
                "idempotency_key": "real-correction-api-request",
                "real_execution_confirmed": False,
            },
        )

        assert response.status_code == 422, response.text
        assert response.json()["detail"]["code"] == (
            "workflow_candidate_real_confirmation_required"
        )
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        engine.dispose()


def test_real_budget_admits_a_full_size_baseline_regression() -> None:
    """守住「运行全量回归」按钮：运营最大一轮是 3000 条素材（A+B 共 6000 次调用）。
    开发期的 12 次 / 10 元占位上限曾把 250 条的正常一轮整个挡在门外，运营点不动按钮。"""

    from app.workflow_model_budget import (
        MAX_REAL_COST_MICROS,
        MAX_REAL_INPUT_TOKENS,
        MAX_REAL_OUTPUT_TOKENS,
        MAX_REAL_REQUESTS,
        real_execution_budget_for_selection,
    )

    # 实测量级：单次调用输入约 1.5 万 token、输出约 1400 token、成本约 0.034 元。
    model = _priced_model(
        max_input_tokens=16_000,
        max_tokens=2_000,
        input_micros_per_million_tokens=1_200_000,
        output_micros_per_million_tokens=1_200_000,
    )
    manifest = {
        "nodes": [_prompt("prompt_a"), _prompt("prompt_b")],
        "edges": [{"from": "prompt_a", "to": "prompt_b"}],
    }

    # 不抛 WorkflowModelBudgetError 本身就是断言：超限时这里会直接报错。
    budget = real_execution_budget_for_selection(
        manifest, item_count=3_000, model_configs={1: model}
    )

    assert budget.expected_requests == 6_000
    assert budget.expected_requests <= MAX_REAL_REQUESTS
    assert budget.expected_input_tokens <= MAX_REAL_INPUT_TOKENS
    assert budget.expected_output_tokens <= MAX_REAL_OUTPUT_TOKENS
    assert budget.max_cost_micros <= MAX_REAL_COST_MICROS


def test_real_budget_ceiling_is_env_overridable() -> None:
    """上限要能不改代码临时收紧或放开；非法值必须回落默认值而不是变成 0。"""

    from app.workflow_model_budget import _env_budget

    assert _env_budget("__LABELLAB_ABSENT__", 5_000) == 5_000
    try:
        os.environ["__LABELLAB_BUDGET_PROBE__"] = "77"
        assert _env_budget("__LABELLAB_BUDGET_PROBE__", 5_000) == 77
        for bad in ("", "  ", "abc", "0", "-3"):
            os.environ["__LABELLAB_BUDGET_PROBE__"] = bad
            assert _env_budget("__LABELLAB_BUDGET_PROBE__", 5_000) == 5_000
    finally:
        os.environ.pop("__LABELLAB_BUDGET_PROBE__", None)


def test_incremental_production_path_does_not_consume_real_regression_budget() -> None:
    """增量评测每天 10 万条以上，绝不能被存量回归的安全上限挡住。

    增量走 evaluation_jobs 的 production_batch 队列（worker 的老执行链路），
    与本模块无关。这里钉住这条边界：worker 不得直接引用真实评测预算模块，
    否则增量量级会瞬间撞上为存量回归设定的上限。
    """

    import pathlib

    worker_source = (
        pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "app" / "worker.py"
    ).read_text(encoding="utf-8")

    assert "workflow_model_budget" not in worker_source
    assert "reserve_model_invocation" not in worker_source


def test_budget_preview_reports_cost_and_does_not_block_when_over_cap(monkeypatch) -> None:
    """预估要能报出「这轮花多少钱」，且超限时照样给数字而不是抛错。

    运营需要在勾选真实模型之前看到费用；超限时只给一句「超过上限」而不告诉
    超了多少、超在哪一项，等于让人对着黑箱试。
    """

    from app.workflow_model_budget import (
        MAX_REAL_REQUESTS,
        WorkflowModelBudgetError,
        real_execution_budget_for_selection,
    )

    model = _priced_model(
        max_input_tokens=16_000,
        max_tokens=2_000,
        input_micros_per_million_tokens=1_200_000,
        output_micros_per_million_tokens=1_200_000,
    )
    manifest = {
        "nodes": [_prompt("prompt_a"), _prompt("prompt_b")],
        "edges": [{"from": "prompt_a", "to": "prompt_b"}],
    }

    budget = real_execution_budget_for_selection(
        manifest, item_count=3_000, model_configs={1: model}
    )
    assert budget.expected_cost_micros > 0

    # 远超上限的一轮：enforce=False 必须照常返回数字供界面展示。
    huge_items = MAX_REAL_REQUESTS * 10
    preview = real_execution_budget_for_selection(
        manifest, item_count=huge_items, model_configs={1: model}, enforce=False
    )
    assert preview.expected_requests > MAX_REAL_REQUESTS
    assert preview.expected_cost_micros > 0

    # 2026-09-16 老大裁决后，全局天花板默认关闭：同一轮 enforce=True 也不再被拦。
    # 挡住一轮建不起来的从来不是误操作，运营真实的最大一轮本来就会撞上去。
    assert real_execution_budget_for_selection(
        manifest, item_count=huge_items, model_configs={1: model}
    ).expected_requests > MAX_REAL_REQUESTS

    # 需要时仍可整体打开，届时行为和以前完全一样。
    import app.workflow_model_budget as budget_module
    monkeypatch.setattr(budget_module, "REAL_EXECUTION_CEILINGS_ENFORCED", True)
    with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError) as exc_info:
        real_execution_budget_for_selection(
            manifest, item_count=huge_items, model_configs={1: model}
        )
    assert exc_info.value.code == "model_budget_requests_exhausted"

def test_real_execution_preview_api_uses_frozen_inputs_for_tier_evidence(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.main import app, current_user

    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'tier-preview-api.db'}",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        baseline = _baseline(db)
        workflow = _mechanism(db)
        model = _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)
        model.max_input_tokens = 32_001
        model.max_tokens = 8_192
        model.pricing_contract_json = canonical_json(
            doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing()
        )
        user = User(
            username="tier-preview-viewer",
            password_hash="unused",
            display_name="分档预览测试员",
            role="admin",
            is_admin=True,
        )
        db.add(user)
        db.commit()
        app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: (yield db)
        app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
        client = TestClient(app)
        try:
            response = client.get(
                f"/api/baseline-sets/{baseline.id}/real-execution-preview",
                # 同上：本用例验的是分档计价证据，走在线价目。
                params={"workflow_version_id": workflow.id, "inference_mode": "online"},
            )
            assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
            body = response.json()
            assert body["expected"]["cost_micros"] > 0
            assert len(body["node_pricing"]) == 1
            node = body["node_pricing"][0]
            assert node["pricing_version"] == "model-pricing-v3"
            assert node["expected_cost_basis"] == "cold_cost_upper_bound"
            assert node["pricing_tier_key"] == "32000-128000"
            assert node["expected_pricing_tier_key"] == "0-32000"
            assert node["expected_request_count"] == 1
            assert node["expected_tier_buckets"][0][
                "pricing_tier_key"
            ] == "0-32000"
            assert node["pricing_tiers"][0] == {
                "tier_key": "0-32000",
                "min_input_tokens": 0,
                "max_input_tokens": 32_000,
                "input_micros_per_1k": 600,
                "cached_input_micros_per_1k": 120,
                "output_micros_per_1k": 3_600,
            }
        finally:
            app.dependency_overrides.clear()
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_cache_observation_summary_counts_statuses_and_requires_exact_rows() -> None:
    from app.main import _cache_observation_summary
    from app.model_pricing import price_usage

    pricing = doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing()

    def reported_settlement(
        *, input_tokens: int, output_tokens: int, cached_input_tokens: int
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        quote = price_usage(
            pricing,
            input_tokens=input_tokens,
            output_tokens=output_tokens,
            cached_input_tokens=cached_input_tokens,
        )
        return {
            "schema_version": "workflow-model-pricing-settlement-v2",
            "settlement_basis": "provider_cache_reported",
            "cache_observation_status": (
                "hit" if cached_input_tokens else "miss"
            ),
            "pricing_version": pricing["schema_version"],
            "pricing_tier_key": quote.tier_key,
            "pricing_contract_hash": quote.pricing_contract_hash,
            "input_tokens": quote.input_tokens,
            "reported_cached_input_tokens": cached_input_tokens,
            "cached_input_tokens": quote.cached_input_tokens,
            "uncached_input_tokens": quote.uncached_input_tokens,
            "output_tokens": quote.output_tokens,
            "uncached_input_cost_micros": quote.uncached_input_cost_micros,
            "cached_input_cost_micros": quote.cached_input_cost_micros,
            "input_cost_micros": quote.input_cost_micros,
            "output_cost_micros": quote.output_cost_micros,
            "full_price_cost_micros": quote.full_price_cost_micros,
            "cache_savings_micros": quote.cache_savings_micros,
            "actual_cost_micros": quote.total_cost_micros,
        }

    hit = reported_settlement(
        input_tokens=8_000,
        output_tokens=300,
        cached_input_tokens=7_000,
    )
    miss = reported_settlement(
        input_tokens=1_000,
        output_tokens=100,
        cached_input_tokens=0,
    )
    unreported = {
        "schema_version": "workflow-model-pricing-settlement-v2",
        "settlement_basis": "conservative_full_input",
        "cache_observation_status": "unreported",
        "input_tokens": 500,
        "reported_cached_input_tokens": None,
        "cache_savings_micros": 0,
    }
    invalid = {
        "schema_version": "workflow-model-pricing-settlement-v2",
        "settlement_basis": "conservative_full_input",
        "cache_observation_status": "invalid",
        "input_tokens": 500,
        "reported_cached_input_tokens": None,
        "cache_savings_micros": 0,
    }

    mixed = _cache_observation_summary(
        [hit, miss, unreported, invalid, {"schema_version": "legacy"}],
        actual_cost_known=True,
    )
    assert mixed == {
        "schema_version": "baseline-cache-observation-v1",
        "cached_input_tokens": 7_000,
        "observed_input_tokens": 9_000,
        "cached_token_ratio": 7_000 / 9_000,
        "hit_count": 1,
        "miss_count": 1,
        "unreported_count": 1,
        "invalid_count": 1,
        "legacy_count": 1,
        "cache_savings_micros": None,
        "cache_adjusted_cost_exact": False,
    }

    exact = _cache_observation_summary(
        [hit, miss],
        actual_cost_known=True,
        actual_costs=[2_520, 960],
        pricing_contexts=[
            (pricing, 8_000, 300),
            (pricing, 1_000, 100),
        ],
    )
    assert exact["cache_savings_micros"] == 3_360
    assert exact["cache_adjusted_cost_exact"] is True

    self_consistent_but_mispriced = dict(hit)
    self_consistent_but_mispriced["full_price_cost_micros"] = 1_000_000
    self_consistent_but_mispriced["cache_savings_micros"] = 997_480
    mispriced = _cache_observation_summary(
        [self_consistent_but_mispriced],
        actual_cost_known=True,
        actual_costs=[2_520],
        pricing_contexts=[(pricing, 8_000, 300)],
    )
    assert mispriced["cache_savings_micros"] is None
    assert mispriced["cache_adjusted_cost_exact"] is False

    rounded = reported_settlement(
        input_tokens=3,
        output_tokens=0,
        cached_input_tokens=1,
    )
    assert rounded["actual_cost_micros"] > rounded["full_price_cost_micros"]
    assert rounded["cache_savings_micros"] == 0
    rounded_summary = _cache_observation_summary(
        [rounded],
        actual_cost_known=True,
        actual_costs=[rounded["actual_cost_micros"]],
        pricing_contexts=[(pricing, 3, 0)],
    )
    assert rounded_summary["cache_savings_micros"] == 0
    assert rounded_summary["cache_adjusted_cost_exact"] is True

    corrupt = dict(hit)
    corrupt["actual_cost_micros"] = 2_521
    unverifiable = _cache_observation_summary(
        [corrupt],
        actual_cost_known=True,
        actual_costs=[2_520],
        pricing_contexts=[(pricing, 8_000, 300)],
    )
    assert unverifiable["cache_savings_micros"] is None
    assert unverifiable["cache_adjusted_cost_exact"] is False

    incomplete = _cache_observation_summary(
        [
            {
                "schema_version": "workflow-model-pricing-settlement-v2",
                "settlement_basis": "provider_cache_reported",
                "cache_observation_status": "hit",
                "input_tokens": 8_000,
                "reported_cached_input_tokens": 7_000,
                "cache_savings_micros": 0,
            }
        ],
        actual_cost_known=True,
        actual_costs=[2_520],
        pricing_contexts=[(pricing, 8_000, 300)],
    )
    assert incomplete["cache_savings_micros"] is None
    assert incomplete["cache_adjusted_cost_exact"] is False


def test_cancel_reaches_native_workflow_runs(tmp_path) -> None:
    """「取消本轮」必须停得住新工作流路径的回归。

    native workflow 的回归条目没有 evaluation_job（job_id 全为 NULL），旧取消
    接口只扫 job 表——运营点取消，接口返回成功、实际影响 0 条，模型继续调、
    钱继续花（2026-08-28 run135，最后只能手工改库止血）。
    """

    from app.models import BaselineRegressionItem, RuntimeDispatchItem
    from app.native_baseline_regression import (
        cancel_native_baseline_runs,
        create_native_baseline_run,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'native-cancel.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db)
            workflow = _mechanism(db)
            _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)
            aggregate = create_native_baseline_run(
                db,
                baseline_set=baseline,
                workflow_version=workflow,
                actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="native-cancel",
                model_execution_mode="real",
                real_execution_confirmed=True,
            )
            run_id = aggregate.id
            production_id = aggregate.items[0].production_run_id
            assert production_id is not None
            attempt = db.scalar(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                    ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == production_id
                )
            )
            assert attempt is not None
            attempt.status = "running"
            invocation = WorkflowModelInvocation(
                baseline_run_id=run_id,
                production_run_id=production_id,
                step_attempt_id=attempt.id,
                node_instance_id=attempt.step_key,
                status="reserved",
                provider="doubao",
                model_id="cancel-orphan-model",
                reserved_input_tokens=100,
                reserved_output_tokens=50,
                estimated_cost_micros=20,
                evidence_json=(
                    '{"schema_version":"workflow-model-invocation-v1"}'
                ),
            )
            db.add(invocation)
            db.commit()

        with Session(engine) as db:
            affected = cancel_native_baseline_runs(
                db, baseline_run_ids=[run_id]
            )
            db.commit()
            assert affected > 0, "没有任何条目被取消——run135 的缺陷复现了"

        with Session(engine) as db:
            # 调度队列必须清空：worker 不能再认领任何步骤。
            assert (
                db.scalar(
                    select(func.count(RuntimeDispatchItem.id)).where(
                        RuntimeDispatchItem.status.in_(("queued", "leased"))
                    )
                )
                == 0
            )
            run = db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, run_id)
            assert run is not None and run.status == "failed"
            items = db.scalars(
                select(BaselineRegressionItem).where(
                    BaselineRegressionItem.run_id == run_id
                )
            ).all()
            assert items
            assert all(item.status == "failed" for item in items)
            assert all(
                item.error_message == "technical:operator_canceled"
                for item in items
            )
            invocation = db.scalar(
                select(WorkflowModelInvocation).where(
                    WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id == production_id
                )
            )
            assert invocation is not None
            assert invocation.status == "failed"
            assert invocation.error_code == "model_invocation_outcome_unknown"
            assert invocation.finished_at is not None
            assert (
                db.scalar(
                    select(func.count(ProductionRun.id)).where(
                        ProductionRun.status.in_(
                            ("planned", "queued", "running")
                        )
                    )
                )
                == 0
            )
            # 不得残留 leased/running 步骤：它们计入并发占用，worker 换代后
            # 没人收尾，会把后续 run 饿死在队列里（run135 → run136 事故）。
            assert (
                db.scalar(
                    select(func.count(ProductionStepAttempt.id)).where(
                        ProductionStepAttempt.status.in_(("leased", "running"))
                    )
                )
                == 0
            )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_mysql_shared_run_budget_serializes_sibling_reservations(mysql_engine, monkeypatch) -> None:
    """Different material runs must not each spend the last batch-wide slot."""
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    import app.workflow_model_budget as budget

    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection)
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        baseline = _baseline(db)
        _add_level_truth_item(db, baseline, suffix="shared-cap-2")
        workflow = _mechanism(db)
        _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)
        run = create_native_baseline_run(
            db, baseline_set=baseline, workflow_version=workflow, actor="ops",
            idempotency_key="shared-cap", model_execution_mode="real",
            real_execution_confirmed=True,
        )
        targets = []
        for item in run.items:
            production = db.get(ProductionRun, item.production_run_id)
            snapshot = json.loads(production.snapshot_json)
            node = next(n for n in snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]
                        if n["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_c")
            attempt_id = db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt.id).where(
                ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == production.id,
                ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == "prompt_c",
            ))
            targets.append(dict(attempt_id=attempt_id, node=node, run_snapshot=snapshot))
        db.commit()
    reserved = Event()
    second_started = Event()
    release = Event()

    # 2026-09-16 起全局天花板默认关闭（650f73e7）；本用例验证的是共享账本锁下的序列化，
    # 所以显式开启天花板，让第二笔预留仍然必须撞上它。
    monkeypatch.setattr(budget, "REAL_EXECUTION_CEILINGS_ENFORCED", True)

    def first_worker():
        with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
            row = budget.reserve_model_invocation(db, **targets[0])
            monkeypatch.setattr(budget, "MAX_REAL_COST_MICROS", row.estimated_cost_micros)
            reserved.set()
            assert release.wait(10)
            db.commit()

    def second_worker():
        assert reserved.wait(10)
        with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
            # Begin this transaction before the sibling commits.
            db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id)))
            second_started.set()
            try:
                budget.reserve_model_invocation(db, **targets[1])
                db.commit()
                return "overspent"
            except budget.WorkflowModelBudgetError as error:
                db.rollback()
                return error.code

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
        first = pool.submit(first_worker)
        second = pool.submit(second_worker)
        try:
            assert second_started.wait(10)
            time.sleep(0.25)
        finally:
            release.set()
        first.result(timeout=10)
        assert second.result(timeout=10) == "model_budget_cost_exhausted"
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id))) == 1


def test_mysql_cancel_serializes_with_a_concurrent_model_reservation(
    mysql_engine,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """Cancel must close or reject a reservation created at its scan boundary."""

    import app.native_baseline_regression as native_regression
    from app.workflow_model_budget import (
        WorkflowModelBudgetError,
        reserve_model_invocation,
    )

    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection)

    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        baseline = _baseline(db)
        workflow = _mechanism(db)
        _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)
        aggregate = native_regression.create_native_baseline_run(
            db,
            baseline_set=baseline,
            workflow_version=workflow,
            actor="ops",
            idempotency_key="mysql-cancel-reservation-race",
            model_execution_mode="real",
            real_execution_confirmed=True,
        )
        baseline_run_id = aggregate.id
        production_run_id = aggregate.items[0].production_run_id
        assert production_run_id is not None
        production = db.get(ProductionRun, production_run_id)
        assert production is not None
        attempt = db.scalar(
            select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == production_run_id,
                ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == "prompt_c",
            )
        )
        assert attempt is not None
        snapshot = json.loads(production.snapshot_json)
        node = next(
            item
            for item in snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]
            if item["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_c"
        )
        attempt_id = attempt.id
        db.commit()

    scan_finished = Event()
    original_close = (
        native_regression.fail_reserved_model_invocations_for_runs
    )

    def close_then_pause(*args, **kwargs):
        closed = original_close(*args, **kwargs)
        scan_finished.set()
        time.sleep(0.25)
        return closed

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        native_regression,
        "fail_reserved_model_invocations_for_runs",
        close_then_pause,
    )

    def cancel_run() -> int:
        with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
            affected = native_regression.cancel_native_baseline_runs(
                db,
                baseline_run_ids=[baseline_run_id],
            )
            db.commit()
            return affected

    def reserve_during_cancel() -> str:
        assert scan_finished.wait(timeout=5)
        with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
            try:
                reserve_model_invocation(
                    db,
                    attempt_id=attempt_id,
                    node=node,
                    run_snapshot=snapshot,
                )
                db.commit()
                return "reserved"
            except WorkflowModelBudgetError as exc:
                db.rollback()
                return exc.code

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
        cancel_future = pool.submit(cancel_run)
        reserve_future = pool.submit(reserve_during_cancel)
        assert cancel_future.result(timeout=10) > 0
        assert reserve_future.result(timeout=10) == "model_run_terminal_state"

    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        assert (
            db.scalar(
                select(func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id)).where(
                    WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id
                    == production_run_id,
                    WorkflowModelInvocation.status == "reserved",
                )
            )
            == 0
        )


def test_mysql_provider_settlement_and_cancel_share_one_lock_order(
    mysql_engine,
) -> None:
    """Provider settlement must not deadlock against operator cancellation."""

    import app.native_baseline_regression as native_regression
    from app.workflow_model_budget import (
        reserve_model_invocation,
        settle_model_invocation,
    )
    from app.workflow_runtime import complete_runtime_step

    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection)

    lease_token = "mysql-provider-settlement-race"
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        baseline = _baseline(db)
        workflow = _mechanism(db)
        _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)
        aggregate = native_regression.create_native_baseline_run(
            db,
            baseline_set=baseline,
            workflow_version=workflow,
            actor="ops",
            idempotency_key="mysql-provider-settlement-race",
            model_execution_mode="real",
            real_execution_confirmed=True,
        )
        baseline_run_id = aggregate.id
        production_run_id = aggregate.items[0].production_run_id
        assert production_run_id is not None
        production = db.get(ProductionRun, production_run_id)
        assert production is not None
        production.status = "running"
        attempt = db.scalar(
            select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == production_run_id,
                ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == "prompt_c",
            )
        )
        assert attempt is not None
        attempt.status = "leased"
        attempt.lease_owner = "mysql-settlement-worker"
        attempt.lease_token = lease_token
        snapshot = json.loads(production.snapshot_json)
        node = next(
            item
            for item in snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]
            if item["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_c"
        )
        invocation = reserve_model_invocation(
            db,
            attempt_id=attempt.id,
            node=node,
            run_snapshot=snapshot,
        )
        invocation_id = invocation.id
        attempt_id = attempt.id
        db.commit()

    settlement_started = Event()

    def settle_then_complete() -> str:
        with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
            settled = settle_model_invocation(
                db,
                invocation_id=invocation_id,
                request_correlation_id="mysql-settlement-race",
                upstream_status_code=200,
                input_tokens=1,
                output_tokens=1,
                evidence={"attempt_count": 1},
            )
            settlement_started.set()
            time.sleep(0.25)
            complete_runtime_step(
                db,
                attempt_id,
                lease_token,
                {"status": "succeeded", "result": {"level": "L1"}},
            )
            db.commit()
            return settled.status

    def cancel_after_settlement_starts() -> int:
        assert settlement_started.wait(timeout=5)
        with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
            affected = native_regression.cancel_native_baseline_runs(
                db,
                baseline_run_ids=[baseline_run_id],
            )
            db.commit()
            return affected

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
        settlement_future = pool.submit(settle_then_complete)
        cancel_future = pool.submit(cancel_after_settlement_starts)
        assert settlement_future.result(timeout=10) == "succeeded"
        assert cancel_future.result(timeout=10) > 0

    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        invocation = db.get(WorkflowModelInvocation, invocation_id)
        assert invocation is not None
        assert invocation.status == "succeeded"
        assert invocation.finished_at is not None


def test_mysql_paid_correction_retry_is_serialized_and_scheduled_once(
    mysql_engine,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """Two concurrent paid retries must produce one attempt and one task."""

    import app.main as main_module
    from app.database import get_db
    from app.main import app, current_user

    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection)

    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        _source, correction, _candidate = _real_correction_case(
            db,
            real_execution_confirmed=True,
        )
        correction.status = "failed"
        correction.stage = "analysis"
        correction.progress = 10
        correction.error_code = "provider_failed"
        correction.error_message = "provider failed"
        correction.attempt_count = 1
        correction_id = correction.id
        db.commit()

    def override_db():
        with Session(mysql_engine, expire_on_commit=False) as db:
            yield db

    manager = User(
        username="manager",
        password_hash="unused",
        display_name="项目管理员",
        role="product_rd",
        is_admin=False,
    )
    request_start = Barrier(2)
    pre_audit = Barrier(2)
    provider_calls: list[int] = []
    original_append_audit_event = main_module.append_audit_event

    def synchronized_append_audit_event(*args, **kwargs):
        try:
            pre_audit.wait(timeout=0.5)
        except BrokenBarrierError:
            pass
        return original_append_audit_event(*args, **kwargs)

    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = override_db
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: manager
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        main_module,
        "append_audit_event",
        synchronized_append_audit_event,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        main_module,
        "_run_baseline_correction_background",
        lambda row_id, *_args, **_kwargs: provider_calls.append(row_id),
    )

    def retry_once() -> tuple[int, dict]:
        request_start.wait(timeout=5)
        response = TestClient(app).post(
            f"/api/baseline-corrections/{correction_id}/retry",
            json={"real_execution_confirmed": True},
        )
        return response.status_code, response.json()

    try:
        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
            outcomes = [
                future.result(timeout=10)
                for future in (pool.submit(retry_once), pool.submit(retry_once))
            ]

        assert sorted(status for status, _payload in outcomes) == [200, 409]
        assert provider_calls == [correction_id]
        with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
            frozen = db.get(BaselineCorrectionRun, correction_id)
            assert frozen is not None
            assert frozen.status == "processing"
            assert frozen.attempt_count == 2
            assert (
                db.scalar(
                    select(func.count(AuditEvent.id)).where(
                        AuditEvent.action == "correction_retried",
                        AuditEvent.subject_id == str(correction_id),
                    )
                )
                == 1
            )
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()


def test_mysql_stale_correction_reconcile_is_serialized(
    mysql_engine,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """Concurrent status polls must converge through one locked recovery event."""

    import app.main as main_module
    from app.database import get_db
    from app.main import app, current_user

    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection)

    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        _source, correction, _candidate = _real_correction_case(
            db,
            real_execution_confirmed=True,
        )
        correction.status = "processing"
        correction.stage = "analysis"
        correction.progress = 10
        correction.orchestration_json = canonical_json(
            {
                "background_claim": {
                    "schema_version": "baseline-correction-claim-v1",
                    "attempt_count": correction.attempt_count,
                    "claim_token": "stale-claim-token",
                    "phase": "provider_io_started",
                    "claimed_at": "2026-08-31T00:00:00+00:00",
                    "lease_expires_at": "2026-08-31T00:30:00+00:00",
                }
            }
        )
        correction_id = correction.id
        db.commit()

    def override_db():
        with Session(mysql_engine, expire_on_commit=False) as db:
            yield db

    manager = User(
        username="manager",
        password_hash="unused",
        display_name="项目管理员",
        role="product_rd",
        is_admin=False,
    )
    reconcile_barrier = Barrier(2)
    original_append_audit_event = main_module.append_audit_event

    def synchronized_append_audit_event(*args, **kwargs):
        if kwargs.get("action") == "correction_background_reconciled":
            try:
                reconcile_barrier.wait(timeout=0.5)
            except BrokenBarrierError:
                pass
        return original_append_audit_event(*args, **kwargs)

    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = override_db
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: manager
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        main_module,
        "append_audit_event",
        synchronized_append_audit_event,
    )

    def poll_once() -> tuple[int, dict]:
        response = TestClient(app).get(
            f"/api/baseline-corrections/{correction_id}"
        )
        return response.status_code, response.json()

    try:
        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
            outcomes = [
                future.result(timeout=10)
                for future in (pool.submit(poll_once), pool.submit(poll_once))
            ]

        assert [status for status, _payload in outcomes] == [200, 200]
        assert all(payload["status"] == "failed" for _status, payload in outcomes)
        with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
            assert (
                db.scalar(
                    select(func.count(AuditEvent.id)).where(
                        AuditEvent.action == "correction_background_reconciled",
                        AuditEvent.subject_id == str(correction_id),
                    )
                )
                == 1
            )
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()


def test_paid_correction_retry_requires_a_fresh_confirmation(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """Every paid retry is a new provider charge and needs a new acknowledgement."""

    from app.database import get_db
    from app.main import app, current_user

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'paid-retry-confirm.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    background_calls: list[int] = []
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            _source, correction, _candidate = _real_correction_case(
                db,
                real_execution_confirmed=True,
            )
            correction.status = "failed"
            correction.stage = "analysis"
            correction.progress = 0
            correction.error_code = "provider_failed"
            correction.error_message = "provider failed"
            correction_id = correction.id
            db.commit()

        def override_db():
            with sessions() as db:
                yield db

        app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = override_db
        app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: User(
            username="manager",
            password_hash="unused",
            display_name="项目管理员",
            role="product_rd",
            is_admin=False,
        )
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            "app.main._run_baseline_correction_background",
            lambda row_id, *_args, **_kwargs: background_calls.append(row_id),
        )
        client = TestClient(app)

        denied = client.post(
            f"/api/baseline-corrections/{correction_id}/retry",
            json={"real_execution_confirmed": False},
        )

        assert denied.status_code == 422, denied.text
        assert denied.json()["detail"]["code"] == (
            "workflow_candidate_real_confirmation_required"
        )
        assert background_calls == []
        with sessions() as db:
            frozen = db.get(BaselineCorrectionRun, correction_id)
            assert frozen is not None
            assert frozen.status == "failed"
            assert frozen.attempt_count == 1

        allowed = client.post(
            f"/api/baseline-corrections/{correction_id}/retry",
            json={"real_execution_confirmed": True},
        )

        assert allowed.status_code == 200, allowed.text
        assert allowed.json()["attempt_count"] == 2
        assert background_calls == [correction_id]
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        engine.dispose()


def test_stale_paid_correction_claim_is_failed_without_automatic_replay(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """A crash after claim must converge to retryable failure, never replay provider I/O."""

    from app.audit import append_audit_event
    from app.database import get_db
    from app.main import app, current_user

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'stale-paid-claim.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    analysis_calls: list[int] = []
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            _source, correction, _candidate = _real_correction_case(
                db,
                real_execution_confirmed=True,
            )
            correction.status = "processing"
            correction.stage = "analysis"
            correction.progress = 10
            correction.orchestration_json = canonical_json(
                {
                    "background_claim": {
                        "schema_version": "baseline-correction-claim-v1",
                        "attempt_count": correction.attempt_count,
                        "phase": "provider_io_started",
                        "claimed_at": "2026-08-31T00:00:00+00:00",
                        "lease_expires_at": "2026-08-31T00:30:00+00:00",
                    }
                }
            )
            append_audit_event(
                db,
                category="baseline_regression",
                action="correction_background_claimed",
                subject_type="baseline_correction_run",
                subject_id=correction.id,
                actor="automatic-correction",
                payload={"attempt_count": correction.attempt_count},
                event_key=(
                    f"baseline-correction:{correction.id}:background:"
                    f"{correction.attempt_count}"
                ),
            )
            correction_id = correction.id
            db.commit()

        def override_db():
            with sessions() as db:
                yield db

        app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = override_db
        app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: User(
            username="manager",
            password_hash="unused",
            display_name="项目管理员",
            role="product_rd",
            is_admin=False,
        )
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            "app.main.analyze_workflow_corrections",
            lambda *_args, **_kwargs: analysis_calls.append(correction_id),
        )

        response = TestClient(app).get(
            f"/api/baseline-corrections/{correction_id}"
        )

        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        assert response.json()["status"] == "failed"
        assert response.json()["error"] == {
            "code": "CORRECTION_PROVIDER_OUTCOME_UNKNOWN",
            "message": "纠偏后台任务在外部调用阶段失去租约；为避免重复计费，系统未自动重放，请人工确认后重试",
            "retryable": True,
        }
        assert analysis_calls == []
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        engine.dispose()


def test_late_provider_result_cannot_overwrite_reconciled_failure(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """A provider reply arriving after lease recovery must be discarded."""

    from datetime import datetime, timezone

    import app.main as main_module

    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'late-provider-result.db'}",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    provider_started = Event()
    release_provider = Event()
    advance_calls: list[int] = []
    try:
        with sessions() as db:
            source, correction, _candidate = _real_correction_case(
                db,
                real_execution_confirmed=True,
            )
            source.execution_engine = "legacy_worker"
            correction_id = correction.id
            attempt_count = correction.attempt_count
            db.commit()

        monkeypatch.setattr(
            main_module,
            "prepare_correction_generation",
            lambda _db, _row: {"prepared": True},
        )
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            main_module,
            "configured_correction_generator",
            lambda _db: object(),
        )

        def delayed_generation(_prepared, _generator):
            provider_started.set()
            assert release_provider.wait(timeout=10)
            return {"provider_result": "late"}

        def forbidden_advance(_db, row, _unused, _generated):
            advance_calls.append(row.id)
            row.status = "awaiting_decision"
            row.stage = "decision"

        monkeypatch.setattr(
            main_module,
            "generate_correction_candidate",
            delayed_generation,
        )
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            main_module,
            "advance_correction_run",
            forbidden_advance,
        )

        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1) as pool:
            future = pool.submit(
                main_module._run_baseline_correction_background,
                correction_id,
                engine,
                attempt_count,
            )
            assert provider_started.wait(timeout=10)
            with sessions() as db:
                row = main_module._locked_baseline_correction(db, correction_id)
                assert row is not None
                assert main_module._reconcile_stale_correction_background(
                    db,
                    row,
                    now=datetime(2030, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
                ) is True
                db.commit()
            release_provider.set()
            future.result(timeout=10)

        with sessions() as db:
            frozen = db.get(BaselineCorrectionRun, correction_id)
            assert frozen is not None
            assert frozen.status == "failed"
            assert frozen.error_code == "CORRECTION_PROVIDER_OUTCOME_UNKNOWN"
            assert advance_calls == []
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_run_detail_reports_step_progress_while_running(tmp_path) -> None:
    """运行中的 run 详情要报出步骤级进度，供进度条平滑显示。

    11 步流水线按「整条走完」计数时，前 80% 时间进度都趴在原地——run136 跑到
    第 40 分钟界面还显示 2/250，运营两次以为卡死。
    """

    from app.main import app, current_user
    from app.database import get_db
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run

    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'step-progress.db'}",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        baseline = _baseline(db)
        workflow = _mechanism(db)
        _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)
        aggregate = create_native_baseline_run(
            db,
            baseline_set=baseline,
            workflow_version=workflow,
            actor="ops",
            idempotency_key="step-progress",
            model_execution_mode="real",
            real_execution_confirmed=True,
        )
        db.commit()
        user = User(
            username="step-progress-viewer",
            password_hash="unused",
            display_name="步骤进度测试员",
            role="admin",
            is_admin=True,
        )
        db.add(user)
        db.commit()
        app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: (yield db)
        app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
        client = TestClient(app)
        try:
            detail = client.get(f"/api/baseline-regressions/{aggregate.id}")
            assert detail.status_code == 200, detail.text
            body = detail.json()
            assert body["summary"]["status"] == "running", body["summary"]
            progress = body["step_progress"]
            assert progress is not None
            assert progress["total_steps"] > 0
            assert progress["done_steps"] >= 0
            assert isinstance(progress["per_step"], dict)
            assert progress["per_step"], "至少要有一个步骤的分布"
        finally:
            app.dependency_overrides.clear()
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_real_budget_uses_historical_output_expectation_without_lowering_ceiling() -> None:
    """预计输出按历史 p95 缩小，但 max_* 预留仍按 max_tokens：预览便宜了，配额扣留不能跟着变小。"""

    from app.workflow_model_budget import (
        real_execution_budget_for_selection,
        real_execution_node_limits,
    )

    prompt = _prompt("prompt_unified")
    prompt["retry_policy"] = {"kind": "none"}
    prompt["config"]["model_snapshot"]["provider"] = "doubao"
    manifest = {"nodes": [prompt], "edges": []}
    expectation = {
        "prompt_unified": {
            "source": "prompt_history",
            "sample_size": 18,
            "p95_output_tokens": 50,
            "output_tokens": 50,
            "output_tokens_per_request": 50,
            "output_cap_per_request": 200,
        }
    }

    budget = real_execution_budget_for_selection(
        manifest,
        item_count=2,
        model_configs={1: _priced_model()},
        input_manifests=[{}, {}],
        output_expectations=expectation,
    )
    baseline = real_execution_budget_for_selection(
        manifest,
        item_count=2,
        model_configs={1: _priced_model()},
        input_manifests=[{}, {}],
    )

    assert baseline.expected_output_tokens == 400
    assert budget.expected_output_tokens == 100
    assert budget.expected_cost_micros < baseline.expected_cost_micros
    assert budget.max_output_tokens == baseline.max_output_tokens == 400
    assert budget.max_cost_micros == baseline.max_cost_micros

    limits = real_execution_node_limits(
        manifest,
        model_configs={1: _priced_model()},
        input_manifests=[{}, {}],
        output_expectations=expectation,
    )
    assert limits["prompt_unified"]["max_output_tokens"] == 200
    assert limits["prompt_unified"]["expected_output_tokens"] == 100
    assert limits["prompt_unified"]["expected_output_basis"]["source"] == "prompt_history"

    # 历史 p95 高于上限时不能反向抬高预计
    inflated = real_execution_budget_for_selection(
        manifest,
        item_count=1,
        model_configs={1: _priced_model()},
        input_manifests=[{}],
        output_expectations={"prompt_unified": {"output_tokens": 9_999}},
    )
    assert inflated.expected_output_tokens == 200


def _settled_invocation(
    db: Session,
    *,
    step_attempt_id: int,
    node_instance_id: str,
    output_tokens: int,
    component_hash: str,
    thinking_mode: str | None,
    model_id: str = "budget-model",
) -> WorkflowModelInvocation:
    attempt = ProductionStepAttempt(
        id=step_attempt_id,
        run_id=1,
        step_key=f"{node_instance_id}-{step_attempt_id}",
        step_type="model_call",
        sequence=step_attempt_id,
        script_version_id=1,
        status="succeeded",
        attempt_no=1,
        idempotency_key=f"history-attempt-{step_attempt_id}",
        input_manifest_json="{}",
        input_hash="b" * 64,
        output_manifest_json="{}",
        checkpoint_json="{}",
        last_error_code="",
        last_error_message="",
    )
    db.add(attempt)
    db.flush()
    evidence = {"schema_version": "workflow-model-evidence-v1", "component_hash": component_hash}
    if thinking_mode is not None:
        evidence["thinking_mode"] = thinking_mode
    row = WorkflowModelInvocation(
        baseline_run_id=None,
        production_run_id=1,
        step_attempt_id=step_attempt_id,
        node_instance_id=node_instance_id,
        status="succeeded",
        provider="doubao",
        model_id=model_id,
        reserved_input_tokens=1_000,
        reserved_output_tokens=200,
        input_tokens=500,
        output_tokens=output_tokens,
        total_tokens=500 + output_tokens,
        estimated_cost_micros=20,
        actual_cost_micros=10,
        evidence_json=canonical_json(evidence),
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.flush()
    return row


def test_historical_output_expectations_prefer_same_prompt_and_respect_thinking(tmp_path) -> None:
    """同提示词版本优先；思考开关不同的样本必须排除（思考 token 计入输出，量级差十倍）。"""

    from app.workflow_model_budget import historical_output_expectations

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'output-history.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            db.add(
                ProductionRun(
                    id=1,
                    run_key="history-run",
                    idempotency_key="history-run",
                    workflow_definition_id=1,
                    workflow_version_id=1,
                    snapshot_json="{}",
                    snapshot_hash="a" * 64,
                    status="succeeded",
                    requested_by="tests",
                    owner="tests",
                    reason="output history fixture",
                    environment="dry_run",
                    model_execution_mode="real",
                    total_steps=1,
                    completed_steps=1,
                    failed_steps=0,
                    attempt_count=1,
                    error_code="",
                    error_message="",
                )
            )
            db.flush()
            next_id = 1
            def add(node: str, outputs: list[int], *, component_hash: str, thinking_mode: str | None):
                nonlocal next_id
                for value in outputs:
                    _settled_invocation(
                        db,
                        step_attempt_id=next_id,
                        node_instance_id=node,
                        output_tokens=value,
                        component_hash=component_hash,
                        thinking_mode=thinking_mode,
                    )
                    next_id += 1

            # 同提示词、思考关闭：6 条，p95 取到 130
            add("prompt_single", [80, 90, 100, 110, 120, 130], component_hash="c" * 64, thinking_mode="disabled")
            # 旧提示词版本、思考关闭：更大的数，只有在同版本样本不足时才会被用到
            add("prompt_single", [900, 950, 1_000, 1_050, 1_100, 1_150], component_hash="d" * 64, thinking_mode="disabled")
            # 思考开启的样本：不能污染「思考关闭」节点的预计
            add("prompt_single", [5_000] * 6, component_hash="c" * 64, thinking_mode="enabled")
            # 另一个节点只有 2 条：样本不足，退回上限
            add("prompt_pair", [70, 75], component_hash="e" * 64, thinking_mode="disabled")

            def node(node_id: str, *, content_hash: str, thinking: bool, cap: int = 8_192) -> dict:
                return {
                    "node_instance_id": node_id,
                    "kind": "prompt",
                    "config": {
                        "max_tokens": cap,
                        "thinking_enabled": thinking,
                        "thinking_level": "high" if thinking else "none",
                        "component_snapshot": {"content_hash": content_hash},
                        "model_snapshot": {"source_model_config_id": 1, "model_id": "budget-model"},
                    },
                }

            same_prompt = historical_output_expectations(
                db, {"nodes": [node("prompt_single", content_hash="c" * 64, thinking=False)]}
            )
            assert same_prompt["prompt_single"] == {
                "source": "prompt_history",
                "sample_size": 6,
                "p95_output_tokens": 130,
                "output_tokens": 130,
                "output_tokens_per_request": 130,
                "output_cap_per_request": 8_192,
            }

            revised_prompt = historical_output_expectations(
                db, {"nodes": [node("prompt_single", content_hash="f" * 64, thinking=False)]}
            )
            assert revised_prompt["prompt_single"]["source"] == "node_history"
            assert revised_prompt["prompt_single"]["sample_size"] == 12
            assert revised_prompt["prompt_single"]["p95_output_tokens"] == 1_150

            thinking_on = historical_output_expectations(
                db, {"nodes": [node("prompt_single", content_hash="c" * 64, thinking=True)]}
            )
            assert thinking_on["prompt_single"]["source"] == "prompt_history"
            assert thinking_on["prompt_single"]["p95_output_tokens"] == 5_000

            capped = historical_output_expectations(
                db, {"nodes": [node("prompt_single", content_hash="c" * 64, thinking=False, cap=100)]}
            )
            assert capped["prompt_single"]["output_tokens"] == 100
            assert capped["prompt_single"]["p95_output_tokens"] == 130

            sparse = historical_output_expectations(
                db, {"nodes": [node("prompt_pair", content_hash="e" * 64, thinking=False)]}
            )
            assert sparse["prompt_pair"] == {
                "source": "output_cap",
                "sample_size": 2,
                "p95_output_tokens": None,
                "output_tokens_per_request": 8_192,
                "output_cap_per_request": 8_192,
            }

            unknown = historical_output_expectations(
                db, {"nodes": [node("prompt_never_ran", content_hash="e" * 64, thinking=False)]}
            )
            assert unknown["prompt_never_ran"]["source"] == "output_cap"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def _vote_manifest(extra):
    import json as _json
    prompt = _prompt("prompt_b")
    prompt["config"].update({"max_tokens": 1_000, "component_snapshot": {"kind": "prompt", "content": {
        "stage": "B", "system_prompt": "评分。", "user_prompt": "返回结构化结果。"}}})
    prompt["config"]["model_snapshot"]["provider"] = "doubao"
    prompt["config"].update(extra)
    anchors = {"node_instance_id": "anchor_calibration_6", "kind": "rule_module", "config": {"component_snapshot": {
        "content_json": _json.dumps({"module_type_key": "anchor_calibration", "anchors": [{"level": "L1", "asset_id": i} for i in range(15)]})}}}
    return {"nodes": [prompt, anchors], "edges": []}


def test_node_limits_and_budget_scale_with_vote_and_pairwise_call_counts() -> None:
    """投票 5 次 / 成对比较 15 张锚点：节点限额记 call_count（单次计价不变），期望请求数与运行预算按实际请求次数放大（run 350 全军覆没的根因）。"""
    from app.workflow_model_budget import prompt_call_count, real_execution_budget_for_selection, real_execution_node_limits

    model = _priced_model(max_input_tokens=128_001, max_tokens=1_000,
                          pricing_contract_json=canonical_json(doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing()))
    inputs = [{"description": "small"}]
    base = real_execution_node_limits(_vote_manifest({}), model_configs={1: model}, input_manifests=inputs)["prompt_b"]
    vote = real_execution_node_limits(_vote_manifest({"vote_samples": 5}), model_configs={1: model}, input_manifests=inputs)["prompt_b"]
    pair = real_execution_node_limits(_vote_manifest({"pairwise_anchors": True, "pairwise_samples": 1}), model_configs={1: model}, input_manifests=inputs)["prompt_b"]
    both = _vote_manifest({"vote_samples": 5, "pairwise_anchors": True, "pairwise_samples": 2})

    assert base["call_count"] == 1 and vote["call_count"] == 5 and pair["call_count"] == 16
    assert prompt_call_count(both["nodes"][0], both) == 5 + 30
    assert vote["max_input_tokens"] == base["max_input_tokens"] and vote["estimated_cost_micros"] == base["estimated_cost_micros"]
    assert vote["expected_request_count"] == 5 * base["expected_request_count"]
    assert vote["expected_cost_micros"] == 5 * base["expected_cost_micros"]

    base_budget = real_execution_budget_for_selection(_vote_manifest({}), 1, model_configs={1: model}, input_manifests=inputs)
    vote_budget = real_execution_budget_for_selection(_vote_manifest({"vote_samples": 5}), 1, model_configs={1: model}, input_manifests=inputs)
    assert vote_budget.expected_requests == 5 * base_budget.expected_requests
    assert vote_budget.max_input_tokens == 5 * base_budget.max_input_tokens
    assert vote_budget.max_cost_micros == 5 * base_budget.max_cost_micros


def test_online_fallback_limits_count_pairwise_anchor_calls() -> None:
    """在线兜底限额不能只看单节点清单：成对比较对 anchor_calibration 每张锚点的请求也要计入 call_count。"""
    from app.workflow_model_budget import real_execution_node_limits

    pricing = canonical_json(doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing())
    model = _priced_model(max_input_tokens=128_001, max_tokens=1_000, pricing_contract_json=pricing)
    model._online_fallback_model = _priced_model(max_input_tokens=128_001, max_tokens=1_000, pricing_contract_json=pricing)
    manifest = _vote_manifest({"vote_samples": 3, "pairwise_anchors": True, "pairwise_samples": 2})
    limits = real_execution_node_limits(manifest, model_configs={1: model})["prompt_b"]
    assert limits["call_count"] == 3 + 15 * 2
    assert limits["online_fallback_limits"]["call_count"] == limits["call_count"]


def test_reservation_scales_with_frozen_call_count(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.workflow_model_budget import reserve_model_invocation

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'real-reservation-callcount.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            baseline = _baseline(db); workflow = _mechanism(db); _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)
            aggregate = create_native_baseline_run(db, baseline_set=baseline, workflow_version=workflow, actor="ops",
                idempotency_key="real-reservation-callcount", model_execution_mode="real", real_execution_confirmed=True)
            production_id = aggregate.items[0].production_run_id
            db.commit()
        with Session(engine) as db:
            production = db.get(ProductionRun, production_id)
            attempt = db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == production.id, ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == "prompt_c"))
            snapshot = json.loads(production.snapshot_json)
            runtime = snapshot["runtime_context"]
            runtime["real_execution_node_limits"]["prompt_c"]["call_count"] = 2
            for key in ("max_requests", "max_input_tokens", "max_output_tokens", "max_cost_micros"):
                runtime["real_execution_budget"][key] *= 2
            production.snapshot_json = json.dumps(snapshot); db.flush()
            node = next(item for item in snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"] if item["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_c")
            voted = reserve_model_invocation(db, attempt_id=attempt.id, node=node, run_snapshot=snapshot, dispatch_identity="a" * 64)
            assert voted.reserved_input_tokens == 24_000
            assert voted.reserved_output_tokens == 16_384
            assert voted.estimated_cost_micros == 2 * 28_384
            assert json.loads(voted.evidence_json)["reservation"]["call_count"] == 2
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def _reserved_multi_call_invocation(db: Session, *, call_count: int):
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.workflow_model_budget import reserve_model_invocation

    baseline = _baseline(db); workflow = _mechanism(db); _attach_priced_model(db, workflow)
    aggregate = create_native_baseline_run(db, baseline_set=baseline, workflow_version=workflow, actor="ops",
        idempotency_key=f"multi-call-settle-{call_count}", model_execution_mode="real", real_execution_confirmed=True)
    production = db.get(ProductionRun, aggregate.items[0].production_run_id)
    attempt = db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
        ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == production.id, ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == "prompt_c"))
    snapshot = json.loads(production.snapshot_json)
    runtime = snapshot["runtime_context"]
    runtime["real_execution_node_limits"]["prompt_c"]["call_count"] = call_count
    for key in ("max_requests", "max_input_tokens", "max_output_tokens", "max_cost_micros"):
        runtime["real_execution_budget"][key] *= call_count
    production.snapshot_json = json.dumps(snapshot); db.flush()
    node = next(item for item in snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"] if item["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_c")
    row = reserve_model_invocation(db, attempt_id=attempt.id, node=node, run_snapshot=snapshot, dispatch_identity="b" * 64)
    # 换成阶梯计价快照并放大预留，只验证结算选档口径。
    row.pricing_snapshot_json = canonical_json(doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing())
    row.reserved_input_tokens = 1_000_000
    row.reserved_output_tokens = 100_000
    db.flush()
    return row


def test_multi_call_settlement_prices_each_request_tier_not_the_sum(tmp_path) -> None:
    """投票/成对比较节点：合计 token 不能当一次请求选档（偏高计费；合计超 256k 直接 tier_not_found）。"""
    from app.workflow_model_budget import settle_model_invocation

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'multi-call-settle.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            row = _reserved_multi_call_invocation(db, call_count=10)
            # 10 次请求各 30k 输入 / 1k 输出，合计 300k：每次都落 0-32000 档。
            settled = settle_model_invocation(db, invocation_id=row.id, request_correlation_id="req-vote",
                upstream_status_code=200, input_tokens=300_000, output_tokens=10_000, evidence={"ok": True})
            assert settled.status == "succeeded"
            assert settled.actual_cost_micros == 10 * (30 * 600 + 1 * 3_600)
            assert settled.pricing_tier_key == "0-32000"
            pricing = json.loads(settled.evidence_json)["pricing_settlement"]
            assert pricing["request_count"] == 10
            assert {entry["pricing_tier_key"] for entry in pricing["per_request"]} == {"0-32000"}
            db.rollback()
        with Session(engine) as db:
            row = _reserved_multi_call_invocation(db, call_count=2)
            # 执行器上报逐请求用量：一次 20k（0-32000 档）+ 一次 40k（32000-128000 档）。
            settled = settle_model_invocation(db, invocation_id=row.id, request_correlation_id="req-pair",
                upstream_status_code=200, input_tokens=60_000, output_tokens=2_000, evidence={"ok": True},
                request_usages=[{"input_tokens": 20_000, "output_tokens": 1_000},
                                {"input_tokens": 40_000, "output_tokens": 1_000}])
            assert settled.status == "succeeded"
            assert settled.actual_cost_micros == (20 * 600 + 3_600) + (40 * 900 + 5_400)
            assert settled.pricing_tier_key == "0-32000+32000-128000"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_native_workflow_scheduling_drift.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from app.native_workflow_migration import _comparable_manifest


def _manifest(concurrency: int, prompt_version: str = "v1") -> dict:
    return {
        "nodes": [
            {
                "node_instance_id": "prompt_a",
                "kind": "prompt",
                "config": {
                    "component_snapshot": {"version": prompt_version},
                    "model_snapshot": {
                        "model_id": "doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260215",
                        "runtime_config": {
                            "max_concurrency": concurrency,
                            "max_retries": 1,
                            "timeout_seconds": 60,
                            "max_tokens": 4096,
                        },
                    },
                },
            }
        ]
    }


def test_changing_concurrency_does_not_look_like_a_mechanism_change() -> None:
    """改并发不该让服务起不来。

    2026-08-28：把模型并发从 8 调到 3 后，测试服重启时启动自检判定
    「版本 native-r9 已存在但内容与迁移结果不同」，部署与回滚双双失败，
    容器陷入重启循环。并发只影响怎么跑，不影响跑出什么。
    """

    assert _comparable_manifest(_manifest(8)) == _comparable_manifest(_manifest(3))


def test_real_mechanism_drift_is_still_detected() -> None:
    """放宽只针对调度参数：提示词换版仍必须被判定为内容不同。"""

    assert _comparable_manifest(_manifest(8, "v1")) != _comparable_manifest(
        _manifest(8, "v2")
    )


def test_model_identity_and_output_params_still_compared() -> None:
    """模型身份与影响输出的参数不在豁免之列。"""

    changed = _manifest(8)
    changed["nodes"][0]["config"]["model_snapshot"]["model_id"] = "other-model"
    assert _comparable_manifest(_manifest(8)) != _comparable_manifest(changed)

    tokens = _manifest(8)
    tokens["nodes"][0]["config"]["model_snapshot"]["runtime_config"][
        "max_tokens"
    ] = 2048
    assert _comparable_manifest(_manifest(8)) != _comparable_manifest(tokens)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_new_server_acceptance.py =====
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest


def evidence():
    start = datetime(2026, 9, 14, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    end = start + timedelta(hours=4)
    return dict(start=start.isoformat(), end=end.isoformat(), evidence_kind='actual_e2e',
        inventory_complete=True,
        samples=[dict(observed_at=(start + timedelta(seconds=15*i)).isoformat(),
                      scope_revision='fixture-revision', workers_expected=3, workers_seen=3,
                      submitted_total=i, settled_total=i, dispatch_paused=False,
                      backlog_high=10, backlog_normal=20, oldest_normal_age_seconds=120)
                 for i in range(960)],
        items=[dict(event_id='h1', service_class='high',
                    first_queued_at=start.isoformat(),
                    usable_result_at=(start + timedelta(hours=1)).isoformat()),
               dict(event_id='n1', service_class='normal', first_queued_at=start.isoformat(),
                    usable_result_at=(start + timedelta(hours=1)).isoformat())]
              + [dict(event_id=f'{service}-{hour}', service_class=service,
                      first_queued_at=(start + timedelta(hours=hour)).isoformat(),
                      usable_result_at=(start + timedelta(hours=hour, minutes=30)).isoformat())
                 for hour in (1, 2, 3) for service in ('high', 'normal')],
        events=[dict(event_id='four-hour-1', kind='four_hour', occurred_at=end.isoformat(),
                     logged_at=end.isoformat(), log_sequence=962, log_hash='a'*64)],
        receipts=[dict(event_id='four-hour-1', received_at=end.isoformat(),
                       receipt_id='external-receipt-1')])


def assess(data):
    from app.new_server_acceptance import assess_window
    return assess_window(**data)


def test_complete_window_is_only_a_window_not_daily_capacity_proof():
    result = assess(evidence())
    assert result['accepted'] and result['coverage'] == 1
    assert result['daily_100k_proven'] is False
    data = evidence()
    data['evidence_kind'] = 'synthetic'
    result = assess(data)
    assert not result['accepted'] and result['software_window_passed']


@pytest.mark.parametrize('missing_class', ['both', 'high', 'normal'])
def test_single_batch_or_missing_hour_cannot_prove_continuous_mixed_input(missing_class):
    data = evidence()
    if missing_class == 'both':
        data['items'] = data['items'][:2]
    else:
        data['items'] = [item for item in data['items']
                         if item['event_id'] != f'{missing_class}-2']
    result = assess(data)
    assert not result['accepted']
    assert 'continuous_input_missing' in result['blockers']


def test_continuous_input_counts_arrivals_not_completion_times():
    data = evidence()
    for item in data['items']:
        item['first_queued_at'] = data['start']
    result = assess(data)
    assert not result['accepted']
    assert 'continuous_input_missing' in result['blockers']


@pytest.mark.parametrize('mutation,blocker', [
    ('short', 'window_short'), ('duplicates', 'duplicate_samples'),
    ('missing', 'coverage_gap'), ('worker_missing', 'workers_missing'),
    ('pause', 'dispatch_paused'), ('pending_overdue', 'high_sla_violation'),
    ('late_result', 'high_sla_violation'), ('no_log', 'event_not_persisted'),
