    ('early_four_hour', 'four_hour_event_invalid'), ('reset', 'counter_reset'),
    ('growing_backlog', 'normal_backlog_growing'), ('incomplete', 'inventory_incomplete'),
    ('stall', 'no_returns_with_backlog'), ('no_normal', 'mixed_workload_missing'),
])
def test_incomplete_or_unsafe_windows_fail_closed(mutation, blocker):
    data = evidence()
    if mutation == 'short': data['end'] = data['samples'][-1]['observed_at']
    if mutation == 'duplicates': data['samples'] = [data['samples'][0]] * 960
    if mutation == 'missing': data['samples'] = data['samples'][50:]
    if mutation == 'worker_missing': data['samples'][100]['workers_seen'] = 2
    if mutation == 'pause': data['samples'][100]['dispatch_paused'] = True
    if mutation == 'pending_overdue':
        data['items'][0].update(first_queued_at='2026-09-13T20:00:00+00:00', usable_result_at=None)
    if mutation == 'late_result':
        data['items'][0].update(first_queued_at='2026-09-13T20:00:00+00:00',
                                usable_result_at='2026-09-14T02:00:00+00:00')
    if mutation == 'no_log': data['events'][0].pop('log_hash')
    if mutation == 'early_four_hour': data['events'][0]['occurred_at'] = data['start']
    if mutation == 'reset': data['samples'][-1]['submitted_total'] = 0
    if mutation == 'growing_backlog': data['samples'][-1]['backlog_normal'] = 100
    if mutation == 'incomplete': data['inventory_complete'] = False
    if mutation == 'stall':
        for sample in data['samples']: sample['settled_total'] = 0
    if mutation == 'no_normal': data['items'] = data['items'][:1]
    result = assess(data)
    assert not result['accepted'] and blocker in result['blockers']


def test_invalid_and_out_of_order_evidence_is_not_silently_sorted():
    data = evidence()
    data['samples'][0], data['samples'][1] = data['samples'][1], data['samples'][0]
    assert 'sample_time_order' in assess(data)['blockers']
    data = evidence()
    data['items'][0]['first_queued_at'] = '2026-09-14T00:00:00'
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='timezone'):
        assess(data)


def test_readonly_transaction_rejects_writes_and_restores_connection(tmp_path):
    from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text
    from sqlalchemy.exc import OperationalError
    from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
    from app.incremental_capacity_observer import readonly_observation
    engine = create_engine(f'sqlite:///{tmp_path / "readonly.db"}')
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.execute(text('CREATE TABLE sentinel (id INTEGER)'))
        with Session(engine) as db:
            with pytest.raises(OperationalError, match='readonly'):
                with readonly_observation(db):
                    db.execute(text('INSERT INTO sentinel VALUES (1)'))
            assert not db.in_transaction()
            assert db.scalar(text('PRAGMA query_only')) == 0
            assert db.scalar(text('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sentinel')) == 0
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_root_cli_cannot_start_ingress():
    from scripts.measure_incremental_capacity import parser
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        parser().parse_args(['actual-ingress'])


def test_persisted_log_does_not_require_notifications_or_receipts():
    data = evidence()
    data.pop('receipts')
    assert assess(data)['accepted']

===== FILE backend/tests/test_new_server_capacity.py =====
import asyncio
from dataclasses import replace
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.bounded_runtime import BoundedRuntime
from app.models import ModelQuotaPool
from test_batch_submission_pacing import database
from test_native_multiprocess_capacity_probe import probe_module


@pytest.mark.parametrize('total,processes,slots,threads', [
    (96, 3, 32, 1), (192, 3, 64, 4), (384, 6, 64, 4), (768, 6, 128, 8),
])
def test_stage_plan_is_bounded_and_is_not_a_validation_receipt(total, processes, slots, threads):
    from app.new_server_capacity import CapacityEnvelope
    stage = CapacityEnvelope.for_stage(total)
    assert (stage.processes, stage.slots_per_process, stage.phase_threads) == (processes, slots, threads)
    assert stage.total_slots == total
    assert stage.validated is False
    assert stage.connection_budget <= stage.mysql_max_connections
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='connection'):
        replace(stage, mysql_max_connections=10)


def test_expansion_is_opt_in_and_cannot_remove_probe_bounds():
    module = probe_module()
    assert module.validate_envelope(3, 32, extended=False) == 96
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        module.validate_envelope(3, 64, extended=False)
    assert module.validate_envelope(6, 128, extended=True) == 768
    for p, s in [(0, 32), (9, 32), (3, 0), (3, 257), (8, 128)]:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            module.validate_envelope(p, s, extended=True)


def test_feedback_measurement_uses_real_queries_without_changing_decision(database):
    from app.batch_submission import _feedback
    engine, pool_id = database

    async def scenario():
        async with BoundedRuntime(slots=1, phase_workers=1) as runtime:
            def query():
                with Session(engine) as db:
                    return _feedback(db, db.get(ModelQuotaPool, pool_id), datetime.now(timezone.utc))
            result = await runtime.phase(query)
            assert result['retry_at'] == 0 and result['overload_rate'] == 0
            metrics = runtime.lock_metrics.snapshot()
            assert metrics['batch_feedback']['count'] == 1
            assert metrics['batch_feedback']['p99_seconds'] >= 0
            assert metrics['pool_lock_wait']['count'] == 0
    asyncio.run(scenario())


def test_probe_can_exercise_split_runtime_without_disabling_old_mode():
    base = probe_module().load_base()

    async def scenario():
        args = SimpleNamespace(slots=3, phase_workers=2, split_admission=True)
        async with base.probe_runtime(args) as runtime:
            assert runtime.phase_admission.limit == 2
            assert await runtime.phase(lambda: 7) == 7
        args.split_admission = False
        async with base.probe_runtime(args) as runtime:
            assert runtime.phase_admission is None
            assert await runtime.phase(lambda: 8) == 8
    asyncio.run(scenario())

===== FILE backend/tests/test_node_correction.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi import HTTPException
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.dimension_deduction_bridge import empty_deduction_output
from app.evaluation_v3_pipeline import recompute_qualified_v3
from app.inspiration_category_seed import (
    build_inspiration_classification_map,
    build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions,
    build_inspiration_v3_contract,
)
from app.models import EvaluationResult
from app.node_correction_api import (
    CorrectNodeRequest,
    apply_node_correction,
    build_node_correction_router,
)
from app.worker_v3_authoritative import build_v3_authoritative_scoring


def _context() -> dict:
    return {
        "contract": build_inspiration_v3_contract(),
        "classification_map": build_inspiration_classification_map(),
        "subcategory_dimensions": build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions(),
        "config_revision": 3,
    }


def _bonus_context() -> dict:
    context = _context()
    dimensions = context["subcategory_dimensions"]["class_one"]["common_group"][
        "schema_definition"
    ]["dimensions"]
    for dimension in dimensions:
        dimension["dimension_score_cap"] = 100
        dimension["bonus_rules"] = []
    dimensions[0]["bonus_rules"] = [
        {
            "rule_id": "composition_clear",
            "description": "主体、留白与层级关系清晰",
            "bonus": 8,
            "tags": ["构图"],
        }
    ]
    return context


def _precheck() -> dict:
    return {
        "classification": {
            "scope_status": "in_scope",
            "primary_category": "建筑设计",
            "primary_confidence": 0.96,
        },
        "production_fields": {"reason": [], "trait": "实景照片"},
    }


def _full_call_a_precheck() -> dict:
    precheck = _precheck()
    precheck["production_fields"] = {
        "title": "现代住宅",
        "seotitle": "现代住宅空间设计参考",
        "category": "居住空间,大平层",
        "style": "现代简约",
        "tags": ["住宅", "客厅", "木饰面", "自然光"],
        "cons": "局部层次略显单薄",
        "design": "以自然光和材质层次组织空间",
        "score": 70,
        "reason": [],
        "image_defects": "",
        "trait": "实景照片",
    }
    return precheck


def _result_for_call_a(sessions: sessionmaker) -> int:
    context = _context()
    precheck = _full_call_a_precheck()
    dimension_output = empty_deduction_output(
        context["subcategory_dimensions"]["class_one"]
    )
    aggregate = recompute_qualified_v3(
        v3_context=context,
        precheck=precheck,
        dimension_output=dimension_output,
    )
    scoring = build_v3_authoritative_scoring(aggregate, precheck=precheck)
    scoring.pop("_dimension_deduction_raw_payload", None)
    scoring["v3_context"] = context
    scoring["score"] = 70
    scoring["level"] = "L2"
    with sessions() as db:
        row = EvaluationResult(
            asset_id=1,
            job_id=1,
            precheck_json=json.dumps(precheck, ensure_ascii=False),
            aesthetic_json=json.dumps(dimension_output, ensure_ascii=False),
            scoring_json=json.dumps(scoring, ensure_ascii=False),
            correction_history_json="[]",
            raw_response_a='{"immutable":"provider payload"}',
            score=70,
            level="L2",
            confidence=scoring["confidence"],
            needs_review=False,
            model_id="fake",
            prompt_a_version="a",
            rubric_version="r",
            engine_version="e",
        )
        db.add(row)
        db.commit()
        return int(row.id)


def test_correct_dimension_rule_appends_evidence_and_recomputes_downstream() -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    context = _context()
    config = context["subcategory_dimensions"]["class_one"]
    dimension_output = empty_deduction_output(config)
    precheck = _precheck()
    aggregate = recompute_qualified_v3(
        v3_context=context,
        precheck=precheck,
        dimension_output=dimension_output,
    )
    scoring = build_v3_authoritative_scoring(aggregate, precheck=precheck)
    scoring.pop("_dimension_deduction_raw_payload", None)
    scoring["v3_context"] = context

    with sessions() as db:
        row = EvaluationResult(
            asset_id=1,
            job_id=1,
            precheck_json=json.dumps(precheck, ensure_ascii=False),
            aesthetic_json=json.dumps(dimension_output, ensure_ascii=False),
            scoring_json=json.dumps(scoring, ensure_ascii=False),
            correction_history_json="[]",
            raw_response_a="{}",
            score=scoring["score"],
            level=scoring["level"],
            confidence=scoring["confidence"],
            needs_review=False,
            model_id="fake",
            prompt_a_version="a",
            rubric_version="r",
            engine_version="e",
        )
        db.add(row)
        db.commit()
        result_id = row.id
        original_score = row.score

    def db_dependency():
        db = sessions()
        try:
            yield db
        finally:
            db.close()

    def reviewer():
        return SimpleNamespace(username="reviewer", display_name="审核员")

    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(build_node_correction_router(reviewer))
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = db_dependency
    first_dimension = config["common_group"]["schema_definition"]["dimensions"][0]
    first_rule = first_dimension["deduction_rules"][0]
    new_hit = {
        "rule_id": first_rule["rule_id"],
        "confidence": "medium",
        "evidence": "主体明显偏移，左侧大面积空置",
    }
    payload = {
        "correction_key": "case-1-rule-1",
        "node_type": "dimension_rule",
        "node_path": f"dimension.{first_dimension['key']}.hit_rules.{first_rule['rule_id']}",
        "old_value": None,
        "new_value": new_hit,
        "evidence": [
            {
                "rule_id": first_rule["rule_id"],
                "old_confidence": None,
                "new_confidence": "medium",
                "old_evidence": "",
                "new_evidence": new_hit["evidence"],
            }
        ],
        "reason": "人工复核确认规则命中",
    }
    client = TestClient(app)
    invalid_payload = {
        **payload,
        "correction_key": "case-invalid-chinese-confidence",
        "new_value": {**new_hit, "confidence": "中"},
    }
    invalid = client.post(
        f"/api/evaluation-results/{result_id}/correct-node", json=invalid_payload
    )
    assert invalid.status_code == 400
    assert invalid.json()["detail"]["code"] == "dimension_rule_invalid"

    response = client.post(
        f"/api/evaluation-results/{result_id}/correct-node", json=payload
    )
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    body = response.json()
    assert body["score"] < original_score
    assert body["correction"]["downstream_recomputed"] is True
    assert body["correction"]["evidence"][0]["new_evidence"] == new_hit["evidence"]
    assert body["correction"]["new_value"]["confidence"] == "medium"
    assert body["correction_history"][0]["new_value"]["confidence"] == "medium"
    assert len(body["correction_history"]) == 1

    with sessions() as db:
        stored = db.get(EvaluationResult, result_id)
        dimensions = json.loads(stored.aesthetic_json)["dimensions"]
        assert dimensions[first_dimension["key"]]["hit_rules"][0]["confidence"] == "medium"
        history = json.loads(stored.correction_history_json)
        assert history[0]["new_value"]["confidence"] == "medium"

    # Same correction key is idempotent and never appends twice.
    replay = client.post(
        f"/api/evaluation-results/{result_id}/correct-node", json=payload
    )
    assert replay.status_code == 200
    assert len(replay.json()["correction_history"]) == 1

    with sessions() as db:
        stored = db.get(EvaluationResult, result_id)
        assert stored is not None
        old_level = stored.level
        new_level = "L5" if old_level != "L5" else "L4"
        apply_node_correction(
            db,
            result=stored,
            payload=CorrectNodeRequest(
                correction_key="auto-corrector-test",
                node_type="final_level",
                node_path="final_level",
                old_value=old_level,
                new_value=new_level,
                evidence=[],
                reason="黄金集高置信度校准",
            ),
            corrector="auto-corrector-v1",
            corrector_confidence=0.91,
            corrector_policy="level-confusion-calibration-v1",
        )
        db.commit()
        history = json.loads(stored.correction_history_json)
        assert history[-1]["corrector"] == "auto-corrector-v1"
        assert history[-1]["corrector_confidence"] == 0.91
        assert history[-1]["corrector_policy"] == "level-confusion-calibration-v1"
    engine.dispose()


def test_correct_bonus_rule_hit_recomputes_with_frozen_context() -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    context = _bonus_context()
    config = context["subcategory_dimensions"]["class_one"]
    first_dimension = config["common_group"]["schema_definition"]["dimensions"][0]
    first_rule = first_dimension["deduction_rules"][0]
    bonus_rule = first_dimension["bonus_rules"][0]
    dimension_output = empty_deduction_output(config)
    dimension_output["dimensions"][first_dimension["key"]]["hit_rules"] = [
        {
            "rule_id": first_rule["rule_id"],
            "confidence": "high",
            "evidence": "主体明显偏移",
        }
    ]
    precheck = _precheck()
    aggregate = recompute_qualified_v3(
        v3_context=context,
        precheck=precheck,
        dimension_output=dimension_output,
    )
    scoring = build_v3_authoritative_scoring(aggregate, precheck=precheck)
    scoring.pop("_dimension_deduction_raw_payload", None)
    scoring["v3_context"] = context

    with sessions() as db:
        row = EvaluationResult(
            asset_id=1,
            job_id=1,
            precheck_json=json.dumps(precheck, ensure_ascii=False),
            aesthetic_json=json.dumps(dimension_output, ensure_ascii=False),
            scoring_json=json.dumps(scoring, ensure_ascii=False),
            correction_history_json="[]",
            raw_response_a='{"immutable":"provider payload"}',
            score=scoring["score"],
            level=scoring["level"],
            confidence=scoring["confidence"],
            needs_review=False,
            model_id="fake",
            prompt_a_version="a",
            rubric_version="r",
            engine_version="e",
        )
        db.add(row)
        db.commit()
        result_id = row.id
        original_score = row.score

    def db_dependency():
        db = sessions()
        try:
            yield db
        finally:
            db.close()

    def reviewer():
        return SimpleNamespace(username="reviewer", display_name="审核员")

    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(build_node_correction_router(reviewer))
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = db_dependency
    client = TestClient(app)
    new_hit = {
        "rule_id": bonus_rule["rule_id"],
        "confidence": "high",
        "evidence": "主体、留白与层级关系清晰",
    }
    payload = {
        "correction_key": "bonus-add-1",
        "node_type": "dimension_rule",
        "node_path": (
            f"dimension.{first_dimension['key']}.hit_bonus_rules."
            f"{bonus_rule['rule_id']}"
        ),
        "old_value": None,
        "new_value": new_hit,
        "evidence": [],
        "reason": "人工确认正向规则命中",
    }

    response = client.post(
        f"/api/evaluation-results/{result_id}/correct-node", json=payload
    )
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    body = response.json()
    assert body["score"] > original_score
    assert body["aesthetic"]["dimensions"][first_dimension["key"]][
        "hit_bonus_rules"
    ] == [new_hit]
    assert body["correction_history"][-1]["node_path"].endswith(
        "hit_bonus_rules.composition_clear"
    )

    replay = client.post(
        f"/api/evaluation-results/{result_id}/correct-node", json=payload
    )
    assert replay.status_code == 200
    assert len(replay.json()["correction_history"]) == 1

    stale = client.post(
        f"/api/evaluation-results/{result_id}/correct-node",
        json={**payload, "correction_key": "bonus-stale", "old_value": None},
    )
    assert stale.status_code == 409
    assert stale.json()["detail"]["code"] == "node_value_conflict"

    unknown = client.post(
        f"/api/evaluation-results/{result_id}/correct-node",
        json={
            **payload,
            "correction_key": "bonus-unknown",
            "node_path": (
                f"dimension.{first_dimension['key']}.hit_bonus_rules.unknown_bonus"
            ),
            "old_value": None,
            "new_value": {**new_hit, "rule_id": "unknown_bonus"},
        },
    )
    assert unknown.status_code == 400
    assert unknown.json()["detail"]["code"] == "rule_unknown"

    with sessions() as db:
        stored = db.get(EvaluationResult, result_id)
        assert stored is not None
        assert stored.raw_response_a == '{"immutable":"provider payload"}'
        history = json.loads(stored.correction_history_json)
        assert len(history) == 1
    engine.dispose()


def test_correct_precheck_redline_track_and_final_level_replays_full_path() -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    context = _context()
    dimension_output = empty_deduction_output(
        context["subcategory_dimensions"]["class_one"]
    )
    precheck = _precheck()
    aggregate = recompute_qualified_v3(
        v3_context=context,
        precheck=precheck,
        dimension_output=dimension_output,
    )
    scoring = build_v3_authoritative_scoring(aggregate, precheck=precheck)
    scoring.pop("_dimension_deduction_raw_payload", None)
    scoring["v3_context"] = context

    with sessions() as db:
        row = EvaluationResult(
            asset_id=1,
            job_id=1,
            precheck_json=json.dumps(precheck, ensure_ascii=False),
            aesthetic_json=json.dumps(dimension_output, ensure_ascii=False),
            scoring_json=json.dumps(scoring, ensure_ascii=False),
            correction_history_json="[]",
            raw_response_a="{}",
            score=scoring["score"],
            level=scoring["level"],
            confidence=scoring["confidence"],
            needs_review=False,
            model_id="fake",
            prompt_a_version="a",
            rubric_version="r",
            engine_version="e",
        )
        db.add(row)
        db.commit()
        result_id = row.id
        original_score = row.score

    def db_dependency():
        db = sessions()
        try:
            yield db
        finally:
            db.close()

    def reviewer():
        return SimpleNamespace(username="reviewer", display_name="审核员")

    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(build_node_correction_router(reviewer))
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = db_dependency
    client = TestClient(app)

    def correct(
        key: str,
        node_type: str,
        node_path: str,
        old_value: object,
        new_value: object,
    ) -> dict:
        response = client.post(
            f"/api/evaluation-results/{result_id}/correct-node",
            json={
                "correction_key": key,
                "node_type": node_type,
                "node_path": node_path,
                "old_value": old_value,
                "new_value": new_value,
                "evidence": [],
                "reason": "前端节点纠偏集成测试",
            },
        )
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        return response.json()

    media = correct(
        "ui-media",
        "precheck_field",
        "precheck.production_fields.trait",
        "实景照片",
        "AI图",
    )
    assert media["score"] == original_score
    assert media["scoring"]["media_key"] is None
    assert media["scoring"]["media_penalty_enabled"] is False

    redline = correct(
        "ui-redline-on",
        "redline",
        "redline.production_fields.reason",
        [],
        ["是截图"],
    )
    assert redline["score"] == 20
    assert redline["level"] == "L5"
    assert redline["scoring"]["hit_rules"] == ["screenshot"]

    restored = correct(
        "ui-redline-off",
        "redline",
        "redline.production_fields.reason",
        ["是截图"],
        [],
    )
    assert restored["score"] == media["score"]

    track = correct(
        "ui-track",
        "track",
        "track_key",
        "class_one",
        "class_three",
    )
    assert track["score"] == 70
    assert track["level"] == "L3"
    assert track["scoring"]["track_key"] == "class_three"
    assert set(track["aesthetic"]["dimensions"]) == {
        "subject_focus",
        "mood_atmosphere",
        "composition_lighting",
        "reference_value",
        "visual_impact",
    }

    final = correct(
        "ui-final-level",
        "final_level",
        "final_level",
        "L3",
        "L4",
    )
    assert final["score"] == track["score"]
    assert final["level"] == "L4"
    assert final["correction"]["downstream_recomputed"] is False
    assert final["correction"]["corrector"] == "审核员"
    assert len(final["correction_history"]) == 5
    engine.dispose()


def test_call_a_business_fields_persist_without_changing_score() -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    result_id = _result_for_call_a(sessions)

    corrections = [
        ("title", "现代住宅", "光影住宅"),
        ("seotitle", "现代住宅空间设计参考", "光影住宅室内设计灵感"),
        ("style", "现代简约", "侘寂风"),
        ("cons", "局部层次略显单薄", "入口转折略显生硬"),
        ("design", "以自然光和材质层次组织空间", "以连续拱券组织空间序列"),
        ("category", "居住空间,大平层", "酒店民宿,度假酒店"),
        ("tags", ["住宅", "客厅", "木饰面", "自然光"], ["酒店", "大堂", "石材", "灯光"]),
        ("trait", "实景照片", "3D数字效果图"),
        ("reason", [], ["是截图"]),
        ("image_defects", "", "有水印"),
    ]
    with sessions() as db:
        row = db.get(EvaluationResult, result_id)
        assert row is not None
        raw_before = row.raw_response_a
        for index, (field, old_value, new_value) in enumerate(corrections, 1):
            response = apply_node_correction(
                db,
                result=row,
                payload=CorrectNodeRequest(
                    correction_key=f"call-a-field-{index}",
                    node_type="call_a_field",
                    node_path=f"call_a.{field}",
                    old_value=old_value,
                    new_value=new_value,
                    evidence=[],
                    reason=f"人工核对{field}",
                ),
                corrector="审核员",
            )
            assert response["score"] == 70
            assert response["level"] == "L2"

        db.commit()
        production = json.loads(row.precheck_json)["production_fields"]
        assert production["title"] == "光影住宅"
        assert production["seotitle"] == "光影住宅室内设计灵感"
        assert production["style"] == "侘寂风"
        assert production["cons"] == "入口转折略显生硬"
        assert production["design"] == "以连续拱券组织空间序列"
        assert production["category"] == "酒店民宿,度假酒店"
        assert production["tags"] == ["酒店", "大堂", "石材", "灯光"]
        assert production["trait"] == "3D数字效果图"
        assert production["reason"] == ["是截图"]
        assert production["image_defects"] == "有水印"
        assert row.raw_response_a == raw_before
        history = json.loads(row.correction_history_json)
        assert [item["node_path"] for item in history] == [
            "call_a.title",
            "call_a.seotitle",
            "call_a.style",
            "call_a.cons",
            "call_a.design",
            "call_a.category",
            "call_a.tags",
            "call_a.trait",
            "call_a.reason",
            "call_a.image_defects",
        ]
        assert history[-1]["node_type"] == "call_a_field"
        assert history[-1]["old_value"] == ""
        assert history[-1]["new_value"] == "有水印"
        assert history[-1]["corrector"] == "审核员"
        assert history[-1]["reason"] == "人工核对image_defects"
        assert history[-1]["corrected_at"].endswith("Z")
        assert history[-1]["downstream_recomputed"] is False
    engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("score", "expected_grade"),
    [(100, "L1"), (81, "L1"), (80, "L2"), (61, "L2"), (60, "L3"),
     (41, "L3"), (40, "L4"), (21, "L4"), (20, "L5"), (0, "L5")],
)
def test_call_a_score_recomputes_grade(score: int, expected_grade: str) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    result_id = _result_for_call_a(sessions)
    with sessions() as db:
        row = db.get(EvaluationResult, result_id)
        assert row is not None
        response = apply_node_correction(
            db,
            result=row,
            payload=CorrectNodeRequest(
                correction_key=f"call-a-score-{score}",
                node_type="call_a_field",
                node_path="call_a.score",
                old_value=70,
                new_value=score,
                evidence=[],
                reason="人工校准综合评分",
            ),
            corrector="审核员",
        )
        db.commit()
        assert response["score"] == score
        assert response["level"] == expected_grade
        assert row.score == score
        assert row.level == expected_grade
        assert json.loads(row.precheck_json)["production_fields"]["score"] == score
        scoring = json.loads(row.scoring_json)
        assert scoring["score"] == score
        assert scoring["level"] == expected_grade
        assert scoring["manual_call_a_score"] == score
        history = json.loads(row.correction_history_json)
        assert history[-1]["downstream_recomputed"] is True
    engine.dispose()


def test_call_a_grade_is_manual_override_and_keeps_score() -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    result_id = _result_for_call_a(sessions)
    with sessions() as db:
        row = db.get(EvaluationResult, result_id)
        assert row is not None
        response = apply_node_correction(
            db,
            result=row,
            payload=CorrectNodeRequest(
                correction_key="call-a-grade-manual",
                node_type="call_a_field",
                node_path="call_a.grade",
                old_value="L2",
                new_value="L4",
                evidence=[],
                reason="人工等级结论优先",
            ),
            corrector="审核员",
        )
        db.commit()
        assert response["score"] == 70
        assert response["level"] == "L4"
        assert row.score == 70
        assert row.level == "L4"
        scoring = json.loads(row.scoring_json)
        assert scoring["manual_call_a_grade"] == "L4"
        history = json.loads(row.correction_history_json)
        assert history[-1]["downstream_recomputed"] is False
    engine.dispose()


def test_call_a_manual_score_and_grade_survive_later_node_replay() -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    result_id = _result_for_call_a(sessions)
    with sessions() as db:
        row = db.get(EvaluationResult, result_id)
        assert row is not None

        def correct(key: str, node_type: str, node_path: str, old: object, new: object):
            return apply_node_correction(
                db,
                result=row,
                payload=CorrectNodeRequest(
                    correction_key=key,
                    node_type=node_type,
                    node_path=node_path,
                    old_value=old,
                    new_value=new,
                    evidence=[],
                    reason="连续纠偏一致性测试",
                ),
                corrector="审核员",
            )

        correct("manual-score", "call_a_field", "call_a.score", 70, 82)
        replay_after_score = correct(
            "replay-after-score",
            "precheck_field",
            "precheck.production_fields.trait",
            "实景照片",
            "AI图",
        )
        assert replay_after_score["score"] == 82
        assert replay_after_score["level"] == "L1"

        correct("manual-grade", "call_a_field", "call_a.grade", "L1", "L4")
        replay_after_grade = correct(
            "replay-after-grade",
            "precheck_field",
            "precheck.production_fields.trait",
            "AI图",
            "其它",
        )
        assert replay_after_grade["score"] == 82
        assert replay_after_grade["level"] == "L4"
        scoring = json.loads(row.scoring_json)
        assert scoring["manual_call_a_score"] == 82
        assert scoring["manual_call_a_grade"] == "L4"
    engine.dispose()


def test_call_a_missing_legacy_field_fails_safely() -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    result_id = _result_for_call_a(sessions)
    with sessions() as db:
        row = db.get(EvaluationResult, result_id)
        assert row is not None
        precheck = json.loads(row.precheck_json)
        del precheck["production_fields"]["design"]
        row.precheck_json = json.dumps(precheck, ensure_ascii=False)
        with pytest.raises(HTTPException) as caught:
            apply_node_correction(
                db,
                result=row,
                payload=CorrectNodeRequest(
                    node_type="call_a_field",
                    node_path="call_a.design",
                    old_value=None,
                    new_value="补录设计理念",
                    evidence=[],
                    reason="旧记录补录尝试",
                ),
                corrector="审核员",
            )
        assert caught.value.status_code == 409
        assert caught.value.detail["code"] == "call_a_field_missing"
    engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_offhost_backup.py =====
"""Encrypted off-host copies: real streams, damaged transfers, and safe retention."""
import hashlib
import importlib.util
import io
import json
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tarfile
import tempfile
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

SOURCE = Path(__file__).parents[2] / "deploy/server/offhost_backup.py"
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("offhost_backup", SOURCE)
vault = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(vault)


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def keys(tmp_path_factory):
    if not shutil.which("gpg"):
        pytest.skip("GnuPG is needed for an actual encrypt/decrypt verification")
    # GnuPG agent uses Unix sockets; pytest's macOS path can exceed sun_path.
    temporary = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="offhost-gpg-", dir="/tmp")
    home = Path(temporary.name)
    home.chmod(0o700)
    result = subprocess.run(["gpg", "--homedir", str(home), "--batch", "--pinentry-mode", "loopback",
                    "--passphrase", "", "--quick-generate-key", "Offhost fixture", "rsa2048", "encr", "0"],
                   capture_output=True)
    assert result.returncode == 0, result.stderr.decode()
    listing = subprocess.check_output(["gpg", "--homedir", str(home), "--with-colons", "--list-keys"], text=True)
    fingerprint = next(line.split(":")[9] for line in listing.splitlines() if line.startswith("fpr:"))
    yield home, fingerprint
    subprocess.run(["gpgconf", "--homedir", str(home), "--kill", "gpg-agent"], capture_output=True)
    temporary.cleanup()


SQL_BY_FORMAT = {"label-system-server-backup-v1": "mysql.sql", "label-system-server-backup-v2": "mysql.sql.zst"}


def fixture(tmp_path, keys, day=1, *, stamp=None, prefix="predeploy-fixture",
            backup_format="label-system-server-backup-v1"):
    home, fingerprint = keys
    source = tmp_path / "source"
    source.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    backup = source / (prefix + "-" + (stamp or f"202609{day:02d}T000000Z"))
    backup.mkdir()
    sql = SQL_BY_FORMAT[backup_format]
    # v2 的 SQL 是 zstd 压缩字节；这里只校验字节与哈希，不需要真解压
    (backup / sql).write_bytes((b"(\xb5/\xfd" if sql.endswith(".zst") else b"") + b"CREATE TABLE sample(id INT);\n" * 1000)
    with tarfile.open(backup / "platform-data.tar.gz", "w:gz") as t:
        for name, payload in [("assets/a.txt", b"fixture material"), ("secrets/master.key", b"A" * 43 + b"=")]:
            info = tarfile.TarInfo(name)
            info.size, info.mode = len(payload), 0o600
            t.addfile(info, io.BytesIO(payload))
    files = [{"path": name, "size": (backup / name).stat().st_size,
              "sha256": hashlib.sha256((backup / name).read_bytes()).hexdigest()}
             for name in (sql, "platform-data.tar.gz")]
    (backup / "manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps({"format": backup_format,
                                                     "source_schema": "fixture", "files": files}))
    cfg = {"environment": "production", "schema": "fixture", "origin": "fixture-source",
           "source_roots": [str(source)], "gpg_home": str(home), "recipient": fingerprint,
           "vault": str(tmp_path / "vault"), "mountpoint": str(tmp_path), "require_mount": False,
           "reserve_bytes": 0, "max_backup_bytes": 10**7,
           "retention": {"days": 7, "per_day": 1, "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai",
                         "current_day": "keep_all", "cleanup_trigger": "next_daily_backup_success"},
           "allowed_origins": ["fixture-source"], "retention_enabled": True,
           "source_ready": False}
    config = tmp_path / "config.json"
    config.write_text(json.dumps(cfg))
    config.chmod(0o600)
    return backup, cfg, config


def receive(cfg, config, backup):
    return vault.receive(cfg, [sys.executable, str(SOURCE), "export", "--config", str(config), "--id", backup.name])


@pytest.mark.parametrize('size', [8 * 1024**3 - 1, 8 * 1024**3, 42615119461, 128 * 1024**3])
def test_large_production_sql_header_reaches_stream_without_ustar_overflow(size):
    class HeaderWritten(Exception):
        pass
    class StopBeforePayload:
        def read(self, n):
            raise HeaderWritten
    stream = io.BytesIO()
    info = tarfile.TarInfo('mysql.sql'); info.size = size
    # Execute the writer used by export; stop at its first data read so no
    # multi-GB allocation or disk fixture is needed. Old ustar fails sooner.
    with pytest.raises(HeaderWritten):
        with vault.backup_bundle(stream) as bundle:
            bundle.addfile(info, StopBeforePayload())
    with tarfile.open(fileobj=io.BytesIO(stream.getvalue()), mode='r|') as bundle:
        decoded = bundle.next()
        assert decoded.name == 'mysql.sql' and decoded.size == size


def test_real_encryption_roundtrip_checks_all_plaintext_without_extraction(tmp_path, keys):
    backup, cfg, config = fixture(tmp_path, keys)
    result = receive(cfg, config, backup)
    folder = Path(cfg["vault"]) / "completed" / backup.name
    encrypted = folder / "backup.tar.gpg"
    assert result["status"] == "ciphertext_verified"
    assert encrypted.read_bytes() != (backup / "mysql.sql").read_bytes()
    p = subprocess.Popen(["gpg", "--homedir", str(keys[0]), "--batch", "--decrypt", str(encrypted)],
                         stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE)
    checked = vault.verify_plain_stream(p.stdout, result)
    p.stdout.close()
    assert p.wait() == 0
    assert checked["files_verified"] == 3
    assert not (folder / "mysql.sql").exists()
    assert encrypted.stat().st_mode & 0o077 == 0
    assert receive(cfg, config, backup)["status"] == "already_present"


def test_failed_export_and_truncated_transfer_never_publish_or_prune(tmp_path, keys):
    backup, cfg, config = fixture(tmp_path, keys)
    receive(cfg, config, backup)
    before = (Path(cfg["vault"]) / "completed" / backup.name / "backup.tar.gpg").read_bytes()
    bad, _, _ = fixture(tmp_path, keys, 2)
    (bad / "mysql.sql").write_bytes(b"corrupt same identity")
    with pytest.raises((ValueError, RuntimeError)):
        receive(cfg, config, bad)
    assert not (Path(cfg["vault"]) / "completed" / bad.name).exists()
    assert (Path(cfg["vault"]) / "completed" / backup.name / "backup.tar.gpg").read_bytes() == before


def test_eighth_day_keeps_seven_and_corruption_blocks_deletion(tmp_path, keys):
    reference = datetime(2026, 9, 8, 12, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    for day in range(1, 9):
        backup, cfg, config = fixture(tmp_path, keys, day)
        receive(cfg, config, backup)
    completed = Path(cfg["vault"]) / "completed"
    newest = completed / backup.name / "backup.tar.gpg"
    original = newest.read_bytes()
    newest.write_bytes(bytes([original[0] ^ 1]) + original[1:])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="checksum"):
        vault.retain(cfg, apply=True, reference_time=reference)
    assert len(list(completed.iterdir())) == 8
    newest.write_bytes(original)
    result = vault.retain(cfg, apply=True, reference_time=reference)
    assert len(result["deleted"]) == 1
    assert len(list(completed.iterdir())) == 7
    assert sorted(p.name for p in completed.iterdir())[0].endswith("20260902T000000Z")


def test_pinned_incident_copy_is_preserved_and_failed_stage_not_counted(tmp_path, keys):
    for day in range(1, 9):
        backup, cfg, config = fixture(tmp_path, keys, day)
        receive(cfg, config, backup)
    first = sorted((Path(cfg["vault"]) / "completed").iterdir())[0]
    (first / "PINNED").write_text("incident recovery anchor")
    (Path(cfg["vault"]) / "incoming" / "failed-transfer").mkdir()
    reference = datetime(2026, 9, 8, 12, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    assert vault.retain(cfg, apply=True, reference_time=reference)["deleted"] == []
    assert len(list((Path(cfg["vault"]) / "completed").iterdir())) == 8


def test_daily_window_uses_shanghai_dates_and_not_seven_most_recent_backup_days():
    ids = ["daily-fixture-" + stamp for stamp in [
        "20260831T155959Z", "20260831T160000Z", "20260901T010000Z",
        "20260906T150000Z", "20260906T160000Z"]]
    result = vault.daily_selection(ids, reference_time=datetime(2026, 9, 7, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc))
    assert result["window_start"] == "2026-09-01"
    assert result["window_end"] == "2026-09-07"
    assert result["latest_by_day"] == {
        "2026-09-01": ids[2], "2026-09-06": ids[3], "2026-09-07": ids[4]}


def test_same_day_keeps_latest_source_backup_even_if_older_is_copied_later(tmp_path, keys):
    # UTC 15:59 is still the same Shanghai day; UTC 16:00 begins the next day.
    stamps = ["20260906T155959Z", "20260906T000000Z", "20260906T160000Z"]
    names = []
    for stamp in stamps:
        backup, cfg, config = fixture(tmp_path, keys, stamp=stamp)
        receive(cfg, config, backup)
        names.append(backup.name)
    result = vault.retain(cfg, apply=True, reference_time=datetime(2026, 9, 7, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc))
    assert result["keep"] == [names[0], names[2]]
    assert result["deleted"] == [names[1]]


def test_no_recent_success_never_ages_away_the_last_available_copies(tmp_path, keys):
    for day in [1, 2]:
        backup, cfg, config = fixture(tmp_path, keys, day)
        receive(cfg, config, backup)
    result = vault.retain(cfg, apply=True, reference_time=datetime(2026, 9, 20, tzinfo=timezone.utc))
    assert result["status"] == "blocked_no_recent_backup"
    assert result["delete"] == result["deleted"] == []
    assert len(result["keep"]) == 2


@pytest.mark.parametrize("policy", [None, {"days": 7, "per_day": True, "timezone": "Asia/Shanghai"},
                                   {"days": 7, "per_day": 1, "timezone": "UTC"}])
def test_policy_must_be_explicit_and_exact(tmp_path, keys, policy):
    _, cfg, config = fixture(tmp_path, keys)
    cfg["retention"] = None if policy is None else {**cfg["retention"], **policy}
    config.write_text(json.dumps(cfg))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="retention"):
        vault.load_config(config)


def source_command(tmp_path, config):
    source = tmp_path / "catalog-source.py"
    source.write_text(
        "import runpy,sys\n"
        "sys.argv = [" + repr(str(SOURCE)) + ", sys.argv[1], '--config', " + repr(str(config)) +
        "] + (['--id', sys.argv[2]] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else [])\n"
        "runpy.run_path(" + repr(str(SOURCE)) + ", run_name='__main__')\n")
    return [sys.executable, str(source)]


def test_sync_acknowledges_verified_bytes_without_remote_delete_permission(tmp_path, keys):
    backup, cfg, config = fixture(tmp_path, keys, stamp="20260907T000000Z", prefix="daily")
    cfg.update(source_retention_audit=str(tmp_path / "source-audit"),
               source_ready=True, confirm_source_copies=True,
               source_command=source_command(tmp_path, config))
    config.write_text(json.dumps(cfg))
    vault.sync(cfg, reference_time=datetime(2026, 9, 7, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc))
    saved = json.loads((Path(cfg['vault']) / 'completed' / backup.name / 'receipt.json').read_text())
    proof = json.loads((tmp_path / 'source-audit' / 'confirmations' / (backup.name + '.json')).read_text())
    assert proof['ciphertext_sha256'] == saved['ciphertext_sha256']
    assert proof['descriptor_sha256'] == hashlib.sha256(vault.canonical(saved['backup'])).hexdigest()
    assert backup.exists()
    # The forced command cannot turn a receiver confirmation into source deletion.
    import os
    for forbidden in ('source-retain --apply', 'source-queue ' + backup.name,
                      'confirm ' + backup.name + ' --apply'):
        result = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(SOURCE), 'serve', '--config', str(config)],
                                env={**os.environ, 'SSH_ORIGINAL_COMMAND': forbidden}, capture_output=True)
        assert result.returncode != 0
    assert backup.exists()


def test_sync_never_acknowledges_a_damaged_existing_ciphertext(tmp_path, keys):
    backup, cfg, config = fixture(tmp_path, keys, stamp="20260907T000000Z", prefix="daily")
    receive(cfg, config, backup)
    cfg.update(source_retention_audit=str(tmp_path / 'source-audit'), source_ready=True,
               confirm_source_copies=True, source_command=source_command(tmp_path, config))
    config.write_text(json.dumps(cfg))
    (Path(cfg['vault']) / 'completed' / backup.name / 'backup.tar.gpg').write_bytes(b'corrupted')
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        vault.sync(cfg, reference_time=datetime(2026, 9, 7, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc))
    assert not (tmp_path / 'source-audit' / 'confirmations').exists()
    assert backup.exists()


def test_sync_keeps_all_today_but_fetches_only_the_latest_for_prior_dates(tmp_path, keys):
    _, cfg, config = fixture(tmp_path, keys, day=1)
    for stamp in ["20260906T000000Z", "20260906T155959Z", "20260906T160000Z", "20260907T000000Z"]:
        backup, _, _ = fixture(tmp_path, keys, stamp=stamp)
    cfg.update(source_ready=True, source_command=source_command(tmp_path, config))
    result = vault.sync(cfg, reference_time=datetime(2026, 9, 7, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc))
    expected = {"predeploy-fixture-" + stamp for stamp in [
        "20260901T000000Z", "20260906T155959Z", "20260906T160000Z", "20260907T000000Z"]}
    assert set(result["copied"]) == expected
    assert result["retention_result"] is None
    assert {p.name for p in (Path(cfg["vault"]) / "completed").iterdir()} == expected
    # The exporter may later have only an older historical file. Do not copy it
    # again every tick when the vault already holds that day's newer success.
    shutil.rmtree(tmp_path / "source" / "predeploy-fixture-20260906T155959Z")
    again = vault.sync(cfg, reference_time=datetime(2026, 9, 7, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc))
    assert again["copied"] == []
    assert {p.name for p in (Path(cfg["vault"]) / "completed").iterdir()} == expected


def test_current_day_is_not_compacted_even_by_explicit_retention(tmp_path, keys):
    names = []
    for stamp in ["20260906T000000Z", "20260906T010000Z", "20260906T020000Z"]:
        backup, cfg, config = fixture(tmp_path, keys, stamp=stamp)
        receive(cfg, config, backup)
        names.append(backup.name)
    result = vault.retain(cfg, apply=True, reference_time=datetime(2026, 9, 6, 3, tzinfo=timezone.utc))
    assert result["keep"] == result["current_day_copies"] == names
    assert result["deleted"] == []


def test_only_the_next_successful_daily_backup_compacts_prior_days(tmp_path, keys):
    names = []
    for stamp in ["20260906T000000Z", "20260906T010000Z"]:
        backup, cfg, config = fixture(tmp_path, keys, stamp=stamp)
        receive(cfg, config, backup)
        names.append(backup.name)
    cfg.update(source_ready=True, source_command=source_command(tmp_path, config))
    reference = datetime(2026, 9, 7, 3, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    polled = vault.sync(cfg, reference_time=reference)
    completed = Path(cfg["vault"]) / "completed"
    assert polled["retention_result"] is None
    assert {p.name for p in completed.iterdir()} == set(names)
    deploy_backup, _, _ = fixture(tmp_path, keys, stamp="20260907T000000Z")
    deployed = vault.sync(cfg, reference_time=reference)
    assert deployed["retention_result"] is None
    assert (completed / names[0]).exists()
    daily, _, _ = fixture(tmp_path, keys, stamp="20260907T010000Z", prefix="daily")
    original = (daily / "mysql.sql").read_bytes()
    (daily / "mysql.sql").write_bytes(b"failed daily backup")
    with pytest.raises((ValueError, RuntimeError, subprocess.CalledProcessError)):
        vault.sync(cfg, reference_time=reference)
    assert (completed / names[0]).exists()
    (daily / "mysql.sql").write_bytes(original)
    finished = vault.sync(cfg, reference_time=reference)
    assert finished["retention_trigger_backup"] == daily.name
    assert finished["retention_result"]["deleted"] == [names[0]]
    assert {p.name for p in completed.iterdir()} == {names[1], deploy_backup.name, daily.name}
    again = vault.sync(cfg, reference_time=reference)
    assert again["copied"] == [] and again["retention_result"] is None


def test_old_count_policy_is_not_silently_accepted(tmp_path, keys):
    _, cfg, config = fixture(tmp_path, keys)
    cfg["keep"] = 3
    config.write_text(json.dumps(cfg))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="retention"):
        vault.load_config(config)


def test_failed_newest_same_day_does_not_remove_previous_success(tmp_path, keys):
    old, cfg, config = fixture(tmp_path, keys, stamp="20260906T000000Z")
    receive(cfg, config, old)
    new, _, _ = fixture(tmp_path, keys, stamp="20260906T155959Z")
    (new / "mysql.sql").write_bytes(b"failed backup")
    with pytest.raises((ValueError, RuntimeError)):
        receive(cfg, config, new)
    result = vault.retain(cfg, apply=True, reference_time=datetime(2026, 9, 7, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc))
    assert result["keep"] == [old.name]
    assert result["deleted"] == []


@pytest.mark.parametrize("damage", ["schema", "traversal", "symlink", "duplicate", "origin"])
def test_untrusted_backup_contract_is_rejected(tmp_path, keys, damage):
    backup, cfg, config = fixture(tmp_path, keys)
    m = json.loads((backup / "manifest.json").read_text())
    if damage == "schema":
        m["source_schema"] = "other_environment"
    elif damage == "traversal":
        m["files"][0]["path"] = "../mysql.sql"
    elif damage == "duplicate":
        m["files"].append(m["files"][0])
    elif damage == "symlink":
        (backup / "mysql.sql").unlink()
        (backup / "mysql.sql").symlink_to(config)
    elif damage == "origin":
        cfg["allowed_origins"] = ["different-source"]
    (backup / "manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps(m))
    with pytest.raises((ValueError, RuntimeError)):
        receive(cfg, config, backup)
    assert not (Path(cfg["vault"]) / "completed" / backup.name).exists()


def test_mount_missing_or_space_shortfall_preserves_prior_copies(tmp_path, keys, monkeypatch):
    backup, cfg, config = fixture(tmp_path, keys)
    cfg["require_mount"] = True
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="mount"):
        receive(cfg, config, backup)
    cfg["require_mount"] = False
    cfg["reserve_bytes"] = 2**63
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="space"):
        receive(cfg, config, backup)
    assert not (Path(cfg["vault"]) / "completed" / backup.name).exists()


def test_wrong_recipient_or_unconfigured_source_fails_closed(tmp_path, keys):
    backup, cfg, config = fixture(tmp_path, keys)
    cfg["recipient"] = "0" * 40
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="recipient"):
        receive(cfg, config, backup)
    cfg["recipient"] = keys[1]
    assert vault.sync(cfg)["status"] == "blocked_source_not_configured"


def test_interrupted_receiver_leaves_only_incoming(tmp_path, keys):
    backup, cfg, config = fixture(tmp_path, keys)
    metadata = vault.describe(backup, cfg)
    partial = tmp_path / "partial.py"
    partial.write_text("import sys,json\nsys.stdout.buffer.write(" + repr(json.dumps(metadata).encode() + b"\n" + b"x" * 1024) + ")\nsys.exit(7)\n")
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="source"):
        vault.receive(cfg, [sys.executable, str(partial)])
    assert not (Path(cfg["vault"]) / "completed" / backup.name).exists()


def test_disabled_retention_and_symlink_vault_refuse_mutation(tmp_path, keys):
    backup, cfg, config = fixture(tmp_path, keys)
    cfg["retention_enabled"] = False
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="retention"):
        vault.retain(cfg, apply=True)
    target = tmp_path / "unrelated"
    target.mkdir()
    Path(cfg["vault"]).symlink_to(target)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="symlink"):
        receive(cfg, config, backup)


def test_successful_process_cannot_pass_a_plaintext_file_as_encrypted(tmp_path, keys):
    backup, cfg, config = fixture(tmp_path, keys)
    metadata = vault.describe(backup, cfg)
    fake = tmp_path / "fake.py"
    fake.write_text("import sys\nsys.stdout.buffer.write(" + repr(json.dumps(metadata).encode() + b"\n" + b"not encrypted" * 100) + ")\n")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="encrypted"):
        vault.receive(cfg, [sys.executable, str(fake)])
    assert not (Path(cfg["vault"]) / "completed" / backup.name).exists()


def test_same_backup_id_with_different_source_bytes_preserves_original(tmp_path, keys):
    backup, cfg, config = fixture(tmp_path, keys)
    receive(cfg, config, backup)
    folder = Path(cfg["vault"]) / "completed" / backup.name
    old = (folder / "backup.tar.gpg").read_bytes()
    (backup / "mysql.sql").write_bytes(b"different valid source")
    manifest = json.loads((backup / "manifest.json").read_text())
    entry = next(e for e in manifest["files"] if e["path"] == "mysql.sql")
    entry.update(size=(backup / "mysql.sql").stat().st_size, sha256=vault.digest(backup / "mysql.sql"))
    (backup / "manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps(manifest))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="different contents"):
        receive(cfg, config, backup)
    assert (folder / "backup.tar.gpg").read_bytes() == old


def test_corrupted_encrypted_copy_is_not_accepted_by_decrypt_verification(tmp_path, keys):
    backup, cfg, config = fixture(tmp_path, keys)
    result = receive(cfg, config, backup)
    folder = Path(cfg["vault"]) / "completed" / backup.name
    encrypted = folder / "backup.tar.gpg"
    encrypted.write_bytes(encrypted.read_bytes()[:-16])
    check = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(SOURCE), "verify", "--cipher", str(encrypted),
                            "--receipt", str(folder / "receipt.json"), "--gpg-home", str(keys[0])], capture_output=True)
    assert check.returncode != 0
    assert b"plaintext_checksums_verified" not in check.stdout


def test_cli_receive_parses_source_arguments_only_after_separator(tmp_path, keys):
    backup, cfg, config = fixture(tmp_path, keys)
    r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(SOURCE), "receive", "--config", str(config), "--",
                        sys.executable, str(SOURCE), "export", "--config", str(config), "--id", backup.name], capture_output=True)
    assert r.returncode == 0, r.stderr.decode()
    assert json.loads(r.stdout)["backup"]["id"] == backup.name


def confirm_fixture_sources(cfg):
    for folder in vault.source_folders(cfg):
        vault.confirm_source(cfg, folder.name, {
            'descriptor_sha256': hashlib.sha256(vault.canonical(vault.describe(folder, cfg))).hexdigest(),
            'ciphertext_sha256': 'a' * 64, 'ciphertext_bytes': 100,
        })


def test_source_seven_days_compacts_only_after_next_daily_success(tmp_path, keys):
    reference = datetime(2026, 9, 8, 12, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    backups = []
    for day in range(1, 9):
        p, cfg, _ = fixture(tmp_path, keys, day)
        backups.append(p)
    earlier, _, _ = fixture(tmp_path, keys, stamp='20260908T010000Z', prefix='predeploy-second')
    trigger, _, _ = fixture(tmp_path, keys, stamp='20260908T020000Z', prefix='daily')
    incomplete = Path(cfg['source_roots'][0]) / 'predeploy-interrupted-20260901T010000Z'
    incomplete.mkdir(); (incomplete / 'mysql.raw.sql').write_text('preserve incident')
    cfg.update(source_retention_enabled=True, source_retention_audit=str(tmp_path / 'audit'),
               pinned_source_ids=[backups[0].name])
    confirm_fixture_sources(cfg)
    planned = vault.retain_source(cfg, trigger.name, reference_time=reference)
    assert planned['delete'] == []
    assert {backups[-1].name, earlier.name, trigger.name} <= set(planned['keep'])
    # The next day has a new daily success; yesterday's latest survives, the
    # earlier same-day versions are now eligible, not before that boundary.
    next_daily, _, _ = fixture(tmp_path, keys, stamp='20260909T020000Z', prefix='daily')
    confirm_fixture_sources(cfg)
    result = vault.retain_source(cfg, next_daily.name, apply=True,
                                reference_time=datetime(2026, 9, 9, 12, tzinfo=timezone.utc))
    assert set(result['deleted']) == {backups[1].name, backups[-1].name, earlier.name}
    assert backups[0].exists() and trigger.exists() and next_daily.exists()
    assert (incomplete / 'mysql.raw.sql').read_text() == 'preserve incident'


def test_source_cleanup_corruption_and_disabled_policy_preserve_every_copy(tmp_path, keys):
    old, cfg, _ = fixture(tmp_path, keys, day=1)
    newest, _, _ = fixture(tmp_path, keys, stamp='20260909T020000Z', prefix='daily')
    reference = datetime(2026, 9, 9, 12, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='not enabled'):
        vault.retain_source(cfg, newest.name, apply=True, reference_time=reference)
    cfg.update(source_retention_enabled=True, source_retention_audit=str(tmp_path / 'audit'))
    confirm_fixture_sources(cfg)
    sql = newest / 'mysql.sql'; data = sql.read_bytes(); sql.write_bytes(b'X' + data[1:])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='checksum'):
        vault.retain_source(cfg, newest.name, apply=True, reference_time=reference)
    assert old.exists() and not list((tmp_path / 'audit').glob('*.json'))


def test_source_cleanup_needs_current_day_daily_trigger_and_excludes_export(tmp_path, keys):
    old, cfg, _ = fixture(tmp_path, keys, day=1)
    trigger, _, _ = fixture(tmp_path, keys, stamp='20260909T020000Z', prefix='daily')
    reference = datetime(2026, 9, 9, 12, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    for invalid in (old.name, 'daily-20260908T020000Z', 'daily-20260909T040000Z'):
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='trigger'):
            vault.retain_source(cfg, invalid, reference_time=reference)
    with vault.source_lock(cfg):
        with pytest.raises(BlockingIOError):
            vault.retain_source(cfg, trigger.name, reference_time=reference)
    assert old.exists()


def test_restricted_export_account_cannot_request_cleanup(tmp_path, keys):
    _, _, config = fixture(tmp_path, keys)
    import os
    result = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(SOURCE), 'serve', '--config', str(config)],
                            env={**os.environ, 'SSH_ORIGINAL_COMMAND': 'source-retain --apply'},
                            capture_output=True)
    assert result.returncode != 0 and b'restricted backup command refused' in result.stderr


def test_source_pending_cleanup_requires_every_surviving_offhost_identity(tmp_path, keys):
    old, cfg, _ = fixture(tmp_path, keys, day=1)
    today, _, _ = fixture(tmp_path, keys, stamp='20260909T020000Z', prefix='daily')
    reference = datetime(2026, 9, 9, 12, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    cfg.update(source_retention_enabled=True, source_retention_audit=str(tmp_path / 'audit'))
    vault.queue_source_retention(cfg, today.name, reference_time=reference)
    blocked = vault.retain_source(cfg, apply=True, reference_time=reference)
    assert blocked['status'] == 'blocked_waiting_offhost_confirmation'
    assert old.exists() and today.exists()
    confirm_fixture_sources(cfg)
    result = vault.retain_source(cfg, apply=True, reference_time=reference)
    assert result['deleted'] == [old.name] and today.exists()
    assert vault.retain_source(cfg, apply=True, reference_time=reference)['status'] == 'no_pending_daily_backup'


def test_confirmation_is_bound_to_descriptor_and_cannot_delete(tmp_path, keys):
    folder, cfg, _ = fixture(tmp_path, keys)
    cfg['source_retention_audit'] = str(tmp_path / 'audit')
    proof = {'descriptor_sha256': '0' * 64, 'ciphertext_sha256': 'a' * 64, 'ciphertext_bytes': 100}
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='descriptor mismatch'):
        vault.confirm_source(cfg, folder.name, proof)
    proof['descriptor_sha256'] = hashlib.sha256(vault.canonical(vault.describe(folder, cfg))).hexdigest()
    vault.confirm_source(cfg, folder.name, proof)
    assert folder.exists() and frozenset(p.name for p in folder.iterdir()) in vault.FILE_SETS
    for invalid in ({**proof, 'delete': True}, {**proof, 'ciphertext_bytes': True}, {**proof, 'ciphertext_sha256': '../x'}):
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            vault.confirm_source(cfg, folder.name, invalid)


def test_compressed_v2_backup_roundtrips_through_encryption_and_decrypt_verification(tmp_path, keys):
    """备份格式 v2（mysql.sql.zst）与 v1 走同一条加密导出、接收、解密核对链路。"""
    backup, cfg, config = fixture(tmp_path, keys, backup_format="label-system-server-backup-v2")
    result = receive(cfg, config, backup)
    assert result["status"] == "ciphertext_verified"
    assert {e["path"] for e in result["backup"]["files"]} == {"manifest.json", "mysql.sql.zst", "platform-data.tar.gz"}
    encrypted = Path(cfg["vault"]) / "completed" / backup.name / "backup.tar.gpg"
    p = subprocess.Popen(["gpg", "--homedir", str(keys[0]), "--batch", "--decrypt", str(encrypted)],
                         stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE)
    checked = vault.verify_plain_stream(p.stdout, result)
    p.stdout.close()
    assert p.wait() == 0 and checked["files_verified"] == 3


def test_manifest_format_must_match_the_sql_file_actually_present(tmp_path, keys):
    backup, cfg, _ = fixture(tmp_path, keys, backup_format="label-system-server-backup-v2")
    manifest = json.loads((backup / "manifest.json").read_text())
    manifest["format"] = "label-system-server-backup-v1"
    (backup / "manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps(manifest))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        vault.describe(backup, cfg)
    both = Path(cfg["source_roots"][0]) / "predeploy-both-20260902T000000Z"
    shutil.copytree(backup, both)
    (both / "mysql.sql").write_bytes(b"stray")
    assert both.name not in {f.name for f in vault.source_folders(cfg)}


def test_source_cleanup_deletes_old_v1_copies_once_v2_dailies_arrive(tmp_path, keys):
    reference = datetime(2026, 9, 9, 12, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    old, cfg, _ = fixture(tmp_path, keys, stamp="20260901T020000Z", prefix="daily")
    yesterday, _, _ = fixture(tmp_path, keys, stamp="20260908T020000Z", prefix="daily",
                              backup_format="label-system-server-backup-v2")
    earlier, _, _ = fixture(tmp_path, keys, stamp="20260908T010000Z", prefix="predeploy-second")
    today, _, _ = fixture(tmp_path, keys, stamp="20260909T020000Z", prefix="daily",
                          backup_format="label-system-server-backup-v2")
    cfg.update(source_retention_enabled=True, source_retention_audit=str(tmp_path / "audit"))
    confirm_fixture_sources(cfg)
    result = vault.retain_source(cfg, today.name, apply=True, reference_time=reference)
    assert set(result["deleted"]) == {old.name, earlier.name}
    assert not old.exists() and not earlier.exists()
    assert yesterday.exists() and today.exists()


def failing_source(tmp_path, config, fail_action, message, code=3):
    """真实源命令的包装：遇到指定动作时往 stderr 写原因并以非 0 退出，模拟 .85 受限命令失败。"""
    real = source_command(tmp_path, config)
    wrapper = tmp_path / ("failing-" + fail_action + ".py")
    wrapper.write_text(
        "import subprocess,sys\n"
        "if sys.argv[1] == " + repr(fail_action) + ":\n"
        "    sys.stderr.write(" + repr(message) + ")\n"
        "    sys.exit(" + str(code) + ")\n"
        "sys.exit(subprocess.run(" + repr(real) + " + sys.argv[1:]).returncode)\n")
    return [sys.executable, str(wrapper)]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("action", ["catalog", "confirm", "export"])
def test_source_command_failures_surface_the_source_stderr(tmp_path, keys, capsys, action):
    """09-29 22:43 .137 向 .85 confirm 返回 1，capture_output 把原因吞掉，journal 里只有 ssh 参数。
    现在 catalog / confirm / export 任一失败，异常消息与结构化日志都带出源端 stderr。"""
    backup, cfg, config = fixture(tmp_path, keys, stamp="20260907T000000Z", prefix="daily")
    message = "ValueError: receiver/source descriptor mismatch for " + action
    cfg.update(source_retention_audit=str(tmp_path / "source-audit"), source_ready=True,
               confirm_source_copies=True, source_command=failing_source(tmp_path, config, action, message))
    config.write_text(json.dumps(cfg))
    with pytest.raises((subprocess.CalledProcessError, RuntimeError)) as caught:
        vault.sync(cfg, reference_time=datetime(2026, 9, 7, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc))
    assert message in str(caught.value)
    if action != "export":
        assert isinstance(caught.value, subprocess.CalledProcessError) and caught.value.returncode == 3
    logged = [json.loads(line) for line in capsys.readouterr().err.splitlines()
              if line.startswith("{") and "source_command_failed" in line]
    assert logged and logged[-1]["returncode"] == 3 and message in logged[-1]["stderr"]
    assert logged[-1]["action"].split()[0] == action
    assert backup.exists()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_online_default_operation.py =====
from scripts.set_online_inference_defaults import prompt_providers,route_action


def test_classification_preserves_local_only_and_detects_foreach_cloud():
    local={'nodes':[{'kind':'prompt','config':{'model_snapshot':{'provider':'local_qwen'}}}]}
    assert route_action(providers=prompt_providers(local),online=False,batch=False)=='preserve_local'
    mixed={'nodes':local['nodes']+[{'kind':'foreach_image','config':{'item_executor':{'model_snapshot':{'provider':'doubao'}}}}]}
    assert prompt_providers(mixed)=={'local_qwen','doubao'}
    assert route_action(providers=prompt_providers(mixed),online=True,batch=True)=='online'


def test_legacy_cloud_slot_is_switched_without_rebinding_the_local_model():
    assert route_action(providers={'local_qwen'},online=True,batch=True)=='online'
    assert route_action(providers=set(),online=False,batch=False)=='online'

===== FILE backend/tests/test_online_fallback_entitlement.py =====
"""The 24 h online fallback belongs to high-priority categories only.

Operator decision 2026-09-15: 3D/SU escalates to the online endpoint when the
batch attempts or the 24 h deadline are spent; an ordinary category does not.

Two things this must not do. It must not remove ``fallback_policy`` from the
route - that field is what switches the whole batch transport state machine on,
and stripping it on 2026-09-15 left production calls hanging to the client
timeout. And it must not answer 'held': a hold retries every fifteen minutes
forever and occupies an in-flight slot nothing can release. Denial is therefore
expressed as 'exhausted', which fails the material the ordinary way.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from app import batch_endpoint_recovery as recovery
from app.incremental_sla import VERSION, online_fallback_allowed

QUEUED = datetime(2026, 9, 15, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


def _decide(*, batch_count, online_allowed, hours=0.0, online_count=0):
    return recovery.decide(queued_at=QUEUED, now=QUEUED + timedelta(hours=hours),
                           batch_count=batch_count, online_count=online_count,
                           online_limit=2, online_allowed=online_allowed)


# --- the decision itself ------------------------------------------------------

def test_batch_attempts_are_unaffected_by_entitlement():
    for allowed in (True, False):
        assert _decide(batch_count=0, online_allowed=allowed).mode == 'batch'
        assert _decide(batch_count=recovery.BATCH_MAX_ATTEMPTS - 1, online_allowed=allowed).mode == 'batch'


def test_a_high_category_escalates_when_batch_attempts_are_spent():
    decision = _decide(batch_count=recovery.BATCH_MAX_ATTEMPTS, online_allowed=True)
    assert decision.mode == 'online' and decision.attempt_no == 1


def test_an_ordinary_category_is_exhausted_instead_of_escalating():
    decision = _decide(batch_count=recovery.BATCH_MAX_ATTEMPTS, online_allowed=False)
    assert decision.mode == 'exhausted' and decision.attempt_no is None
    # Never a hold: that would retry forever and hold an in-flight slot.
    assert decision.mode != 'held'


def test_the_same_split_applies_at_the_24_hour_deadline():
    past = recovery.DEADLINE_SECONDS / 3600 + 1
    assert _decide(batch_count=0, online_allowed=True, hours=past).mode == 'online'
    assert _decide(batch_count=0, online_allowed=False, hours=past).mode == 'exhausted'
    assert _decide(batch_count=0, online_allowed=False, hours=past).remaining_seconds == 0


def test_an_already_started_online_attempt_is_never_reversed():
    # History wins over entitlement: a paid online call that exists stays owned.
    assert _decide(batch_count=0, online_allowed=False, online_count=1).mode == 'exhausted'
    started = recovery.decide(queued_at=QUEUED, now=QUEUED, batch_count=0, online_count=0,
                              online_limit=2, online_started=True, online_allowed=True)
    assert started.mode == 'online'


def test_entitlement_defaults_to_allowed_so_nothing_changes_unasked():
    assert _decide(batch_count=recovery.BATCH_MAX_ATTEMPTS, online_allowed=True).mode == 'online'
    assert recovery.decide(queued_at=QUEUED, now=QUEUED, batch_count=recovery.BATCH_MAX_ATTEMPTS,
                           online_count=0, online_limit=2).mode == 'online'


def test_transport_decision_forwards_entitlement():
    rows = [SimpleNamespace(status='failed', error_code='model_provider_json_truncated',
                            upstream_status_code=None, evidence_json='{}',
                            invocation_key='p:batch:%d' % n) for n in range(recovery.BATCH_MAX_ATTEMPTS)]
    common = dict(prefix='p:', queued_at=QUEUED, now=QUEUED, online_limit=2)
    assert recovery.transport_decision(rows, **common).mode == 'online'
    assert recovery.transport_decision(rows, online_allowed=False, **common).mode == 'exhausted'


# --- who is entitled ----------------------------------------------------------

def _run(sla):
    context = {} if sla is None else {'incremental_sla': sla}
    return SimpleNamespace(snapshot_json=json.dumps({'runtime_context': context}))


def _high(**over):
    return dict({'version': VERSION, 'source_event_id': 7, 'service_class': 'high',
                 'first_queued_at': QUEUED.isoformat(), 'deadline_at': QUEUED.isoformat()}, **over)


def test_with_the_feature_off_everyone_keeps_today_s_behaviour(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_SLA_ENABLED', '0')
    assert online_fallback_allowed(_run(_high(service_class='normal'))) is True
    assert online_fallback_allowed(_run(None)) is True


def test_with_the_feature_on_only_the_high_class_is_entitled(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_SLA_ENABLED', '1')
    assert online_fallback_allowed(_run(_high())) is True
    assert online_fallback_allowed(_run(_high(service_class='normal'))) is False


@pytest.mark.parametrize('sla', [
    None,                              # frozen before the feature was switched on
    {'version': 'incremental-sla-v0', 'service_class': 'normal'},   # older contract
    {'service_class': 'high'},         # no version at all
])
def test_a_run_frozen_without_this_contract_keeps_its_original_behaviour(monkeypatch, sla):
    monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_SLA_ENABLED', '1')
    assert online_fallback_allowed(_run(sla)) is True


@pytest.mark.parametrize('snapshot', ['', 'not json', '[]', None])
def test_an_unreadable_snapshot_never_strands_a_material(monkeypatch, snapshot):
    monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_SLA_ENABLED', '1')
    assert online_fallback_allowed(SimpleNamespace(snapshot_json=snapshot)) is True


def test_classification_comes_from_the_run_not_the_current_category_list(monkeypatch):
    # The frozen class decides, so re-pointing the env list cannot retroactively
    # entitle or strip a material that is already in flight.
    monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_SLA_ENABLED', '1')
    monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_SLA_HIGH_CATEGORIES', 'inspiration_image')
    assert online_fallback_allowed(_run(_high())) is True
    monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_SLA_HIGH_CATEGORIES', 'model_3d_su')
    assert online_fallback_allowed(_run(_high(service_class='normal'))) is False


# --- the deployment switch ----------------------------------------------------
# 2026-09-17 老大：先关掉「满 24 小时/尝试用尽转在线」。关的是升级动作，不是 fallback_policy。


def _spent_batch_rows(count=recovery.BATCH_MAX_ATTEMPTS):
    return [SimpleNamespace(status='failed', error_code='model_provider_json_truncated',
                            upstream_status_code=None, evidence_json='{}',
                            invocation_key='p:batch:%d' % n) for n in range(count)]


def _transport(rows, *, now=QUEUED, **over):
    return recovery.transport_decision(rows, prefix='p:', queued_at=QUEUED, now=now,
                                       online_limit=2, **over)


def test_the_switch_is_on_unless_a_deployment_says_otherwise(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.delenv(recovery.ONLINE_ESCALATION_ENV, raising=False)
    assert recovery.online_escalation_enabled() is True
    assert _transport(_spent_batch_rows()).mode == 'online'


def test_the_switch_off_denies_even_an_entitled_run(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv(recovery.ONLINE_ESCALATION_ENV, '0')
    assert recovery.online_escalation_enabled() is False
    # online_allowed=True 是高优先类目的冻结资格；部署开关关掉时同样不升级。
    assert _transport(_spent_batch_rows(), online_allowed=True).mode == 'exhausted'


def test_the_switch_off_still_spends_every_batch_attempt(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv(recovery.ONLINE_ESCALATION_ENV, '0')
    decision = _transport(_spent_batch_rows(1))
    assert decision.mode == 'batch' and decision.attempt_no == 2


def test_the_switch_off_at_the_deadline_is_exhausted_never_held(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv(recovery.ONLINE_ESCALATION_ENV, '0')
    past = QUEUED + timedelta(seconds=recovery.DEADLINE_SECONDS + 1)
    decision = _transport([], now=past)
    assert decision.mode == 'exhausted' and decision.mode != 'held'


def test_the_switch_never_touches_the_frozen_batch_orchestration(monkeypatch):
    # recovery.policy(...) is not None 是执行器决定走不走批量状态机的判据：
    # 摘掉它 2026-09-15 实测让批量退化成一次普通 HTTP 直连并挂到客户端超时。
    monkeypatch.setenv(recovery.ONLINE_ESCALATION_ENV, '0')
    runtime = {'inference_route': {'mode': 'batch', 'fallback_policy': {
        'version': recovery.VERSION, 'batch_max_attempts': recovery.BATCH_MAX_ATTEMPTS,
        'deadline_seconds': recovery.DEADLINE_SECONDS,
        'online_route': {'schema_version': 'inference-route-v1', 'mode': 'online',
                         'endpoint_id': 'ep-20260901120000-abcde'}}}}
    assert recovery.policy(runtime) is not None
    assert recovery.effective_route(runtime, 'batch') is runtime['inference_route']


@pytest.mark.parametrize('raw', ['', 'false', 'off', 'yes', '2'])
def test_a_switch_value_the_service_cannot_read_is_refused(monkeypatch, raw):
    monkeypatch.setenv(recovery.ONLINE_ESCALATION_ENV, raw)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        recovery.online_escalation_enabled()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_operational_metrics.py =====
