    _database_metrics,
    _worker_metrics,
    build_operational_metrics_router,
    evaluate_operational_alerts,
    operational_metrics_snapshot,
    prometheus_metrics,
)


NOW = datetime(2026, 8, 30, 2, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
BUSINESS_DATE = date(2026, 8, 30)
SECRET_MARKER = "must-never-appear-in-operations-metrics"


def _sessions() -> sessionmaker[Session]:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    return sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)


def _seed_operational_facts(sessions: sessionmaker[Session]) -> None:
    with sessions() as db:
        db.add_all(
            [
                AutomationWorkerStatus(
                    worker_id="worker-ready",
                    started_at=NOW - timedelta(hours=1),
                    last_seen_at=NOW - timedelta(seconds=5),
                    last_tick_at=NOW - timedelta(seconds=5),
                    last_status="idle",
                    readiness="ready",
                    last_error=SECRET_MARKER,
                ),
                AutomationWorkerStatus(
                    worker_id="worker-loop-stale",
                    started_at=NOW - timedelta(hours=1),
                    last_seen_at=NOW - timedelta(seconds=5),
                    last_tick_at=NOW - timedelta(minutes=3),
                    last_status="idle",
                    readiness="ready",
                ),
                RuntimeDispatchItem(
                    step_attempt_id=101,
                    queue_class="validation",
                    priority=50,
                    status="queued",
                    available_at=NOW - timedelta(minutes=10),
                    max_attempts=3,
                ),
                RuntimeDispatchItem(
                    step_attempt_id=102,
                    queue_class="validation",
                    priority=50,
                    status="dead_lettered",
                    available_at=NOW - timedelta(minutes=20),
                    max_attempts=3,
                    last_error_message=SECRET_MARKER,
                    dead_lettered_at=NOW - timedelta(minutes=5),
                ),
                RuntimeOutboxEvent(
                    event_key="metrics-outbox-queued",
                    target_contract_key="target-fixture",
                    business_idempotency_key="metrics:queued",
                    mechanism_version_id=201,
                    payload_blob_id=301,
                    payload_hash="a" * 64,
                    status="queued",
                    available_at=NOW - timedelta(minutes=20),
                    max_attempts=5,
                    last_error_message=SECRET_MARKER,
                ),
                RuntimeOutboxEvent(
                    event_key="metrics-outbox-dead",
                    target_contract_key="target-fixture",
                    business_idempotency_key="metrics:dead",
                    mechanism_version_id=201,
                    payload_blob_id=302,
                    payload_hash="b" * 64,
                    status="dead_lettered",
                    available_at=NOW - timedelta(minutes=30),
                    max_attempts=5,
                    dead_lettered_at=NOW - timedelta(minutes=2),
                    last_error_message=SECRET_MARKER,
                ),
            ]
        )
        pool = ModelQuotaPool(
            pool_key="doubao-main",
            name="豆包主池",
            provider="doubao",
            scope_kind="api_key",
            scope_identifier="redacted-fixture",
            max_concurrency=8,
            lease_seconds=180,
            enabled=True,
            cooldown_until=NOW + timedelta(minutes=5),
            cooldown_reason=SECRET_MARKER,
            created_by="admin",
        )
        db.add(pool)
        db.flush()
        db.add(
            ModelQuotaLease(
                pool_id=pool.id,
                lease_token="metrics-lease",
                owner_key="worker-ready",
                status="active",
                reserved_tokens=10,
                reserved_images=1,
                acquired_at=NOW - timedelta(seconds=30),
                expires_at=NOW + timedelta(seconds=90),
            )
        )
        db.add(
            WorkflowModelInvocation(
                production_run_id=401,
                step_attempt_id=402,
                node_instance_id="prompt-a",
                status="failed",
                provider="doubao",
                model_id="fixture-model",
                upstream_status_code=429,
                error_code="provider_429",
                error_message=SECRET_MARKER,
                created_at=NOW - timedelta(minutes=2),
            )
        )
        policy = HumanReviewQuotaPolicy(
            version=1,
            reviewer_count=1,
            per_reviewer_capacity=100,
            total_capacity=100,
            risk_error_capacity=60,
            cold_start_drift_capacity=20,
            stratified_random_capacity=15,
            golden_sentinel_capacity=5,
            severe_overflow_threshold=1,
            status="active",
            active_slot="global",
            version_hash="c" * 64,
            created_by="admin",
        )
        db.add(policy)
        db.flush()
        batch = HumanReviewDailyBatch(
            business_date=BUSINESS_DATE,
            policy_id=policy.id,
            candidate_set_hash="d" * 64,
            selection_hash="e" * 64,
            capacity=100,
            selected_count=2,
            overflow_count=1,
            status="finalized",
            created_by="system",
        )
        db.add(batch)
        db.flush()
        db.add_all(
            [
                HumanReviewDailyBatchItem(
                    batch_id=batch.id,
                    business_date=BUSINESS_DATE,
                    source_result_key="metrics:pending",
                    category_key="knowledge_graph",
                    bucket="risk_error",
                    stable_rank=1,
                    selection_state="selected_pending",
                ),
                HumanReviewDailyBatchItem(
                    batch_id=batch.id,
                    business_date=BUSINESS_DATE,
                    source_result_key="metrics:reviewed",
                    category_key="knowledge_graph",
                    bucket="risk_error",
                    stable_rank=2,
                    selection_state="selected_reviewed",
                    review_id=999,
                    reviewed_at=NOW - timedelta(minutes=1),
                ),
                HumanReviewOverflowFact(
                    batch_id=batch.id,
                    source_result_key="metrics:overflow",
                    category_key="knowledge_graph",
                    severity="severe",
                ),
            ]
        )
        db.commit()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "lease_expires_in,last_status,expected_stale",
    [(200, "working", 0), (-1, "working", 1), (200, "idle", 1)],
    ids=["live-lease-working", "expired-lease", "idle-with-live-lease"],
)
def test_sync_evaluation_worker_busy_on_a_live_step_lease_is_not_loop_stale(
    lease_expires_in: int, last_status: str, expected_stale: int
) -> None:
    """2026-09-17: the sync evaluation worker held prompt steps for ~56 minutes.

    Its loop tick only advances between jobs, so every long provider wait raised
    a critical worker_loop_stale while the step lease kept being renewed.
    """
    sessions = _sessions()
    worker_id = "evaluation:sync-busy"
    with sessions() as db:
        db.add(
            AutomationWorkerStatus(
                worker_id=worker_id,
                started_at=NOW - timedelta(hours=1),
                last_seen_at=NOW - timedelta(seconds=5),
                last_tick_at=NOW - timedelta(minutes=30),
                last_status=last_status,
                readiness="ready",
            )
        )
        run = ProductionRun(
            run_key="sync-busy", idempotency_key="sync-busy",
            source_type="content_ingress_event", workflow_definition_id=1,
            workflow_version_id=1, snapshot_json="{}", snapshot_hash="a" * 64,
            queue_class="production_batch", requested_by="test", owner="test",
            status="running",
        )
        db.add(run)
        db.flush()
        db.add(
            ProductionStepAttempt(
                run_id=run.id, step_key="prompt_a", step_type="model_call",
                sequence=0, script_version_id=1, idempotency_key="sync-busy-step",
                input_manifest_json="{}", input_hash="a" * 64, status="running",
                lease_owner=worker_id, lease_token="t" * 16,
                lease_expires_at=NOW + timedelta(seconds=lease_expires_in),
                heartbeat_at=NOW - timedelta(seconds=50),
                started_at=NOW - timedelta(minutes=30),
            )
        )
        db.commit()
        workers = _worker_metrics(db, expected_workers=1, current=NOW)
    assert workers["active"] == 1 and workers["missing"] == 0
    assert workers["loop_stale"] == expected_stale
    codes = {item["code"] for item in evaluate_operational_alerts({"workers": workers})}
    assert ("worker_loop_stale" in codes) is bool(expected_stale)


def test_specialized_worker_stalls_are_critical_even_with_fresh_heartbeats() -> None:
    sessions = _sessions()
    with sessions() as db:
        db.add_all(
            [
                AutomationWorkerStatus(
                    worker_id="evaluation:ready",
                    started_at=NOW - timedelta(hours=1),
                    last_seen_at=NOW - timedelta(seconds=5),
                    last_tick_at=NOW - timedelta(seconds=5),
                    last_status="idle",
                    readiness="ready",
                ),
                AutomationWorkerStatus(
                    worker_id="auxiliary:ready",
                    started_at=NOW - timedelta(hours=1),
                    last_seen_at=NOW - timedelta(seconds=5),
                    last_tick_at=NOW - timedelta(seconds=5),
                    last_status="idle",
                    readiness="ready",
                ),
                AutomationWorkerStatus(
                    worker_id="optimization:stalled",
                    started_at=NOW - timedelta(hours=1),
                    last_seen_at=NOW - timedelta(seconds=5),
                    last_tick_at=NOW - timedelta(seconds=5),
                    last_status="worker_error",
                    readiness="blocked",
                ),
                AutomationWorkerStatus(
                    worker_id="projection:stalled",
                    started_at=NOW - timedelta(hours=1),
                    last_seen_at=NOW - timedelta(seconds=5),
                    last_tick_at=NOW - timedelta(seconds=5),
                    last_status="error",
                    readiness="blocked",
                ),
            ]
        )
        db.commit()

        workers = _worker_metrics(db, expected_workers=1, current=NOW)

    assert workers["missing"] == 0
    assert workers["missing_auxiliary"] == 0
    assert workers["missing_optimization"] == 1
    assert workers["missing_projection"] == 1
    assert workers["loop_stale_optimization"] == 1
    assert workers["loop_stale_projection"] == 1
    assert workers["loop_stale"] == 2
    alerts = evaluate_operational_alerts({"workers": workers})
    assert {
        "optimization_worker_missing",
        "projection_worker_missing",
        "worker_loop_stale",
    } <= {item["code"] for item in alerts}


def test_snapshot_contains_only_redacted_counts_ages_and_statuses(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    sessions = _sessions()
    _seed_operational_facts(sessions)
    backup_status = tmp_path / "last-successful-backup.json"
    backup_status.write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "format": BACKUP_STATUS_FORMAT,
                "completed_at": (NOW - timedelta(hours=2)).isoformat(),
                "source_schema": "labelsystem",
                "manifest_sha256": "f" * 64,
            }
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    timestamp = NOW.timestamp() - 60
    os.utime(backup_status, (timestamp, timestamp))
    restore_status = tmp_path / "last-successful-restore.json"
    restore_status.write_text(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "format": RESTORE_STATUS_FORMAT,
                "completed": True,
                "completed_at": (NOW - timedelta(hours=1)).isoformat(),
                "source_schema": "legacy",
                "target_schema": "labelsystem",
                "manifest_sha256": "e" * 64,
                "restored_tables": 119,
                "restore_seconds": 42.5,
            }
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    with sessions() as db:
        payload = operational_metrics_snapshot(
            db,
            expected_workers=3,
            data_root=tmp_path,
            backup_status_file=backup_status,
            restore_status_file=restore_status,
            now=NOW,
        )

    assert payload["schema_version"] == "label-system-operational-metrics-v1"
    assert payload["workers"] == {
        "active": 2,
        "expected": 3,
        "missing": 1,
        "active_auxiliary": 1,
        "missing_auxiliary": 0,
        "active_optimization": 1,
        "missing_optimization": 0,
        "active_projection": 1,
        "missing_projection": 0,
        "loop_stale": 1,
        "loop_stale_auxiliary": 1,
        "loop_stale_optimization": 1,
        "loop_stale_projection": 1,
        "loop_stale_after_seconds": 60,
    }
    validation = next(
        item
        for item in payload["queues"]
        if item["queue_class"] == "validation"
    )
    assert validation["queued"] == 1
    assert validation["dead_lettered"] == 1
    assert validation["dead_lettered_last_15m"] == 1
    assert validation["oldest_queued_age_seconds"] >= 600
    assert payload["outbox"] == {
        "queued": 1,
        "leased": 0,
        "pending": 1,
        "dead_lettered": 1,
        "unknown": 0,
        "oldest_queued_age_seconds": 1200,
    }
    assert payload["provider"] == {
        "http_429_last_15m": 1,
        "cooldown_pools": 1,
        "timeouts_last_15m": 0,
        "confirmed_slots": 8,
        "active_leases": 1,
        "slot_utilization_ratio": 0.125,
        "expired_active_leases": 0,
    }
    assert payload["human_review"] == {
        "business_date": "2026-08-30",
        "capacity": 100,
        "selected": 2,
        "reviewed": 1,
        "pending": 1,
        "overflow": 1,
        "severe_overflow": 1,
        "status": "finalized",
    }
    assert payload["backup"] == {
        "configured": True,
        "available": True,
        "age_seconds": 7200,
    }
    assert payload["restore"] == {
        "configured": True,
        "available": True,
        "age_seconds": 3600,
        "restored_tables": 119,
        "restore_seconds": 42.5,
    }
    serialized = json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)
    assert SECRET_MARKER not in serialized
    for forbidden in ("payload_json", "error_message", "dsn", "token", str(tmp_path)):
        assert forbidden not in serialized.lower()
    codes = {item["code"]: item["severity"] for item in payload["alerts"]}
    assert {
        "worker_missing",
        "worker_loop_stale",
        "outbox_dead_letter",
        "provider_429",
        "human_review_severe_overflow",
    } <= set(codes)
    # validation 队列的死信是回归/模拟跑的测试产物：只提示，不进生产阻塞告警。
    assert codes["validation_dead_letter"] == "warning"
    assert "runtime_dead_letter" not in codes


def test_historical_runtime_dead_letters_stay_visible_without_blocking_release(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    sessions = _sessions()
    _seed_operational_facts(sessions)
    with sessions() as db:
        dead_letter = db.scalar(
            select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
                RuntimeDispatchItem.status == "dead_lettered"
            )
        )
        assert dead_letter is not None
        dead_letter.dead_lettered_at = NOW - timedelta(hours=2)
        db.commit()

    with sessions() as db:
        payload = operational_metrics_snapshot(
            db,
            expected_workers=2,
            data_root=tmp_path,
            backup_status_file=tmp_path / "missing-backup.json",
            now=NOW,
        )

    validation = next(
        item
        for item in payload["queues"]
        if item["queue_class"] == "validation"
    )
    assert validation["dead_lettered"] == 1
    assert validation["dead_lettered_last_15m"] == 0
    codes = {item["code"]: item["severity"] for item in payload["alerts"]}
    # 实时口径：validation 队列仍有未处理死信 → 持续 warning 提示，但不阻断发布。
    assert codes.get("validation_dead_letter") == "warning"
    assert "runtime_dead_letter" not in codes


def test_alert_thresholds_fail_closed_for_capacity_storage_and_backup() -> None:
    snapshot = {
        "workers": {"missing": 0, "loop_stale": 0},
        "queues": [
            {
                "queue_class": "production_batch",
                "queued": 10,
                "leased": 0,
                "dead_lettered": 0,
                "oldest_queued_age_seconds": 901,
            }
        ],
        "outbox": {
            "dead_lettered": 0,
            "unknown": 1,
            "oldest_queued_age_seconds": 0,
        },
        "provider": {
            "http_429_last_15m": 0,
            "cooldown_pools": 0,
            "expired_active_leases": 1,
        },
        "human_review": {
            "capacity": 100,
            "selected": 100,
            "severe_overflow": 0,
        },
        "database": {
            "connection_utilization_ratio": 0.8,
            "innodb_redo_log_checkpoint_utilization_ratio": 0.8,
        },
        "storage": {"available": True, "used_ratio": 0.9},
        "backup": {"configured": True, "available": False, "age_seconds": None},
    }

    alerts = evaluate_operational_alerts(snapshot)
    codes = {item["code"] for item in alerts}

    assert {
        "queue_oldest_age_high",
        "outbox_unknown_status",
        "provider_lease_expired",
        "human_review_capacity_exhausted",
        "mysql_connections_high",
        "mysql_redo_checkpoint_pressure",
        "data_disk_usage_high",
        "backup_missing",
    } <= codes
    assert all(set(item) == {"code", "severity"} for item in alerts)


def test_mysql_snapshot_and_prometheus_expose_redo_and_buffer_pool_pressure() -> None:
    class Result:
        def __init__(self, *, rows=(), row=None) -> None:
            self._rows = rows
            self._row = row

        def all(self):
            return list(self._rows)

        def one(self):
            return self._row

    class MySQLSession:
        def __init__(self) -> None:
            self.calls: list[str] = []

        def get_bind(self):
            return SimpleNamespace(dialect=SimpleNamespace(name="mysql"))

        def execute(self, statement):
            rendered = str(statement)
            self.calls.append(rendered)
            if rendered.startswith("SHOW GLOBAL STATUS"):
                return Result(
                    rows=(
                        ("Threads_connected", "80"),
                        ("Innodb_log_waits", "2"),
                        ("Innodb_buffer_pool_wait_free", "7"),
                        ("Innodb_os_log_written", "9000"),
                        ("Innodb_redo_log_checkpoint_lsn", "1000"),
                        ("Innodb_redo_log_current_lsn", "1800"),
                    )
                )
            return Result(row=(100, 2000, 4096))

    database = _database_metrics(MySQLSession())  # type: ignore[arg-type]

    assert database == {
        "dialect": "mysql",
        "available": True,
        "threads_connected": 80,
        "max_connections": 100,
        "connection_utilization_ratio": 0.8,
        "innodb_log_waits_total": 2,
        "innodb_buffer_pool_wait_free_total": 7,
        "innodb_redo_log_written_bytes_total": 9000,
        "innodb_redo_log_checkpoint_age_bytes": 800,
        "innodb_redo_log_capacity_bytes": 2000,
        "innodb_redo_log_checkpoint_utilization_ratio": 0.4,
        "innodb_buffer_pool_size_bytes": 4096,
    }
    rendered = prometheus_metrics(
        {
            "workers": {"active": 1, "expected": 1, "missing": 0, "loop_stale": 0},
            "queues": [],
            "outbox": {"pending": 0, "dead_lettered": 0, "unknown": 0, "oldest_queued_age_seconds": 0},
            "provider": {"http_429_last_15m": 0, "cooldown_pools": 0, "active_leases": 0, "expired_active_leases": 0},
            "human_review": {"capacity": 100, "selected": 0, "pending": 0, "severe_overflow": 0},
            "database": database,
            "storage": {"available": True, "used_ratio": 0.1},
            "backup": {"available": True, "age_seconds": 1},
            "alerts": [],
        }
    )
    assert "label_system_mysql_innodb_log_waits_total 2" in rendered
    assert "label_system_mysql_innodb_buffer_pool_wait_free_total 7" in rendered
    assert "label_system_mysql_innodb_redo_log_written_bytes_total 9000" in rendered
    assert "label_system_mysql_innodb_redo_log_checkpoint_age_bytes 800" in rendered
    assert "label_system_mysql_innodb_redo_log_capacity_bytes 2000" in rendered
    assert (
        "label_system_mysql_innodb_redo_log_checkpoint_utilization_ratio 0.400000"
        in rendered
    )
    assert "label_system_mysql_innodb_buffer_pool_size_bytes 4096" in rendered


def test_invalid_backup_status_and_unavailable_mysql_or_disk_fail_closed(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    invalid_status = tmp_path / "last-successful-backup.json"
    invalid_status.write_text("{}", encoding="utf-8")
    sessions = _sessions()

    with sessions() as db:
        payload = operational_metrics_snapshot(
            db,
            expected_workers=1,
            data_root=tmp_path,
            backup_status_file=invalid_status,
            now=NOW,
        )
    assert payload["backup"] == {
        "configured": True,
        "available": False,
        "age_seconds": None,
    }

    snapshot = {
        "workers": {"missing": 0, "loop_stale": 0},
        "queues": [],
        "outbox": {
            "dead_lettered": 0,
            "unknown": 0,
            "oldest_queued_age_seconds": 0,
        },
        "provider": {
            "http_429_last_15m": 0,
            "cooldown_pools": 0,
            "expired_active_leases": 0,
        },
        "human_review": {
            "capacity": 0,
            "selected": 0,
            "severe_overflow": 0,
        },
        "database": {
            "dialect": "mysql",
            "available": False,
            "connection_utilization_ratio": None,
        },
        "storage": {"available": False, "used_ratio": None},
        "backup": {
            "configured": True,
            "available": False,
            "age_seconds": None,
        },
    }
    codes = {item["code"] for item in evaluate_operational_alerts(snapshot)}
    assert {
        "human_review_quota_missing",
        "mysql_metrics_unavailable",
        "data_disk_metrics_unavailable",
        "backup_missing",
    } <= codes


def test_admin_json_and_machine_prometheus_routes_enforce_separate_auth(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    sessions = _sessions()
    role = {"value": "viewer"}
    machine_token = "fixture-machine-token"
    settings = SimpleNamespace(
        worker_cluster_expected_count=1,
        worker_process_count=1,
        data_dir=tmp_path,
        backup_status_file=tmp_path / "missing-backup.json",
        restore_status_file=tmp_path / "missing-restore.json",
        operations_metrics_token=machine_token,
    )

    def require_admin() -> object:
        if role["value"] != "admin":
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="仅管理员可查看运行指标")
        return SimpleNamespace(username="admin", is_admin=True)

    def db_override():
        with sessions() as db:
            yield db

    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(
        build_operational_metrics_router(
            require_admin,
            settings_provider=lambda: settings,
            now=lambda: NOW,
        )
    )
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = db_override

    with TestClient(app) as client:
        assert client.get("/api/operations/metrics").status_code == 403
        role["value"] = "admin"
        admin_response = client.get("/api/operations/metrics")
        assert admin_response.status_code == 200
        assert admin_response.json()["workers"]["expected"] == 1

        assert client.get("/metrics").status_code == 401
        assert client.get(
            "/metrics",
            headers={"Authorization": "Bearer wrong"},
        ).status_code == 401
        # 非 ASCII 的 Bearer 不能让 compare_digest 抛 TypeError 成 500（Astra 全仓复审 F6）。
        assert client.get(
            "/metrics",
            headers={"Authorization": "Bearer caf\u00e9".encode("utf-8")},
        ).status_code == 401
        machine_response = client.get(
            "/metrics",
            headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {machine_token}"},
        )
        assert machine_response.status_code == 200
        assert machine_response.headers["content-type"].startswith(
            "text/plain"
        )
        assert "label_system_workers_active 0" in machine_response.text
        assert "label_system_human_review_quota_configured 0" in (
            machine_response.text
        )
        assert "label_system_data_disk_metrics_available 1" in (
            machine_response.text
        )
        assert machine_token not in machine_response.text


def test_main_application_registers_both_operations_metrics_routes() -> None:
    from app.main import app as main_app

    paths: set[str] = set()

    def collect(routes: list[object]) -> None:
        for route in routes:
            path = getattr(route, "path", None)
            if isinstance(path, str):
                paths.add(path)
            original_router = getattr(route, "original_router", None)
            if original_router is not None:
                collect(list(original_router.routes))

    collect(list(main_app.routes))
    assert "/api/operations/metrics" in paths
    assert "/metrics" in paths


def test_production_queue_dead_letters_block_release_but_validation_only_warns() -> None:
    from app.operational_metrics import evaluate_operational_alerts as build_alerts

    def alerts_for(queue_class: str) -> dict[str, str]:
        snapshot = {
            "workers": {},
            "queues": [
                {
                    "queue_class": queue_class,
                    "dead_lettered": 1,
                    "dead_lettered_last_15m": 1,
                    "oldest_queued_age_seconds": 0,
                }
            ],
            "outbox": {},
            "provider": {},
        }
        return {item["code"]: item["severity"] for item in build_alerts(snapshot)}

    production = alerts_for("production_batch")
    assert production.get("runtime_dead_letter") == "critical"
    assert "validation_dead_letter" not in production

    validation = alerts_for("validation")
    assert validation.get("validation_dead_letter") == "warning"
    assert "runtime_dead_letter" not in validation


def test_dead_letter_alert_blocks_only_on_recent_blocking_dead_letters() -> None:
    from app.operational_metrics import evaluate_operational_alerts

    def alerts_for(queue_class: str, *, current: int, recent: int) -> dict[str, str]:
        snapshot = {
            "workers": {},
            "queues": [
                {
                    "queue_class": queue_class,
                    "dead_lettered": current,
                    "dead_lettered_last_15m": recent,
                    "oldest_queued_age_seconds": 0,
                }
            ],
            "outbox": {},
            "provider": {},
        }
        return {item["code"]: item["severity"] for item in evaluate_operational_alerts(snapshot)}

    # 2026-09-16 改口径：老死信仍未处理但不在 15 分钟窗口内 → 只是积压 warning，不拦发布。
    old = alerts_for("production_batch", current=1, recent=0)
    assert "runtime_dead_letter" not in old and old.get("runtime_dead_letter_backlog") == "warning"
    # 15 分钟内新产生的阻塞死信 → critical
    assert alerts_for("production_batch", current=1, recent=1).get("runtime_dead_letter") == "critical"
    # 死信已被处理（当前为 0），即使 15 分钟内曾发生 → 不告警
    assert "runtime_dead_letter" not in alerts_for("production_batch", current=0, recent=3)


def test_dead_letter_list_and_discard_clear_realtime_alert(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    sessions = _sessions()
    _seed_operational_facts(sessions)
    settings = SimpleNamespace(
        worker_cluster_expected_count=1,
        worker_process_count=1,
        data_dir=tmp_path,
        backup_status_file=tmp_path / "missing-backup.json",
        restore_status_file=tmp_path / "missing-restore.json",
        operations_metrics_token="t",
    )

    def require_admin() -> object:
        return SimpleNamespace(username="admin", is_admin=True)

    def db_override():
        with sessions() as db:
            yield db

    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(
        build_operational_metrics_router(
            require_admin, settings_provider=lambda: settings, now=lambda: NOW
        )
    )
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = db_override

    with TestClient(app) as client:
        listed = client.get("/api/operations/dead-letters").json()
        assert listed["total"] >= 1
        validation = next(q for q in listed["queues"] if q["queue_class"] == "validation")
        assert validation["count"] >= 1 and validation["items"]
        before = {a["code"] for a in client.get("/api/operations/metrics").json()["alerts"]}
        assert "validation_dead_letter" in before

        missing = client.post(
            "/api/operations/dead-letters/discard", json={"queue_class": "production_batch"}
        )
        assert missing.status_code == 404

        done = client.post(
            "/api/operations/dead-letters/discard",
            json={"queue_class": "validation", "note": "测试产物"},
        ).json()
        assert done["discarded"] == validation["count"]
        after = client.get("/api/operations/metrics").json()["alerts"]
        assert "validation_dead_letter" not in {a["code"] for a in after}
        assert client.get("/api/operations/dead-letters").json()["total"] == 0


def test_recovery_baseline_dead_letters_keep_validation_severity(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    sessions = _sessions()
    with sessions() as db:
        run = ProductionRun(
            run_key="baseline-recovery",
            idempotency_key="baseline-recovery",
            source_type="baseline_regression_item", workflow_definition_id=1,
            workflow_version_id=1, snapshot_json="{}", snapshot_hash="a" * 64,
            queue_class="validation", requested_by="test", owner="test",
        )
        db.add(run)
        db.flush()
        step = ProductionStepAttempt(
            run_id=run.id, step_key="model", step_type="model_call",
            sequence=0, script_version_id=1, idempotency_key="baseline-step",
            input_manifest_json="{}", input_hash="a" * 64,
        )
        db.add(step)
        db.flush()
        db.add(
            RuntimeDispatchItem(
                step_attempt_id=step.id, queue_class="recovery",
                status="dead_lettered", available_at=NOW, dead_lettered_at=NOW,
            )
        )
        db.commit()

        def snapshot() -> dict[str, object]:
            return operational_metrics_snapshot(
                db, expected_workers=0, data_root=tmp_path,
                backup_status_file=tmp_path / "missing.json", now=NOW,
            )
        payload = snapshot()
        codes = {a["code"]: a["severity"] for a in payload["alerts"]}
        assert "runtime_dead_letter" not in codes
        # 09-18 起存量回归的死信整体不进增量看板：既不阻塞也不再以 validation 告警出现，
        # 只在 regression_dead_lettered 单列（老大：混在一起会把回归失败当业务故障）。
        assert "validation_dead_letter" not in codes
        recovery = next(
            q for q in payload["queues"] if q["queue_class"] == "recovery"
        )
        assert recovery["dead_lettered"] == 0
        assert recovery["regression_dead_lettered"] == 1
        assert recovery["validation_dead_lettered"] == 0
        assert recovery["blocking_dead_lettered"] == 0
        assert (
            'label_system_queue_blocking_dead_lettered{queue_class="recovery"} 0'
            in prometheus_metrics(payload)
        )
        # A genuine production failure in the same retry queue must still block.
        run.source_type = "incremental_item"
        db.commit()
        assert any(
            a == {"code": "runtime_dead_letter", "severity": "critical"}
            for a in snapshot()["alerts"]
        )
        # Missing provenance is never an exemption.
        run.source_type = None
        db.commit()
        assert any(a["code"] == "runtime_dead_letter" for a in snapshot()["alerts"])


def test_recent_blocking_count_prefers_the_explicit_field_and_exempts_validation() -> None:
    from app.operational_metrics import _recent_blocking_dead_letters as recent

    assert recent({"queue_class": "recovery", "dead_lettered": 6, "dead_lettered_last_15m": 4, "blocking_dead_lettered_last_15m": 1}) == 1
    assert recent({"queue_class": "recovery", "dead_lettered": 6, "dead_lettered_last_15m": 4}) == 4, "老快照保守按全部近期死信算"
    assert recent({"queue_class": "recovery", "dead_lettered": 2, "dead_lettered_last_15m": 4}) == 2, "已处理的不再算：以当前总数为上界"
    assert recent({"queue_class": "validation", "dead_lettered": 6, "dead_lettered_last_15m": 4}) == 0


def test_dead_letter_list_exposes_run_and_retry_reports_per_run(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """运营在「失败待处理」直接重试：列表带 run 引用；重试按 run 去重、逐条留结果，非 failed 的 run 明确拒绝。"""
    sessions = _sessions()
    _seed_operational_facts(sessions)
    settings = SimpleNamespace(
        worker_cluster_expected_count=1,
        worker_process_count=1,
        data_dir=tmp_path,
        backup_status_file=tmp_path / "missing-backup.json",
        restore_status_file=tmp_path / "missing-restore.json",
        operations_metrics_token="t",
    )

    def require_admin() -> object:
        return SimpleNamespace(username="ops", is_admin=True)

    def db_override():
        with sessions() as db:
            yield db

    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(
        build_operational_metrics_router(
            require_admin, settings_provider=lambda: settings, now=lambda: NOW
        )
    )
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = db_override

    with TestClient(app) as client:
        listed = client.get("/api/operations/dead-letters").json()
        items = [item for group in listed["queues"] for item in group["items"]]
        assert items and all({"run_id", "run_key", "run_status"} <= set(item) for item in items)
        assert client.post("/api/operations/dead-letters/retry", json={"ids": [999999], "note": "x"}).status_code == 404
        assert client.post("/api/operations/dead-letters/retry", json={"ids": [items[0]["id"]], "note": ""}).status_code == 422
        result = client.post(
            "/api/operations/dead-letters/retry", json={"ids": [item["id"] for item in items], "note": "人工确认重试"}
        ).json()
        assert result["ok_count"] + result["failed_count"] == len(result["results"])
        for row in result["results"]:
            assert row["ok"] is True or row["code"] in {"run_retry_state_invalid", "run_missing", "run_retry_step_ambiguous", "run_resume_step_missing"}
            if row["ok"]:
                assert row["run_id"] is not None and row["superseded"]
        from app.models import AuditEvent

        with sessions() as db:
            audit = db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.action == "dead_letters_retried"))
            assert audit is not None and audit.actor == "ops"
            assert json.loads(audit.payload_json)["note"] == "人工确认重试"



def test_regression_dead_letters_stay_out_of_the_incremental_board(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """run 342（灵感图 Qwen 存量回归）的 357 条死信曾把「失败待处理」推到 676 条，被当成
    3dsu 增量故障。回归是实验：生产批量队列里的回归死信不计入 dead_lettered / total，
    不触发安全排队，只在 regression 桶里单列。"""
    from app.runtime_capacity import runtime_capacity_snapshot
    sessions = _sessions()
    with sessions() as db:
        rows = []
        for key, source in (("reg", "baseline_regression_item"), ("inc", "content_ingress_event")):
            run = ProductionRun(
                run_key=f"dl-{key}", idempotency_key=f"dl-{key}", source_type=source,
                workflow_definition_id=1, workflow_version_id=1, snapshot_json="{}",
                snapshot_hash="a" * 64, queue_class="production_batch",
                requested_by="test", owner="test",
            )
            db.add(run); db.flush()
            step = ProductionStepAttempt(
                run_id=run.id, step_key="model", step_type="model_call", sequence=0,
                script_version_id=1, idempotency_key=f"dl-step-{key}",
                input_manifest_json="{}", input_hash="a" * 64,
            )
            db.add(step); db.flush()
            item = RuntimeDispatchItem(
                step_attempt_id=step.id, queue_class="production_batch",
                status="dead_lettered", available_at=NOW, dead_lettered_at=NOW,
            )
            db.add(item); rows.append(item)
        db.commit()
        snap = runtime_capacity_snapshot(db, now=NOW)
        assert snap["dead_letters"]["runtime"] == 1 and snap["dead_letters"]["total"] == 1
        assert snap["dead_letters"]["regression"] == 1
        batch = next(q for q in snap["queues"] if q["queue_class"] == "production_batch")
        assert batch["dead_letters"] == 1 and batch["regression_dead_letters"] == 1


def test_regression_dead_letters_do_not_cancel_real_recovery_backlog(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """dead_lettered 已不含回归死信；再按回归死信数减一次会把真实积压抵成 0。"""
    sessions = _sessions()
    earlier = NOW - timedelta(hours=1)
    with sessions() as db:
        for key, source in (("reg", "baseline_regression_item"), ("inc", "incremental_item")):
            run = ProductionRun(
                run_key=f"{key}-run", idempotency_key=f"{key}-run",
                source_type=source, workflow_definition_id=1,
                workflow_version_id=1, snapshot_json="{}", snapshot_hash="a" * 64,
                queue_class="recovery", requested_by="test", owner="test",
            )
            db.add(run)
            db.flush()
            step = ProductionStepAttempt(
                run_id=run.id, step_key="model", step_type="model_call",
                sequence=0, script_version_id=1, idempotency_key=f"{key}-step",
                input_manifest_json="{}", input_hash="a" * 64,
            )
            db.add(step)
            db.flush()
            db.add(RuntimeDispatchItem(
                step_attempt_id=step.id, queue_class="recovery",
                status="dead_lettered", available_at=earlier, dead_lettered_at=earlier,
            ))
        db.commit()
        payload = operational_metrics_snapshot(
            db, expected_workers=0, data_root=tmp_path,
            backup_status_file=tmp_path / "missing.json", now=NOW,
        )
    recovery = next(q for q in payload["queues"] if q["queue_class"] == "recovery")
    assert recovery["dead_lettered"] == 1
    assert recovery["regression_dead_lettered"] == 1
    assert recovery["blocking_dead_lettered"] == 1
    assert recovery["blocking_dead_lettered_last_15m"] == 0
    codes = {a["code"]: a["severity"] for a in payload["alerts"]}
    assert codes.get("runtime_dead_letter_backlog") == "warning"
    assert "runtime_dead_letter" not in codes
    assert (
        'label_system_queue_blocking_dead_lettered{queue_class="recovery"} 1'
        in prometheus_metrics(payload)
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize("age_hours,stale", [(24, False), (25.9, False), (26, True)])
def test_backup_stale_leaves_room_for_timer_jitter_and_lock_wait(age_hours, stale) -> None:
    snapshot = {
        "storage": {"available": True, "used_ratio": 0.1},
        "backup": {"configured": True, "available": True, "age_seconds": int(age_hours * 3600)},
        "restore": {"configured": True, "available": True},
    }
    codes = {item["code"] for item in evaluate_operational_alerts(snapshot)}
    assert ("backup_stale" in codes) is stale

===== FILE backend/tests/test_operations_schema.py =====
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, inspect
from app.migrations.runner import MIGRATIONS, run_migrations
from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import mysql_metadata
from app.migrations.postgres_baseline import postgres_metadata
from app.database import Base
from app import models  # noqa: F401

TABLES = {'source_quarantine_rows', 'incremental_material_jobs', 'incremental_reference_results',
    'incremental_capacity_sampling_policies', 'incremental_capacity_samples'}


def test_operations_schema_upgrade_is_additive_and_repeatable():
    assert any(migration.version == 105 for migration in MIGRATIONS)
    engine = create_engine('sqlite://')
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        run_migrations(connection)
        connection.exec_driver_sql('CREATE TABLE operator_preserved (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, value TEXT)')
        connection.exec_driver_sql("INSERT INTO operator_preserved VALUES (1, 'keep')")
        for table in ('incremental_capacity_samples', 'incremental_capacity_sampling_policies',
                      'incremental_reference_results', 'incremental_material_jobs', 'source_quarantine_rows'):
            connection.exec_driver_sql(f'DROP TABLE {table}')
        connection.exec_driver_sql('DELETE FROM schema_migrations WHERE version = 105')
        run_migrations(connection)
        run_migrations(connection)
        assert TABLES <= set(inspect(connection).get_table_names())
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql('SELECT value FROM operator_preserved').scalar_one() == 'keep'
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql('SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations').scalar_one() == MIGRATIONS[-1].version
    engine.dispose()


def test_operations_tables_are_in_both_server_metadata():
    assert TABLES <= set(mysql_metadata().tables)
    assert TABLES <= set(postgres_metadata().tables)


def test_mysql_104_upgrade_entry_reaches_all_operations_tables(monkeypatch):
    from unittest.mock import Mock
    from app.migrations import mysql_baseline as module
    connection = Mock()
    connection.dialect.name = 'mysql'
    connection.exec_driver_sql.return_value.scalar_one_or_none.return_value = 104
    tables = {name: Mock() for name in TABLES}
    metadata = Mock(tables=tables)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, 'mysql_metadata', lambda: metadata)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, '_require_mysql', lambda connection: None)
    module.upgrade_mysql_schema(connection, expected=105)
    for table in tables.values():
        table.create.assert_called_once_with(connection, checkfirst=True)
    assert connection.exec_driver_sql.call_args.args[1] == (105, 'incremental_operations')


def _mysql_117_rerun(monkeypatch, *, columns, indexes):
    from unittest.mock import Mock
    from app.migrations import mysql_baseline as module
    connection = Mock()
    connection.dialect.name = 'mysql'
    connection.exec_driver_sql.return_value.scalar_one_or_none.return_value = 116
    inspector = Mock()
    inspector.get_columns.return_value = [{'name': name} for name in columns]
    inspector.get_indexes.return_value = [{'name': name} for name in indexes]
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, 'inspect', lambda _connection: inspector)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, '_require_mysql', lambda connection: None)
    module.upgrade_mysql_schema(connection, expected=117)
    return [call.args[0] for call in connection.exec_driver_sql.call_args_list]


def test_mysql_117_rerun_adds_missing_index_when_column_already_exists(monkeypatch):
    # 上一次加列成功、建索引失败（DDL 不回滚）：重跑必须补索引，不能因列已存在就跳过。
    statements = _mysql_117_rerun(monkeypatch, columns={'id', 'held_by_release'}, indexes=set())
    assert not any('ADD COLUMN' in sql for sql in statements)
    assert any('CREATE INDEX `ix_production_runs_held_by_release`' in sql for sql in statements)


def test_mysql_117_is_a_no_op_when_column_and_index_exist(monkeypatch):
    statements = _mysql_117_rerun(monkeypatch, columns={'id', 'held_by_release'},
                                  indexes={'ix_production_runs_held_by_release'})
    assert not any('ADD COLUMN' in sql or 'CREATE INDEX' in sql for sql in statements)
    assert any('INSERT INTO schema_migrations' in sql for sql in statements)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_optimization_case_insert.py =====
"""优化样本入队不能用只在 SQLite 上能编译的 on_conflict_do_nothing（正式服是 MySQL）。"""
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, insert, select
from sqlalchemy.dialects import mysql
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app import optimization_case_insert as module
from app.database import Base
from app.models import OptimizationCaseQueue

APP = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "app"


def _values(item_id: int = 1, **extra):
    return {
        "idempotency_key": f"baseline-regression-item:{item_id}",
        "evaluation_id": 10,
        "final_review_id": None,
        "source_type": "baseline_regression",
        "source_event_id": None,
        "baseline_regression_item_id": item_id,
        "prompt_version": "v1",
        "severity": "P2",
        "case_json": "{}",
        "status": "pending",
        **extra,
    }


@pytest.fixture
def db():
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://", connect_args={"check_same_thread": False}, poolclass=StaticPool)
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with Session(engine) as session:
        yield session
    engine.dispose()


def _count(db: Session) -> int:
    return db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(OptimizationCaseQueue))


def test_portable_statement_compiles_for_mysql():
    compiled = str(insert(OptimizationCaseQueue).values(**_values()).compile(dialect=mysql.dialect()))
    assert compiled.startswith("INSERT INTO optimization_case_queue")


def test_production_paths_no_longer_use_the_sqlite_only_insert():
    for name in ("main.py", "inspiration_auto_correction.py"):
        source = (APP / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        assert "dialects.sqlite import insert" not in source, name
        assert "on_conflict_do_nothing" not in source, name


@pytest.mark.parametrize("native", [True, False])
def test_second_insert_of_the_same_case_is_skipped(db, monkeypatch, native):
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "_uses_sqlite_upsert", lambda _db: native)
    assert module.insert_optimization_case_if_absent(db, _values()) is True
    assert module.insert_optimization_case_if_absent(db, _values()) is False
    # 同一基线条目换了幂等键也算已入队（两个唯一索引 1:1）。
    assert module.insert_optimization_case_if_absent(db, _values(idempotency_key="other")) is False
    assert module.insert_optimization_case_if_absent(db, _values(2)) is True
    assert _count(db) == 2


def test_portable_path_treats_a_lost_race_as_already_queued(db, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "_uses_sqlite_upsert", lambda _db: False)
    db.execute(insert(OptimizationCaseQueue).values(**_values()))
    real = module._existing_case_id
    calls = []

    def racing(session, values):
        calls.append(1)
        # 第一次预检看不到（对方尚未提交），插入撞唯一键后回查才看到。
        return None if len(calls) == 1 else real(session, values)

    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "_existing_case_id", racing)
    assert module.insert_optimization_case_if_absent(db, _values()) is False
    assert _count(db) == 1
    # 外层事务仍可继续使用。
    assert module.insert_optimization_case_if_absent(db, _values(3)) is True


def test_portable_path_reraises_non_duplicate_integrity_errors(db, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "_uses_sqlite_upsert", lambda _db: False)
    with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
        module.insert_optimization_case_if_absent(db, _values(severity="P9"))

===== FILE backend/tests/test_outbox_delivery_tick.py =====
"""独立投递器 tick：按目标分组调用 deliver_outbox_batch，未配置适配器的目标只记 warning。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from types import SimpleNamespace

from app import outbox_delivery_tick as tick
from app.connector_delivery import ConnectorDeliveryError

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from app.models import RuntimeOutboxEvent


def _queued(target, now, **overrides):
    return RuntimeOutboxEvent(event_key=f"event-{target}", target_contract_key=f"target-{target}",
        target_contract_version_id=target, business_idempotency_key=f"business-{target}",
        mechanism_version_id=1, payload_blob_id=1, payload_hash="a" * 64,
        available_at=now, **overrides)


def test_target_scan_advances_past_full_unconfigured_page():
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    RuntimeOutboxEvent.__table__.create(engine)
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        db.add_all([_queued(target, now) for target in range(1, 52)])
        db.commit()
    pages = []
    for _ in range(3):
        with Session(engine) as db:
            pages.append(tick.due_outbox_targets(db, now=now))
    assert pages == [list(range(1, 51)), [51], list(range(1, 51))]
    engine.dispose()


def test_scan_includes_expired_leases_but_not_future_work():
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    RuntimeOutboxEvent.__table__.create(engine)
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        db.add_all([
            _queued(1, now, status="leased", lease_expires_at=now-timedelta(seconds=1)),
            _queued(2, now, status="leased", lease_expires_at=now+timedelta(seconds=1)),
            _queued(3, now+timedelta(seconds=1)),
            _queued(4, now, status="leased", lease_expires_at=now-timedelta(seconds=1), attempt_count=5),
        ])
        db.commit()
        assert tick.due_outbox_targets(db, now=now) == [1, 4]
    engine.dispose()


def test_delivers_each_due_target_and_skips_unconfigured(monkeypatch) -> None:
    targets = {
        1: SimpleNamespace(id=1, direction="downstream", status="active", contract_key="d1", version=1),
        2: SimpleNamespace(id=2, direction="downstream", status="active", contract_key="d2", version=1),
        3: SimpleNamespace(id=3, direction="upstream", status="active", contract_key="u", version=1),
    }
    events: list[str] = []

    class FakeDb:
        def get(self, _model, key):
            return targets.get(key)

        def commit(self) -> None:
            events.append("commit")

        def rollback(self) -> None:
            events.append("rollback")

    monkeypatch.setattr(tick, "due_outbox_targets", lambda db, now=None: [1, 2, 3, 9])

    def resolve(target):
        if target.id == 2:
            raise ConnectorDeliveryError("downstream_adapter_unavailable", "未配置")
        return "adapter"

    monkeypatch.setattr(tick, "resolve_configured_downstream_adapter", resolve)

    def deliver(db, target, *, worker_id, adapter, now=None):
        events.append(f"deliver:{target.id}:{worker_id}")
        return SimpleNamespace(
            delivered_event_ids=(10, 11), retry_event_ids=(12,), blocked_event_ids=()
        )

    monkeypatch.setattr(tick, "deliver_outbox_batch", deliver)
    summary = tick.deliver_due_outbox_events(FakeDb(), worker_id="w1")
    assert summary["targets"] == 2
    assert summary["delivered"] == 2 and summary["retry"] == 1
    assert summary["skipped_unconfigured"] == 1
    assert events == ["deliver:1:w1", "commit"]


def test_delivery_error_rolls_back_and_continues(monkeypatch) -> None:
    target = SimpleNamespace(id=1, direction="downstream", status="active", contract_key="d1", version=1)
    calls: list[str] = []

    class FakeDb:
        def get(self, _model, key):
            return target

        def commit(self) -> None:
            calls.append("commit")

        def rollback(self) -> None:
            calls.append("rollback")

    monkeypatch.setattr(tick, "due_outbox_targets", lambda db, now=None: [1])
    monkeypatch.setattr(tick, "resolve_configured_downstream_adapter", lambda t: "adapter")

    def deliver(*_args, **_kwargs):
        raise ConnectorDeliveryError("downstream_schema_drift", "结构漂移")

    monkeypatch.setattr(tick, "deliver_outbox_batch", deliver)
    summary = tick.deliver_due_outbox_events(FakeDb(), worker_id="w1")
    assert summary["errors"] == [{"target": "d1", "code": "downstream_schema_drift"}]
    assert calls == ["rollback"]


def test_factory_slots_parallelize_without_rewriting_contract(monkeypatch):
    from threading import Barrier, Lock
    barrier = Barrier(20)
    lock = Lock()
    identities = []
    target = SimpleNamespace(id=11, direction='downstream', status='active',
        adapter_key='factory-submit-v1', contract_key='factory.prod.downstream', batch_size=1)
    class Db:
        def get(self, *_): return target
        def get_bind(self): return 'test-bind'
        def commit(self): pass
    monkeypatch.setenv('FACTORY_DELIVERY_WORKERS', '20')
    monkeypatch.setattr(tick, 'due_outbox_targets', lambda *a, **k: [11])
    def slot(bind, target_id, worker_id):
        assert bind == 'test-bind' and target_id == 11
        with lock: identities.append(worker_id)
        barrier.wait(timeout=5)
        if worker_id.endswith(':7'): raise RuntimeError('one independent slot fails')
        return SimpleNamespace(delivered_event_ids=(worker_id,), retry_event_ids=(), blocked_event_ids=())
    monkeypatch.setattr(tick, '_deliver_factory_slot', slot)
    result = tick.deliver_due_outbox_events(Db(), worker_id='worker')
    assert len(set(identities)) == 20
    assert result['delivered'] == 19 and len(result['errors']) == 1
    assert target.batch_size == 1


def test_inspiration_factory_slots_parallelize_with_a_cap(monkeypatch):
    """09-29 切正式后灵感图投递只走单适配器路径（每 tick 1 条）成了瓶颈；改走并行槽位，上限 8。"""
    from threading import Lock
    lock = Lock()
    identities = []
    target = SimpleNamespace(id=21, direction='downstream', status='active',
        adapter_key='inspiration-factory-submit-v1', contract_key='inspiration.factory.lgt-prod.downstream', batch_size=1)
    class Db:
        def get(self, *_): return target
        def get_bind(self): return 'test-bind'
        def commit(self): pass
    monkeypatch.setenv('FACTORY_DELIVERY_WORKERS', '20')
    monkeypatch.setattr(tick, 'due_outbox_targets', lambda *a, **k: [21])
    def slot(bind, target_id, worker_id):
        assert bind == 'test-bind' and target_id == 21
        with lock: identities.append(worker_id)
        return SimpleNamespace(delivered_event_ids=(worker_id,), retry_event_ids=(), blocked_event_ids=())
    monkeypatch.setattr(tick, '_deliver_factory_slot', slot)
    monkeypatch.setattr(tick, 'resolve_configured_downstream_adapter', lambda *_: pytest.fail('must not take the single-adapter path'))
    result = tick.deliver_due_outbox_events(Db(), worker_id='worker')
    assert len(set(identities)) == tick.INSPIRATION_DELIVERY_SLOT_CAP == 8
    assert result['delivered'] == 8 and target.batch_size == 1
    assert tick.parallel_delivery_slots('factory-submit-v1') == 20
    assert tick.parallel_delivery_slots('sql-envelope-v1') == 1
    monkeypatch.setenv('FACTORY_DELIVERY_WORKERS', '1')
    assert tick.parallel_delivery_slots('inspiration-factory-submit-v1') == 1


def test_outbox_error_on_one_target_does_not_stop_the_others(monkeypatch) -> None:
    # 复审 09-26：租约过期抛 RuntimeOutboxError 时，tick 只捕获 ConnectorDeliveryError，
    # 同 tick 的其他目标被整体中断。
    from app.runtime_outbox import RuntimeOutboxError

    targets = {i: SimpleNamespace(id=i, direction="downstream", status="active",
                                  contract_key=f"d{i}", version=1) for i in (1, 2)}
    calls: list[str] = []

    class FakeDb:
        def get(self, _model, key):
            return targets.get(key)

        def commit(self) -> None:
            calls.append("commit")

        def rollback(self) -> None:
            calls.append("rollback")

    monkeypatch.setattr(tick, "due_outbox_targets", lambda db, now=None: [1, 2])
    monkeypatch.setattr(tick, "resolve_configured_downstream_adapter", lambda t: "adapter")

    def deliver(db, target, **_kwargs):
        if target.id == 1:
            raise RuntimeOutboxError("outbox_lease_stale", "租约失效")
        return SimpleNamespace(delivered_event_ids=(5,), retry_event_ids=(), blocked_event_ids=())

    monkeypatch.setattr(tick, "deliver_outbox_batch", deliver)
    summary = tick.deliver_due_outbox_events(FakeDb(), worker_id="w1")
    assert summary["errors"] == [{"target": "d1", "code": "outbox_lease_stale"}]
    assert summary["delivered"] == 1
    assert calls == ["rollback", "commit"]

===== FILE backend/tests/test_output_payload_coercion.py =====
"""方舟 strict（beta）回错类型时，按冻结 schema 做无损纠偏；结构性错误仍 fail-closed。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from app.prompt_output_contract import (
    PromptOutputContractError,
    coerce_output_payload,
    parse_output_contract,
    validate_output_payload,
)


def _contract() -> object:
    return parse_output_contract(
        {
            "mode": "json_schema",
            "name": "legacy_review",
            "strict": True,
            "schema": {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {
                    "tags": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}},
                    "score": {"type": "integer"},
                    "hit": {"type": "boolean"},
                    "reason": {"type": "string", "enum": ["a", "b"]},
                    "nested": {
                        "type": "object",
                        "properties": {"n": {"type": "integer"}},
                        "required": ["n"],
                        "additionalProperties": False,
                    },
                },
                "required": ["tags", "score", "hit", "reason", "nested"],
                "additionalProperties": False,
            },
        }
    )


def test_comma_string_tags_and_quoted_score_are_coerced_then_validate() -> None:
    payload = {
        "tags": "滨海休闲, 木质餐吧、海景风光；地产宣传",
        "score": "82",
        "hit": "false",
        "reason": "a",
        "nested": {"n": 3.0},
    }
    coercions = coerce_output_payload(_contract(), payload)
    assert payload["tags"] == ["滨海休闲", "木质餐吧", "海景风光", "地产宣传"]
    assert payload["score"] == 82 and payload["hit"] is False and payload["nested"]["n"] == 3
    assert coercions == [
        "$.tags:string->array",
        "$.score:string->integer",
        "$.hit:string->boolean",
        "$.nested.n:float->integer",
    ]
    validate_output_payload(_contract(), payload)


def test_semantic_errors_are_not_coerced() -> None:
    payload = {"tags": ["x"], "score": "eighty", "hit": True, "reason": "zzz", "nested": {"n": 1}}
    assert coerce_output_payload(_contract(), payload) == []
    with pytest.raises(PromptOutputContractError):
        validate_output_payload(_contract(), payload)


def test_json_object_mode_is_untouched() -> None:
    contract = parse_output_contract({"mode": "json_object"})
    payload = {"tags": "a,b"}
    assert coerce_output_payload(contract, payload) == []
    assert payload == {"tags": "a,b"}


def test_single_text_cons_wrapper_is_unwrapped_without_changing_text() -> None:
    contract = parse_output_contract({"mode": "json_schema", "name": "cons", "strict": True,
        "schema": {"type": "object", "properties": {"cons": {"type": "string"}},
                   "required": ["cons"], "additionalProperties": False}})
    text = " 模型细节简化；材质反射趋同。\n保留原文 "
    payload = {"cons": [text]}
    assert coerce_output_payload(contract, payload) == ["$.cons:singleton_string_array->string"]
    assert payload["cons"] == text
    validate_output_payload(contract, payload)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("value", [[], ["a", "b"], [None], [3], [["a"]]])
def test_string_coercion_never_discards_or_invents_array_content(value) -> None:
    contract = parse_output_contract({"mode": "json_schema", "name": "cons", "strict": True,
        "schema": {"type": "object", "properties": {"cons": {"type": "string"}},
                   "required": ["cons"], "additionalProperties": False}})
    payload = {"cons": value}
    assert coerce_output_payload(contract, payload) == []
    with pytest.raises(PromptOutputContractError):
        validate_output_payload(contract, payload)


def test_singleton_string_array_does_not_change_enum_or_allowed_array() -> None:
    contract = parse_output_contract({"mode": "json_schema", "name": "union", "strict": True,
        "schema": {"type": "object", "properties": {
            "choice": {"type": "string", "enum": ["a", "b"]},
            "either": {"type": ["string", "array"], "items": {"type": "string"}}},
            "required": ["choice", "either"], "additionalProperties": False}})
    payload = {"choice": ["a"], "either": ["text"]}
    assert coerce_output_payload(contract, payload) == []
    assert payload == {"choice": ["a"], "either": ["text"]}
    with pytest.raises(PromptOutputContractError):
        validate_output_payload(contract, payload)


def test_json_array_string_is_decoded_not_split() -> None:
    payload = {
        "tags": '["滨海场景", "木质家具", "休闲氛围", "度假地产"]',
        "score": 78, "hit": True, "reason": "a", "nested": {"n": 1},
    }
    coercions = coerce_output_payload(_contract(), payload)
    assert payload["tags"] == ["滨海场景", "木质家具", "休闲氛围", "度假地产"]
    assert coercions == ["$.tags:json_string->array"]


def test_empty_unknown_keys_are_dropped_but_non_empty_still_fail_closed() -> None:
    """run 209 asset 6113：模型在 additionalProperties=false 的对象里多吐 hit_rules: []。"""

    payload = {
        "tags": ["x"],
        "score": 80,
        "hit": True,
        "reason": "a",
        "nested": {"n": 1, "hit_rules": [], "note": None},
        "extra": "",
    }
    coercions = coerce_output_payload(_contract(), payload)
    assert sorted(coercions) == [
        "$.extra:drop_empty_unknown",
        "$.nested.hit_rules:drop_empty_unknown",
        "$.nested.note:drop_empty_unknown",
    ]
    validate_output_payload(_contract(), payload)
    assert payload["nested"] == {"n": 1}
    non_empty = {"tags": ["x"], "score": 80, "hit": True, "reason": "a", "nested": {"n": 1, "title": "多吐的有值字段"}}
    assert coerce_output_payload(_contract(), non_empty) == []
    with pytest.raises(PromptOutputContractError):
        validate_output_payload(_contract(), non_empty)


def _production_cons_contract(**constraints):
    return parse_output_contract({"mode": "json_schema", "name": "production_cons", "strict": True,
        "schema": {"type": "object", "properties": {"production_fields": {
            "type": "object", "properties": {"cons": {"type": "string", **constraints}},
            "required": ["cons"], "additionalProperties": False}},
            "required": ["production_fields"], "additionalProperties": False}})


def test_production_cons_paragraphs_preserve_every_character_and_order():
    contract = _production_cons_contract()
    parts = [" 模型细节偏基础。", "材质趋同；保留原标点。\n已有换行 "]
    payload = {"production_fields": {"cons": parts.copy()}}
    assert coerce_output_payload(contract, payload) == ["$.production_fields.cons:string_paragraph_array->string"]
    assert payload["production_fields"]["cons"] == "\n".join(parts)
    validate_output_payload(contract, payload)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("value", [[], ["a", None], ["a", 2], ["a", ["b"]]])
def test_production_cons_never_flattens_non_text(value):
    payload = {"production_fields": {"cons": value}}
    assert coerce_output_payload(_production_cons_contract(), payload) == []
    with pytest.raises(PromptOutputContractError):
        validate_output_payload(_production_cons_contract(), payload)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("constraints", [{"enum": ["a", "b"]}, {"const": "a"}])
def test_production_cons_never_coerces_choice_constraints(constraints):
    payload = {"production_fields": {"cons": ["a", "b"]}}
    assert coerce_output_payload(_production_cons_contract(**constraints), payload) == []


def test_production_cons_still_enforces_frozen_length_after_join():
    contract = _production_cons_contract(maxLength=3)
    payload = {"production_fields": {"cons": ["aa", "bb"]}}
    coerce_output_payload(contract, payload)
    with pytest.raises(PromptOutputContractError):
        validate_output_payload(contract, payload)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_p0e_canary_api.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from contextlib import contextmanager
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Iterator
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.main import app, current_user
from app.migrations import run_migrations
from app.models import (
    Asset,
    CanaryRun,
    EvaluationResult,
    User,
)
from app.p0e_candidate_package import CANDIDATE_PACKAGE_VERSION
from app.p0e_image_freeze import MANIFEST_VERSION
from app.p0e_safe_import import PREFLIGHT_SCHEMA_VERSION


_BATCH_KEY = "p0e:" + "a" * 64


@dataclass
class ApiContext:
    client: TestClient
    sessions: sessionmaker[Session]
    user: User


@contextmanager
def _api_context(*, authenticated: bool = True) -> Iterator[ApiContext]:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(
        bind=engine,
        expire_on_commit=False,
        autoflush=False,
    )
    with sessions() as db:
        user = User(is_admin=True, role="admin",
            username="canary-tester",
            password_hash="unused",
            display_name="金丝雀测试员",
        )
        db.add(user)
        db.commit()

    def test_db() -> Iterator[Session]:
        with sessions() as db:
            yield db

    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = test_db
    if authenticated:
        app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
    client = TestClient(app)
    try:
        yield ApiContext(client=client, sessions=sessions, user=user)
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        engine.dispose()


def _create(
    client: TestClient,
    *,
    seed: str = "e3-seed",
    display_name: str | None = "P0-E 金丝雀",
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    response = client.post(
        "/api/canary-runs",
        json={
            "domain": "3D",
            "target_size": 40,
            "seed": seed,
            "display_name": display_name,
        },
    )
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    return response.json()


def _preflight(
    batch_key: str = _BATCH_KEY,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "schema_version": PREFLIGHT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "batch_key": batch_key,
        "mode": "preflight_only",
        "writes_business_database": False,
    }


def _approval(
    *,
    approved_by: str = "operator-01",
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "human_approved": True,
        "approved_by": approved_by,
        "batch_key": _BATCH_KEY,
        "applied_mappings": [],
    }


def _fetch_config() -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "allowed_hosts": [
            "images.example.test",
            "cdn.example.test",
        ],
        "pinned_https_attested": True,
    }


def _manifest() -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "manifest_version": MANIFEST_VERSION,
        "complete": True,
        "expected_source_count": 40,
        "frozen_source_count": 40,
        "errors": [],
        "assets": [],
    }


def _human_review_evidence() -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "candidate_preview": {
            "schema_version": CANDIDATE_PACKAGE_VERSION,
            "complete_for_requested_preview": True,
            "selected_count": 40,
            "forms_gold": False,
            "downloads_performed": False,
            "model_runs_performed": False,
        },
        "human_review_handoff": {
            "all_items_require_review": True,
            "no_truth_or_gold_granted": True,
            "item_count": 40,
        },
    }


def _transition(
    client: TestClient,
    run: dict[str, Any],
    transition: str,
    evidence: dict[str, Any],
    *,
    expected_fingerprint: str | None = None,
    expected_status: int = 200,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    response = client.post(
        (
            f"/api/canary-runs/{run['run_id']}"
            f"/transitions/{transition}"
        ),
        json={
            "expected_snapshot_fingerprint": (
                expected_fingerprint
                or run["snapshot_fingerprint"]
            ),
            "evidence": evidence,
        },
    )
    assert response.status_code == expected_status, response.text
    return response.json()


def _advance_to_human_review(
    client: TestClient,
    run: dict[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    for transition, evidence in (
        ("preflight_ready", _preflight()),
        ("approvals_ready", _approval()),
        ("freeze_ready", _fetch_config()),
        ("candidate_ready", _manifest()),
        ("human_review_ready", _human_review_evidence()),
    ):
        run = _transition(client, run, transition, evidence)
    return run


def _assert_response_has_no_unsafe_url(node: Any) -> None:
    if isinstance(node, str):
        parts = urlsplit(node)
        if parts.scheme.casefold() in {"http", "https"}:
            assert parts.username is None
            assert parts.password is None
            assert not parts.query
            assert not parts.fragment
        return
    if isinstance(node, dict):
        for value in node.values():
            _assert_response_has_no_unsafe_url(value)
    elif isinstance(node, list):
        for value in node:
            _assert_response_has_no_unsafe_url(value)


def test_migration_17_upgrades_an_old_database(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    database_path = tmp_path / "v16.db"
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{database_path}")
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.exec_driver_sql("""
            CREATE TABLE schema_migrations (
                version INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
                applied_at DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            )
        """)
        for version in range(1, 17):
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO schema_migrations (version, name) VALUES (?, ?)",
                (version, f"historical-{version}"),
            )
        run_migrations(connection)

        columns = {
            row[1]
            for row in connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "PRAGMA table_info(canary_runs)"
            )
        }
        assert columns == {
            "run_id",
            "display_name",
            "current_state",
            "plan_json",
            "evidence_json",
            "snapshot_json",
            "snapshot_fingerprint",
            "created_by",
            "created_at",
            "updated_at",
        }
        applied = connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT name FROM schema_migrations WHERE version = 17"
        ).scalar_one()
        assert applied == "add_canary_run_persistence"
    engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("method", "path", "payload"),
    [
        (
            "post",
            "/api/canary-runs",
            {"domain": "3D", "target_size": 40, "seed": "s"},
        ),
        ("get", "/api/canary-runs", None),

        (

            "transitions/preflight_ready",
